> **ARCHIVED 2026-08-20.** Executed one-off: this pass ran 2026-08-14/15 and its outcomes live in
> [`docs/ops/BUG_REPORT_2026-08-14.md`](../ops/BUG_REPORT_2026-08-14.md). Rig-specific and already
> drifted (e.g. the K1 env-var claim) — do not re-run as written.

# Nano Crew — Full-App Test Plan (2026-08-14)

> Produced by an 8-agent code dissection (7 surfaces + a completeness critic) of every screen, option, submission, and tool in the app. **530 tests · 85 flows · 8 areas** — 175 P0 / 248 P1 / 107 P2; 51 tests are boundary-limited (they stop before money/orders/external email and verify server-side effects instead).

## Environment & rig

- **Device:** iPhone 17 Pro simulator (iOS 26.3), Debug build from main (post-vision-bus), Metro on :8081 serving the app AND the `+api.ts` backend routes with `.env.local`.
- **Account:** `claudetest@nanocrew.dev` (pre-confirmed, in `COMP_EMAILS` — AI spends are not billed). Credentials in `.env.local` (`TEST_ACCOUNT_*`).
- **Data reality:** the dev server talks to the PRODUCTION Supabase DB — test rows are real rows. Cleanup notes ride each destructive test.
- **Voice:** the Simulator has no mic input. Voice flows run through keyboard mode / `sendText` / direct API calls; barge-in, echo, and real-mic behavior are flagged `manual-device-only` (Cody / Joe on TestFlight build 50).
- **API-method tests** are copy-pasteable authed fetches against `http://localhost:8081/api/*`.

## Hard boundaries (no exceptions during execution)

1. **Stripe is LIVE in this env** — stop at any payment sheet/plan button; verify the request shape or server log instead. Never submit a payment.
2. **No Printful order creation** — checkout tests end before order submission.
3. **No external email sends** beyond what flows fire inherently; account-deletion tests use throwaway accounts, never the main test account mid-run.

## Known issues (found before testing began)

| # | Severity | Finding |
|---|---|---|
| K1 | High | `DELETE /api/me` reads `SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY`, which is unset (env has `SUPABASE_SECRET_KEY`) → the Supabase auth identity silently survives account deletion. App Store data-deletion compliance incomplete; re-signup with the same email lands in a broken half-state. |
| K2 | Med | `.env.local` carries LIVE Stripe keys — there is no test-mode environment at all; any dev-machine checkout mistake is a real charge. Recommend a parallel test-mode env. |
| K3 | Low | `expo run:ios` cannot parse this Xcode's `devicectl` JSON → misroutes simulator targets into the physical-device signing path. Workaround: raw `xcodebuild` + `simctl install`. |

## Execution order

1. **P0 smoke sweep** — fresh-install welcome → signup/login → Eve guide loads → typed interview turn → design generate → market browse → account screens. Abort-level bugs surface here.
2. **Area passes in order:** Auth/Onboarding → Eve conversation → Eve design+build → Design tab → Market → Account → Cross-cutting API battery → Gaps.
3. P1s inline with their area; P2s last or opportunistically.
4. Bugs land in `docs/ops/BUG_REPORT_2026-08-14.md` as found (id, severity, repro, expected vs actual, evidence); testing continues unless the bug blocks the area.

## Legend

methods: `simulator-ui` = drive the app UI · `api` = direct authed fetch to the dev server · `unit` = runnable in isolation · `manual-device-only` = needs a real phone. **boundary** = where the test must stop and what to verify instead.

---

# Area: Market tab + commerce UI (browse → brand store → product detail → Stripe checkout boundary, creator product lifecycle, purchases/orders-by-email)

## Market tab browsing (Discover / Shop / Brands / Search)

**Entry:** Tab bar → Market (src/components/app-tabs.tsx → /market). Screen: src/app/market.tsx. Data: GET /api/market (src/app/api/market+api.ts, public, no auth). Client-side blocklist filter: src/lib/blocklist.ts (AsyncStorage key nc_blocked_brands). Deep link: /market?store=<slug> opens the BrandStore sheet.

### MKT-1 — Discover tab happy path renders hero + rails from live data [P0]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui, api
- **Steps:**
  1. Launch app (npm run ios), sign in as claudetest@nanocrew.dev
  2. Tap the Market tab
  3. Wait for the ActivityIndicator to clear
  4. Verify the Discover tab is selected by default (underline under 'Discover')
  5. Verify sections render in order: full-bleed Hero (if featured non-empty), 'Fresh drops' horizontal rail (collections with cover art), 'New this week' 2-up grid (trending)
  6. Cross-check against `curl <devserver>/api/market | jq '{featured: .featured|length, collections: .collections|length, trending: .trending|length}'` — each UI section appears iff its array is non-empty
- **Expected:** Sections mirror the API payload exactly; hero images use modelShots[0] ?? imageUrl; every card shows storeName + product name; no section header renders for an empty array.

### MKT-2 — Hero carousel pages horizontally with dot indicator sync [P1]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui
- **Steps:**
  1. On Discover with ≥2 featured items, swipe the hero left
  2. Verify the page snaps (pagingEnabled) and the active dot advances (dotOn style: larger white dot)
  3. Swipe back right; verify the dot returns
  4. Tap a hero page; verify the BrandStore sheet opens for that item's storeSlug
- **Expected:** Dots track contentOffset.x/width rounding; tap-through opens the correct brand (kicker text = storeName uppercased).

### MKT-3 — Tab switching Discover → Shop → Brands preserves loaded data [P0]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui, api
- **Steps:**
  1. Tap 'Shop' — verify a 2-up product grid renders from the `more` pool, plus category chips if >1 category exists
  2. Tap 'Brands' — verify BrandTiles render (preview image, logo, 'N piece(s)' count) only for brands with productCount > 0
  3. Tap back to 'Discover' — verify no refetch spinner (same in-memory data)
- **Expected:** No network call on tab switch (tab is pure client state); grid columns computed from window width; brands with 0 published products are hidden client-side even though /api/market returns them.

### MKT-4 — Shop category chips filter the grid [P1]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui
- **Steps:**
  1. On Shop, note the chip row ('All' + categories derived from products' category field)
  2. Tap a category chip — verify it highlights (tint background, dark text) and the grid filters to that category only
  3. Tap 'All' — full pool returns
  4. If every product has null category, verify the chip row does not render at all (cats.length === 1)
- **Expected:** Filtering is exact string match on product.category; empty filter result shows 'Nothing here yet.'

### MKT-5 — Brand search: debounce, tab nav hides, BrandRow results [P0]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui, api
- **Steps:**
  1. Type a known brand-name substring into the 'Search brands' GlowInput
  2. Verify the Discover/Shop/Brands tab nav disappears while query is non-empty
  3. Verify results appear after the 300ms debounce as compact BrandRows (logo, name, tagline or 'N drops', → chevron)
  4. Verify via `curl '<devserver>/api/market?q=<term>'` that featured/collections/more come back empty and brands is ilike-filtered on name/slug/tagline
  5. Clear the query — tabs reappear and the full feed returns
- **Expected:** Search collapses the feed to brand matches only; each keystroke resets the 300ms timer (no request storm); results tap-through to BrandStore.

### MKT-6 — Search with no matches shows empty card; wildcard chars behave [P2]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui, api
- **Risk:** ilike pattern metacharacters (% and _) in user queries are passed through unescaped — functional quirk, not a security hole.
- **Steps:**
  1. Search 'zzzzqqqq' — verify card: No brands match "zzzzqqqq".
  2. API edge: `curl '<devserver>/api/market?q=%25'` (a literal %) — document that ilike '%…%' treats % as a wildcard so every visible brand matches
  3. Search a term with an apostrophe/quote — verify no 500 (drizzle parameterizes)
- **Expected:** No crash on special characters; % acting as a match-all wildcard is documented behavior (cosmetic, not injectable).

### MKT-7 — Pull-to-refresh refetches the feed [P1]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui
- **Steps:**
  1. On any tab, pull the feed down until RefreshControl spins
  2. Watch the dev-server logs for a fresh GET /api/market
  3. Verify the spinner clears when the response lands
- **Expected:** setRefreshing(true) re-triggers the fetch effect; refreshing clears in .finally even on error.

### MKT-8 — Empty-market state via blocklist (client-side simulation) [P1]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui
- **Risk:** Blocking is device-persistent (AsyncStorage) — remember to clear it or later tests see an empty market.
- **Steps:**
  1. Open a brand from Market, scroll to the moderation footer, tap 'Block', confirm — repeat for every brand visible in the Brands tab
  2. Return to Discover — verify 'No live storefronts yet.' empty card (featured+trending both filtered to empty)
  3. Check Brands tab — same empty card; Shop tab — 'Nothing here yet.'
  4. Cleanup: delete + reinstall the app, or call unblockBrand via a dev hook (AsyncStorage key nc_blocked_brands)
- **Expected:** All three tabs have distinct, non-crashing empty states; blocklist filtering is purely client-side (API still returns full data).

### MKT-9 — Network failure: silent catch shows misleading empty state [P1]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui
- **Risk:** market.tsx line ~205: `.catch(() => {})` — an error state is never surfaced to the user.
- **Steps:**
  1. Stop the dev server (Ctrl+C on npx expo start) or turn Mac Wi-Fi off while the simulator app is open
  2. Cold-open the Market tab (kill and relaunch app first so no cached state)
  3. Observe: spinner clears, then the 'No live storefronts yet.' card renders — the fetch .catch(() => {}) swallows the error and data stays null
  4. Restart the server, pull to refresh — feed recovers
- **Expected:** No crash; documents that offline is indistinguishable from an empty market (no error banner, no retry affordance except pull-to-refresh). Flag as UX gap.

### MKT-10 — Signed-out browsing works end-to-end [P0]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui, api
- **Steps:**
  1. Account tab → sign out
  2. Open Market — verify the full feed loads (GET /api/market sends no Authorization header; route does no auth check)
  3. Open a brand sheet, open a product detail — both load (public GETs)
  4. Confirm via `curl` (no auth header) that /api/market, /api/store/<slug>, /api/store/<slug>/products/<pslug> all return 200
- **Expected:** Browse/brand/product surfaces are fully public; only Buy, Report, and Purchases need a session.

### MKT-11 — Price display: min-variant price, formatting, null-price products [P0]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui, api, unit
- **Steps:**
  1. Pick a product with multiple variant prices; confirm in DB/API that the card shows the MINIMUM retailPriceCents (min() aggregate in /api/market)
  2. Verify format `$XX.XX` (cents/100, 2 decimals) on hero, product cards, and brand-store cards
  3. Find or create a published product with no variants (or all-null prices) — verify the card renders name + storeName with NO price text (priceCents null → empty string, no '$NaN')
- **Expected:** price(c) renders '' for null — never $0.00 or NaN; hero price row collapses to an empty <View/> keeping the Shop pill right-aligned.

### MKT-12 — Deep link /market?store=<slug> opens the BrandStore sheet directly [P1]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui
- **Steps:**
  1. Run `xcrun simctl openurl booted "<app-scheme>://market?store=<live-slug>"` (scheme from app.json) or navigate via router in dev menu
  2. Verify Market mounts with the BrandStore sheet already sliding up for that slug
  3. Repeat with a nonexistent slug — sheet opens, then shows 'Couldn't load this brand.' + Try again (store 404 → readJson throws)
- **Expected:** useLocalSearchParams store param sets storeSlug on mount; bogus slug degrades to the sheet's error state, not a crash.

### MKT-13 — API contract: hero curation rules (photography-first, per-brand cap 4, max 6) [P1]

- **Methods:** api, unit
- **Steps:**
  1. `curl <devserver>/api/market | jq '.featured'`
  2. Assert length ≤ 6
  3. Assert no storeSlug appears more than 4 times
  4. If any product in the pool has videoUrl or modelShots, assert every featured item has one (photography-restricted); else assert fallback to any withImage
  5. Assert `more` ≤ 30, `trending` ≤ 12, `brands` ≤ 30, all only from stores with isPublic=true AND status='live' and isPublished=true products
- **Expected:** Curation invariants hold; a store toggled private (see LC-6) disappears from every array.

### MKT-14 — GET /api/market DB failure returns 500 JSON, not a hang [P2]

- **Methods:** api, unit
- **Steps:**
  1. Unit/integration: point DATABASE_URL at an unreachable host in a scratch env and hit /api/market
  2. Assert 500 with {error: <message>} (the route's catch)
  3. In the app this surfaces as the MKT-9 silent empty state
- **Expected:** Route never throws unhandled; error body is JSON.

## Brand storefront sheet (BrandStore) + moderation + website link-out

**Entry:** Tap any card in Market → <BrandStore slug=… /> modal (src/components/brand-store.tsx). Data: GET /api/store/:slug (src/app/api/store/[slug]+api.ts, public). Link-out: openBrowserAsync(brand.siteUrl) — siteUrl = https://customDomain, else deploymentUrl unless it contains github.com. Moderation: POST /api/report (src/app/api/report+api.ts, authed) + local block (src/lib/blocklist.ts).

### STORE-1 — Brand sheet happy path: palette, header, collections grouping [P0]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui, api
- **Steps:**
  1. Open a live brand from Market
  2. Verify the sheet background/text/accent use the brand's designSystem palette (bgHex/textHex/accentHex), not app chrome colors
  3. Verify header: logo (if set), brand name, tagline, 'N piece(s)' count matching productCount from the API
  4. Verify products are grouped under their catalogue names with season badges; products with no catalogueId appear in a trailing 'More' group
  5. Verify the hero SquareCarousel shows up to 8 deduped product images (or the OG card if the brand has no product imagery)
- **Expected:** UI mirrors GET /api/store/:slug exactly; empty-collection catalogues are omitted; malformed palette hexes fall back to #0b0b0f/#ffffff/#cdd1d9 (hex() regex guard).

### STORE-2 — Website link-out: 'visit website →' and 'Shop the website ↗' [P0]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui, api
- **Steps:**
  1. Open a brand that has a customDomain or non-github deploymentUrl (check via `curl <devserver>/api/store/<slug> | jq .brand.siteUrl`)
  2. Tap 'visit website →' in the top bar — verify an in-app browser (SFSafariViewController via openBrowserAsync) opens the exact siteUrl
  3. Dismiss; tap 'Shop the website ↗' under the header — same URL opens
  4. Verify a customDomain brand gets https://<customDomain> (scheme prepended) and a deploymentUrl containing github.com yields NO buttons at all
- **Expected:** Both buttons render only when siteUrl is non-null; browsing out is read-only (no purchase on the external site during testing).

### STORE-3 — Browse-only brand (no siteUrl) hides link buttons [P1]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui, api
- **Steps:**
  1. Find a brand where siteUrl is null via `curl <devserver>/api/market | jq` + `/api/store/<slug>`
  2. Open it — verify no 'visit website' / 'Shop the website' controls anywhere
  3. Verify products are still tappable and buyable in-app
- **Expected:** In-app store is fully functional without a website; layout has no dangling gaps.

### STORE-4 — Load failure → 'Couldn't load this brand.' + Try again retry [P1]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui
- **Steps:**
  1. Open Market, then stop the dev server
  2. Tap a brand card — verify the sheet opens, spinner runs, then 'Couldn't load this brand.' with a 'Try again' button
  3. Restart the dev server, tap 'Try again' — brand loads
- **Expected:** readJson throws on non-2xx/network → data null → error state; retry calls load() and recovers without closing the sheet.

### STORE-5 — Empty brand: 'hasn't dropped any pieces yet' [P2]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui, api
- **Steps:**
  1. Via API, find a live store whose productCount is 0 (or unlist products first on the test store)
  2. Open its sheet via deep link /market?store=<slug> (the Brands tab hides 0-count brands, deep link doesn't)
  3. Verify the centered 'This brand hasn't dropped any pieces yet.' message
- **Expected:** No crash on empty collections; moderation footer still renders.

### STORE-6 — Report brand: reason picker → POST /api/report (and the signed-out false-success bug) [P1]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui, api
- **Risk:** brand-store.tsx reportBrand(): Alert fires unconditionally after the try/catch — a 401/network failure is invisible to the user. Report sends a real email to ops.
- **Steps:**
  1. Signed in, open any brand, scroll to footer, tap '⚑ Report this brand'
  2. Verify the Alert offers: Offensive or inappropriate / Spam or scam / Other / Cancel
  3. Pick 'Other' with reason marked TEST — verify 'Thanks for the report' alert and a 200 in dev-server logs (route logs [report] <email> reported brand "<slug>")
  4. Sign out, repeat — observe the POST returns 401 yet the app STILL shows 'Thanks for the report' (submit's catch swallows the failure)
  5. API negatives: POST /api/report unauthenticated → 401; authed with missing slug or reason → 400
- **Expected:** Authed report succeeds and fires notifyPlatform ops email (mark reason TEST so ops can ignore); signed-out false success is a real bug to file — the catch comment assumes server-side logging that a 401 never reaches.

### STORE-7 — Block brand: confirm dialog, sheet closes, market filters, persists across restart [P1]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui
- **Steps:**
  1. Open a (non-essential) brand, tap 'Block' in the footer
  2. Verify Alert 'Block this brand?' with Cancel / destructive Block
  3. Tap Block — sheet closes and the brand vanishes from Discover/Shop/Brands (onClose → refreshBlocked)
  4. Kill and relaunch the app — brand is still hidden (AsyncStorage persistence)
  5. Deep-link /market?store=<blocked-slug> — verify the sheet STILL opens (blocklist filters lists only, not direct opens)
  6. Cleanup: erase the simulator app or clear nc_blocked_brands
- **Expected:** Client-side block hides the brand from all Market lists and survives restart; direct deep-link bypass is current (documented) behavior.

### STORE-8 — API contract: /api/store/:slug 404 + caching + palette fallback [P1]

- **Methods:** api
- **Risk:** GET /api/store/:slug returns unlisted/private stores — deliberate for direct links or an oversight; surface to Joe.
- **Steps:**
  1. `curl -i <devserver>/api/store/definitely-not-a-store` → 404 {error:'store not found'}
  2. `curl -i <devserver>/api/store/<live-slug>` → 200 with Cache-Control: public, max-age=120
  3. Assert response brand.siteUrl logic: customDomain wins; github.com deploymentUrl → null
  4. Note: the route does NOT check isPublic/status — a private store's catalog is readable by slug. Document as an information-exposure observation
- **Expected:** 404 and cache headers as coded; the missing visibility check on the store detail route is flagged for review (Market lists filter it, the detail route doesn't).

## Product detail: carousel, variant picker, price label

**Entry:** Tap a product card inside BrandStore → <ProductDetail/> modal (src/components/product-detail.tsx). Data: GET /api/store/:slug/products/:pslug (src/app/api/store/[slug]/products/[pslug]+api.ts, public, published-only).

### PROD-1 — Product page happy path [P0]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui, api
- **Steps:**
  1. Open a multi-variant product
  2. Verify the SquareCarousel shows main image first then model shots, deduped (swipe through, dots track)
  3. Verify name, price, COLOR and SIZE chip groups, description text
  4. Verify default selection = first in-stock variant (its color+size chips are highlighted)
  5. Verify the bottom Buy bar reads 'Buy · $XX.XX' with the selected variant's exact price
- **Expected:** UI mirrors the API payload; variants come back sorted by retailPriceCents ascending; the default is d.variants.find(inStock) ?? variants[0].

### PROD-2 — Color→size dependency: size preserved or jumps to first available [P0]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui, unit
- **Steps:**
  1. On a product where color A has sizes S/M/L and color B only has M/L: select A + S
  2. Tap color B — verify size auto-jumps to M (first available), never stays on an invalid S
  3. Tap color A again with M selected — M is kept (still available)
  4. Verify sizes not offered for the current color render at 0.3 opacity and are not tappable (disabled)
- **Expected:** pickColor keeps the size when the new color has it, else selects sizes[0]; unavailable chips are visually dimmed and inert.

### PROD-3 — Price label edge cases: range, single, empty [P1]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui, unit
- **Steps:**
  1. Verify a selected variant shows its exact price
  2. Unit-check the unselected fallback: min==max → single price; else '$lo–$hi'; no priced variants → '' (Buy button text degrades to just 'Buy')
  3. Verify no '$NaN' or '$0.00' ever appears for null-price variants
- **Expected:** money() returns '' for null; the range branch only renders when selection is incomplete.

### PROD-4 — Sold-out variant: Buy disabled with 'Sold out' label [P0]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui, api
- **Steps:**
  1. Via DB/API find (or set inStock=false on a claudetest-owned test variant) a sold-out variant
  2. Select it — verify the Buy button dims to 0.5 opacity, label 'Sold out', taps do nothing (canBuy false)
  3. Select an in-stock sibling — Buy re-enables
- **Expected:** canBuy = selected && selected.inStock && !buying; no checkout POST fires for out-of-stock.

### PROD-5 — API contract: 404s for unknown/unpublished, image dedupe [P1]

- **Methods:** api
- **Steps:**
  1. `curl -i <devserver>/api/store/<slug>/products/not-a-product` → 404 {error:'product not found'}
  2. `curl -i <devserver>/api/store/not-a-store/products/x` → 404 {error:'store not found'}
  3. For a product whose imageUrl also appears in modelShots, assert images[] has no duplicates and no nulls
  4. Assert Cache-Control: public, max-age=60
- **Expected:** Unpublished products are invisible (isPublished=true in the where clause); images deduped in order (main shot first).

### PROD-6 — Product load failure state [P2]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui
- **Steps:**
  1. Open a brand sheet, stop the dev server, tap a product
  2. Verify 'Couldn't load this product.' renders (no retry button on this screen — only ← back)
  3. Tap '← back' — returns to the brand sheet cleanly
- **Expected:** No crash; no Buy bar renders when product is null.

## In-app Buy → Stripe Checkout (LIVE-KEY BOUNDARY)

**Entry:** ProductDetail Buy button → POST /api/store/:slug/checkout (src/app/api/store/[slug]/checkout+api.ts) → proxies to platform-api /api/public/checkout (platform-api/app/api/public/checkout/route.ts — creates a pending_payment order row in the PRODUCTION DB, then a LIVE-mode Stripe Checkout Session) → app opens session.url via openBrowserAsync.

### BUY-1 — Signed-in buy up to the Stripe payment page (HARD STOP) [P0]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui, api
- **⛔ Boundary:** STOP at the Stripe Checkout page. Never enter card details, never tap Pay — LIVE Stripe keys mean a real charge and a real Printful order via the webhook. Verify the session PAGE CONTENTS and the request/response shapes instead, then close the browser. Cleanup: note the pending_payment order id created in the production DB.
- **Steps:**
  1. Signed in as claudetest@nanocrew.dev, open an in-stock variant, tap Buy
  2. Verify the button shows a spinner (buying state) and a POST /api/store/<slug>/checkout fires with body {items:[{variantId:'<uuid>',quantity:1}]} and an Authorization bearer header (watch dev-server logs / proxy)
  3. Verify the in-app browser opens a checkout.stripe.com URL
  4. VERIFY ONLY, DO NOT TYPE: the Stripe page lists the product name with variant suffix ('Name — Color / Size'), unit price matching the app, a 'Processing fee' line (subtotal < $200), 'Standard shipping' at the flat rate (default $7.99), and shipping restricted to US/CA
  5. Close the browser sheet WITHOUT entering any card/email data
  6. Record the created order id: query the prod DB or platform logs for the newest orders row (status='pending_payment', customerEmail='pending@checkout', stripeSessionId set) for cleanup bookkeeping
- **Expected:** Session created with DB-sourced prices (client never sends a price); order row parked at pending_payment forever (webhook never fires) — harmless residue, but log its id. No Printful call occurs before payment.

### BUY-2 — Guest (signed-out) buy reaches the same boundary [P0]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui, api
- **⛔ Boundary:** Same as BUY-1 — stop at the Stripe page, enter nothing, close the sheet.
- **Steps:**
  1. Sign out, navigate Market → brand → product, tap Buy
  2. Verify the POST carries NO Authorization header and the proxy body omits customerEmail (getUserFromRequest null)
  3. Verify a live Stripe URL still opens (guest checkout is supported)
  4. Close without entering anything
- **Expected:** Guest checkout works identically; attribution would come later from the Stripe-collected email via webhook.

### BUY-3 — Request-shape verification via local mock (zero prod side effects) [P0]

- **Methods:** api, simulator-ui
- **Risk:** DISCREPANCY FOUND: platform-api/app/api/public/checkout/route.ts never READS customerEmail from the body — the signed-in attribution the app route documents is silently dropped, and every order starts as 'pending@checkout'. Verify against the deployed platform-api and file if confirmed.
- **Steps:**
  1. Write a 10-line node server in the scratchpad that logs req body and returns {url:'https://example.com/mock'} on POST /api/public/checkout
  2. Set PLATFORM_API_BASE=http://localhost:<port> in .env.local, restart the dev server
  3. Tap Buy signed-in: assert captured body === {storeSlug:'<slug>', items:[{variantId,quantity:1}], customerEmail:'claudetest@nanocrew.dev'}
  4. Tap Buy signed-out: assert customerEmail key is absent entirely
  5. Verify the app opens the mock URL (proves it uses response.url verbatim)
  6. Restore PLATFORM_API_BASE and restart
- **Expected:** Exact proxy contract verified without touching live Stripe or the prod order table — the preferred way to regression-test this flow.

### BUY-4 — Checkout input validation errors [P0]

- **Methods:** api
- **Steps:**
  1. `curl -X POST <devserver>/api/store/<slug>/checkout -H 'Content-Type: application/json' -d '{}'` → 400 {error:'items required'}
  2. Body with quantity:0 or empty variantId → 400 (filtered out → empty items)
  3. Non-JSON body → 400 (json().catch(null))
  4. Valid shape but unknown variantId (random uuid) → platform-api returns 400 'unknown item in cart' — passed through with status intact
  5. variantId belonging to a DIFFERENT store than the slug in the URL → 400 'unknown item in cart' (cross-store guard)
  6. Out-of-stock variantId → 409 '<name> (<size>) is out of stock'
- **Expected:** All validation happens BEFORE the order insert in platform-api, so none of these create order rows; app route relays platform status codes verbatim.

### BUY-5 — Checkout-unconfigured (503) and network-fail UX notes [P1]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui, api
- **Steps:**
  1. Temporarily comment out PLATFORM_API_BASE in .env.local, restart dev server
  2. Tap Buy — verify note under the description: 'Checkout isn't live yet — it opens once payments are turned on.' (503 branch), spinner clears, no browser opens
  3. Restore env; instead stop the dev server entirely and tap Buy — verify note 'Could not start checkout. Please try again.' (catch branch)
  4. Verify the Buy button is re-enabled after each failure (finally → setBuying(false))
- **Expected:** Both failure notes render in the accent color; no stuck spinner; no retry storm.

### BUY-6 — Double-tap protection on Buy [P1]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui, api
- **Steps:**
  1. Point PLATFORM_API_BASE at the BUY-3 mock with a 3s artificial delay
  2. Tap Buy twice rapidly
  3. Assert the mock logs exactly ONE request (canBuy false while buying=true disables the Pressable)
- **Expected:** No duplicate checkout sessions/order rows from double taps.

### BUY-7 — Quantity/items clamps and fee math (platform-api rules, verified read-only) [P2]

- **Methods:** api, unit
- **⛔ Boundary:** Each live assertion here creates a pending_payment order + live session — keep to at most one live run and do the rest as unit checks of the formula.
- **Steps:**
  1. Via curl to the dev-server proxy with quantity:999 for one item — from code, platform clamps per-line quantity to 20 and items list to 50; verify the created Stripe session line shows qty 20 on the checkout page (then close; note the pending order id)
  2. Verify fee math on the BUY-1 session page: processing fee ≈ (0.029·(subtotal+shipping)+30¢)/(1−0.029), waived when subtotal ≥ $200
  3. Prefer asserting the math as a unit test against the route source rather than creating extra live sessions
- **Expected:** Clamps enforced server-side; fee line matches the gross-up formula; $200+ carts show no fee line.

## Creator product lifecycle: publish propagation, delete, the missing unpublish/edit-price routes, store list/unlist

**Entry:** Publish happens from the Design surface via POST /api/publish (src/app/api/publish+api.ts, rate-limited guardRate('pf:<uid>',30,60)); the ONLY per-product creator write is DELETE /api/creator/products/:id (src/app/api/creator/products/[id]+api.ts); store-level market listing: POST /api/creator/stores/:slug/publish (src/app/api/creator/stores/[slug]/publish+api.ts). Reads: GET /api/creator/products?storeSlug= (authed).

### LIFE-1 — Confirm the lifecycle gap from the API surface: no unpublish, no price edit [P0]

- **Methods:** api, simulator-ui
- **Risk:** This gap is the design constraint the rest of this flow's tests work around: never publish a test product you aren't prepared to hard-delete.
- **Steps:**
  1. `curl -i -X PATCH <devserver>/api/creator/products/<id> -H 'Authorization: Bearer <token>'` → 404/405 (no PATCH export)
  2. `curl -i -X PUT …` and POST to the same path → 404/405
  3. grep confirmation (already done): src/app/api/creator/products/[id]+api.ts exports ONLY DELETE; no route anywhere writes products.isPublished=false or variants.retailPriceCents post-publish
  4. In the app, verify no UI control exists to unpublish a single product or change its price after publish (Design tab product list)
- **Expected:** CONFIRMED GAP: once published, a product can only be deleted (destroying it in Printful too) or the whole store unlisted. Price mistakes are permanent without delete+republish. Document as the known product-lifecycle gap with repro evidence.

### LIFE-2 — Publish → Market propagation (read side) [P0]

- **Methods:** api, simulator-ui
- **⛔ Boundary:** POST /api/publish creates a REAL sync product in the LIVE Printful store. Allowed only for the claudetest store, name-prefixed 'TEST — ', and it MUST be cleaned up via LIFE-3. Do not create Printful ORDERS (that only happens post-payment, which BUY tests never reach).
- **Steps:**
  1. Using the claudetest comp account (credits comped), publish a clearly-marked test product from the Design flow (POST /api/publish) into the test store
  2. Ensure the test store is live+public (LIFE-5)
  3. GET /api/market — assert the product appears in trending/more (newest-first) with its min variant price
  4. GET /api/store/<test-slug> — appears in its catalogue group
  5. GET /api/public/stores/<test-slug>/products — appears with full variant list (the storefront-site contract)
  6. Open Market in the simulator — card renders
- **Expected:** One publish fans out to all three read surfaces immediately (market has no cache; store routes cache ≤120s).

### LIFE-3 — DELETE product: happy path removes it everywhere (and is the cleanup for LIFE-2) [P0]

- **Methods:** api, simulator-ui
- **⛔ Boundary:** Only ever DELETE products owned by claudetest that were created for this test run — deletion hits the LIVE Printful store and is irreversible.
- **Steps:**
  1. `curl -i -X DELETE <devserver>/api/creator/products/<test-product-id> -H 'Authorization: Bearer <claudetest-token>'` → 200 {ok:true}
  2. Assert gone from GET /api/creator/products?storeSlug=<test-slug>
  3. Assert gone from GET /api/market and GET /api/store/<test-slug> (allow the 120s cache to expire or restart dev server)
  4. Verify in Printful dashboard (read-only) that the sync product was removed
  5. Verify dev-server logs show revalidateStorefront fired (fire-and-forget)
- **Expected:** DB row + variants (FK cascade) gone; Printful best-effort delete succeeded; order_items snapshots (if any existed) survive.

### LIFE-4 — DELETE authorization: 401 / foreign product / bad id [P0]

- **Methods:** api
- **Risk:** The foreign-id case runs against real market products — the assertion is that NOTHING changes; abort the suite if it ever returns 200.
- **Steps:**
  1. DELETE with no Authorization header → 401 {error:'unauthorized'}
  2. DELETE a product id belonging to another creator's store (pick any live market product NOT owned by claudetest) → TenantError status (403/404 per src/lib/tenant.ts assertProductOwner) and CONFIRM the product still exists in /api/market afterward
  3. DELETE a random UUID → tenant 404
  4. DELETE a non-uuid string → 500-or-tenant-error; assert JSON error body either way
- **Expected:** Tenant scoping prevents cross-creator deletion; no Printful call is attempted before ownership passes.

### LIFE-5 — Store list/unlist toggle drives Market visibility [P0]

- **Methods:** api, simulator-ui
- **⛔ Boundary:** Toggling the claudetest store briefly changes what REAL users see in the production Market — do the unlist/relist cycle quickly and always restore listed:true (or leave the test store unlisted by design).
- **Risk:** 402 subscription_required cannot be triggered with the comp account (COMP_ENTITLEMENTS.active=true); needs a throwaway non-comp free account via api, or a unit test of getEntitlements gating.
- **Steps:**
  1. POST /api/creator/stores/<test-slug>/publish with {listed:false} (authed as claudetest) → 200 {ok:true,listed:false}
  2. GET /api/market — the brand and all its products vanish from every array
  3. In the simulator, pull-to-refresh Market — brand gone
  4. POST {listed:true} → 200 {ok:true,listed:true,status:'live'} — brand returns (comp account has active entitlements so no 402)
  5. Negative: with zero published products in the store, {listed:true} → 409 {error:'no_published_products'}
  6. Negative: unauthenticated → 401; a slug not owned by claudetest → 404
- **Expected:** isPublic+status drive /api/market's storeVisible filter; unlist with a customDomain keeps status 'live' (domain stays attached), else drops to 'ready'.

### LIFE-6 — Publish rate limit (pf: bucket, 30/60s) — gate fires before side effects [P1]

- **Methods:** api
- **Risk:** Limiter fails OPEN on its own DB error — a flaky run that never 429s may indicate the rate_limits table hiccuped, not a regression.
- **Steps:**
  1. Fire 31 rapid `curl -X POST <devserver>/api/publish -H 'Authorization: Bearer <claudetest-token>' -d '{}'` (empty body)
  2. Assert requests 1–30 → 400 {error:'compositionId, name, variants required'} (guardRate passes, body validation fails — zero side effects)
  3. Assert request 31 → 429 with a retry-after payload (guardRate runs BEFORE body parsing)
  4. Wait 60s, one more empty POST → 400 again (window reset)
- **Expected:** Fixed-window limiter in src/lib/rate-limit.ts enforces 30/60s per creator; invalid bodies are a safe probe because the limiter precedes validation.

### LIFE-7 — GET /api/creator/products auth + scoping [P1]

- **Methods:** api
- **Steps:**
  1. Unauthenticated → 401
  2. Authed, missing storeSlug param → 400 {error:'storeSlug required'}
  3. Authed with a slug claudetest doesn't own → 404
  4. Authed with the test store slug → 200 products[] including isPublished flags (drafts AND published — creator view)
- **Expected:** storeForMember scoping; the creator list shows unpublished drafts that public routes hide.

### LIFE-8 — Catalogue routes: authed CRUD + idempotent create [P1]

- **Methods:** api, simulator-ui
- **Steps:**
  1. GET /api/catalogues unauthenticated → 401; authed with ?store=<test-slug> → 200 sorted catalogue list
  2. POST /api/catalogues {name:'TEST Drop', storeSlug:<test-slug>} → 200 {catalogue:{id,name,slug:'test-drop'}}
  3. POST the identical body again → 200 returning the SAME id (onConflictDoNothing get-or-create, no 500)
  4. POST {name:''} → 400 {error:'name is required'}
  5. POST with a storeSlug claudetest can't access → 404
  6. Verify an ACTIVE catalogue with cover art from a live store appears in /api/market collections ('Fresh drops' rail) — note the test one won't (no coverImageUrl) which itself verifies the isNotNull(coverImageUrl) filter
- **Expected:** Create is idempotent per (storeId, slug); Market's drops rail only shows active catalogues WITH cover art from visible stores.

## Purchases / orders-by-email lookup

**Entry:** Account tab → Purchases modal (src/components/purchases.tsx) → GET /api/customer/orders (src/app/api/customer/orders+api.ts — matches orders on lower(customerEmail)=lower(account email)). Guest web lookup lives in platform-api /api/public/order-lookup (email+orderId pair) — out-of-app, verify by curl only.

### PUR-1 — Signed-in with no orders: empty state [P0]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui, api
- **Steps:**
  1. Sign in as claudetest@nanocrew.dev (assuming no paid orders exist against that email)
  2. Account → Purchases
  3. Verify 'No purchases yet' + the explainer copy about orders showing up with tracking and returns
- **Expected:** GET /api/customer/orders → 200 {orders:[]} and the empty card renders; no crash on empty.

### PUR-2 — API auth + shape [P0]

- **Methods:** api
- **Steps:**
  1. `curl -i <devserver>/api/customer/orders` (no auth) → 401
  2. Authed → 200; if any orders exist assert each has items[], canRequestReturn computed from returnWindowEndsAt AND status not in the NOT_CLAIMABLE list, newest-first, ≤100
- **Expected:** Email matching is case-insensitive; line items grouped per order in one query.

### PUR-3 — Attribution gap: BUY-1's pending order does NOT appear in Purchases [P1]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui, api
- **Risk:** Spec-vs-code disagreement — per AGENTS.md that's a bug to surface, not silently accept.
- **Steps:**
  1. After BUY-1 (signed-in checkout session created, never paid), open Purchases
  2. Verify the pending order is ABSENT — because platform-api inserted customerEmail='pending@checkout', not the account email (see BUY-3 risk note)
  3. Document: per the app-route comment the order SHOULD have been attributed to claudetest up front; the platform-api code drops customerEmail
- **Expected:** Current behavior: invisible until a webhook (which never fires unpaid) overwrites the email. File the customerEmail passthrough gap with both file references (src/app/api/store/[slug]/checkout+api.ts comment vs platform-api/app/api/public/checkout/route.ts body parsing).

### PUR-4 — Purchases load failure + retry [P1]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui
- **Steps:**
  1. Open Purchases with the dev server stopped
  2. Verify 'Could not load your purchases — try again.' + underlined Retry
  3. Restart server, tap Retry — loads
- **Expected:** readJson throws → error state; Retry calls load().

### PUR-5 — Return-request form validation (no live order needed for the negatives) [P1]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui, api
- **⛔ Boundary:** A successful return submission flips a REAL order to return_requested and emails ops — only ever run the success path against an order created and paid by the test account, which this plan never creates (payment is behind the Stripe hard stop). Stop at validation + API negatives.
- **Steps:**
  1. If any order with canRequestReturn exists: tap 'Request a return', verify reason chips (Defective / Wrong item / Damaged in transit / Never arrived)
  2. Submit with no reason → inline 'Pick a reason for the return.' (no network call)
  3. Pick Defective with empty photos → 'Add at least one photo URL…' (defective/damaged require photos; wrong_item/not_received don't)
  4. API negative regardless of UI: POST /api/customer/returns authed with a bogus orderId → error status relayed; unauthenticated → 401
  5. Do NOT submit a real return against a real paid order
- **Expected:** Client-side validation gates the POST; photo URLs parsed from whitespace/comma-separated http(s) strings only.

### PUR-6 — Guest order-lookup contract (platform-api, curl only) [P2]

- **Methods:** api
- **Steps:**
  1. `curl -X POST https://<platform-api>/api/public/order-lookup -d '{"email":"nobody@example.com","orderNumber":"<random-uuid>"}' -H 'Content-Type: application/json'` → not-found/error (email+id pair must BOTH match)
  2. With the BUY-1 order id + 'pending@checkout' → document whether the placeholder email is queryable (it matches lower-cased) — an odd but harmless artifact
  3. Verify knowing only an order id (wrong email) never returns order data
- **Expected:** Email+orderId pairing prevents order enumeration; read-only, no side effects.

# Area: Account tab + money/credits/settings (src/app/account.tsx, src/components/paywall.tsx, src/lib/{billing,credits,comp,rate-limit,push,iap,iap-products}.ts, src/app/api/creator/{subscription,credits,billing/*,connect,push-token,onboarding}+api.ts, src/app/api/me+api.ts)

## Signed-out Account: auth screen (sign-in / sign-up / reset)

**Entry:** Account tab while signed out — src/app/account.tsx renders the authWrap branch (join hero, Apple/Google buttons, email form). Reach it via the Account tab in src/components/app-tabs.tsx or after Sign out. API-level tests authenticate directly against Supabase: POST {EXPO_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL}/auth/v1/token?grant_type=password with the anon key and claudetest@nanocrew.dev credentials, then hit the dev server at http://localhost:8081/api/* with the returned access_token as Bearer.

### AUTH-1 — Email sign-in happy path as claudetest [P0]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui, api
- **Steps:**
  1. Launch app (npm run ios), tap the Account tab
  2. Enter claudetest@nanocrew.dev and its password in the GlowInputs
  3. Tap 'Sign in' (GlowButton shows loading spinner while busy)
  4. Wait for the signed-in view to render
- **Expected:** Session established; screen swaps to profile view showing the email, a plan badge, and 'creator <first-8-of-uuid>'. The bootstrap effect fires 4 requests (/api/me, /api/platform/admin, /api/creator/connect, /api/creator/subscription). Because claudetest is in COMP_EMAILS, /api/creator/subscription returns plan 'advanced' → badge reads 'ADVANCED'.

### AUTH-2 — Client-side validation: empty email / short password [P1]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui
- **Steps:**
  1. On the sign-in form leave email empty (or enter a password shorter than 6 chars)
  2. Tap 'Sign in'
- **Expected:** No network call; inline red error 'Enter an email and a password of at least 6 characters.' appears (DANGER #e24b4a). Button not stuck in loading state.

### AUTH-3 — Wrong password surfaces Supabase error [P1]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui, api
- **Steps:**
  1. Enter claudetest@nanocrew.dev with a deliberately wrong password
  2. Tap 'Sign in'
- **Expected:** Supabase 'Invalid login credentials' message rendered as the inline error; busy flag clears; form remains editable.

### AUTH-4 — Sign-up mode validation gates (name + terms checkbox) — stop before creating an account [P1]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui
- **⛔ Boundary:** Stop before a valid submission — this env points at the PRODUCTION Supabase; don't create throwaway auth users casually. Full sign-up is exercised only in DEL-2 with one purpose-made disposable account.
- **Steps:**
  1. Tap 'New here? Create an account' — form gains Full name, Phone (optional), terms checkbox, and 'Read the full Terms…' link
  2. Enter a fake email + 6-char password but leave Full name empty; tap 'Create account' → expect 'Enter your name.'
  3. Enter a name but leave the checkbox unchecked; tap 'Create account' → expect 'Please accept the Terms & Creator Agreement to create an account.'
  4. Toggle the checkbox on/off and verify the ✓ renders
  5. STOP — do not submit a fully valid sign-up
- **Expected:** Both validation errors fire client-side with no network call. Checkbox toggles visually. Toggling back to 'Have an account? Sign in' clears error/notice state.

### AUTH-5 — Forgot password: neutral copy, no account enumeration [P2]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui
- **Risk:** Sends a real reset email via production Supabase to the internal claudetest@nanocrew.dev address — harmless, but don't follow the link (it would let a stale token linger). The /reset-password deep-link screen itself is another surface's scope.
- **Steps:**
  1. In sign-in mode, tap 'Forgot password?' with the email field EMPTY → expect 'Enter your email above…' error
  2. Enter claudetest@nanocrew.dev, tap 'Forgot password?' again
- **Expected:** Tint-colored notice 'If an account exists for claudetest@nanocrew.dev, a password-reset link is on its way…' — identical copy whether or not the account exists (deliberately neutral, per the comment in account.tsx forgotPassword). No error thrown.

### AUTH-6 — Dev-only OAuth auto-start deep link gate (/account?auto=google) [P2]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui
- **⛔ Boundary:** Do not complete the Google/Apple OAuth flow (account creation / credential entry is out of bounds) — verifying the sheet opens and that cancel is silent is the whole test.
- **Steps:**
  1. While signed OUT, open the deep link exp://…/account?auto=google (e.g. via simctl openurl or the Expo dev menu)
  2. Observe the OAuth browser sheet auto-opens once (autoFired ref)
  3. Cancel the sheet
  4. Re-navigate with ?auto=bogus and confirm nothing fires; sign in, then re-open ?auto=google and confirm nothing fires (session guard)
- **Expected:** Auto-fire happens only when __DEV__ && !session && auto ∈ {google, facebook}, and only once per mount. A cancelled sheet sets no error (the /cancelled/i filter in social()).

### AUTH-7 — Social buttons: busy-state opacity + Apple button only on iOS [P2]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui, manual-device-only
- **Steps:**
  1. Verify 'Continue with Apple' renders above 'Continue with Google' (Apple-first rule on iOS)
  2. Tap Google, and while the sheet is opening observe both buttons at 0.5 opacity (busy)
  3. Cancel the sheet
- **Expected:** Buttons disabled while busy; cancel returns silently with no error banner. Completing the OAuth flow end-to-end is manual-device-only (needs a real Google/Apple session).

### AUTH-8 — Legal links open (terms inline link, footer Privacy/Terms) [P2]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui
- **Steps:**
  1. Tap the underlined 'Terms & Creator Agreement' link in the social-consent paragraph
  2. Tap footer 'Privacy' then 'Terms' (visible signed in AND signed out)
- **Expected:** Linking.openURL fires to TERMS_URL (src/lib/legal.ts) and https://nanocrew-api.vercel.app/{privacy,terms}; Safari opens each page (200, real content).

**Entry:** Account tab signed in as claudetest@nanocrew.dev — src/app/account.tsx session branch. Bootstrap effect loads /api/me (stores), /api/platform/admin (probe), /api/creator/connect (payouts), /api/creator/subscription (plan).

  2. Inspect the profile block

  2. If none: verify single row 'No brands yet / Create one in the Studio tab' with no chevron and not pressable
- **Expected:** Rows mirror GET /api/me stores[]. Tapping a brand opens the BrandStore modal for that slug; closing returns to Account. Empty state row has no onPress.

  2. Close the modal
- **Expected:** Purchases modal (src/components/purchases.tsx) opens and closes; Account state (scroll position, sections) intact after close. (Order content itself is the orders surface's scope.)

### PROF-4 — 'Earnings' row visibility is gated on having stores [P2]

  2. With ≥1 store: confirm 'Earnings' appears first and opens the EarningsCockpit modal

### PROF-5 — Bootstrap fetch failures degrade silently (no crash, no stale admin row) [P1]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui
- **Risk:** Killing Metro breaks live-reload; restart it before continuing. Alternatively point EXPO_PUBLIC_API_URL at an unreachable port for one run.
- **Steps:**
  1. With the app loaded and signed in, stop the Metro/dev server (Ctrl-C) — the JS bundle stays alive but /api/* now fails
  2. Navigate away from Account and back (or re-sign-in) to re-fire the bootstrap effect
  3. Observe the screen
  4. Restart the dev server and re-enter Account
- **Expected:** All four fetches have .catch handlers: stores stay [], isAdmin false, payouts null (row falls back to 'Set up payouts'), plan null (no badge, subscription subtitle falls back to 'Choose a plan & top up credits'). No red screen. After restart, data repopulates.

### PROF-6 — /api/me idempotently ensures the creators row and returns profile+stores [P1]

- **Methods:** api
- **Steps:**
  1. API: GET http://localhost:8081/api/me with claudetest Bearer token — twice
  2. Diff the two responses
- **Expected:** 200 both times: { creator: {id,email,name}, stores: [...] }. The upsert never overwrites an existing name/termsVersion (coalesce). Without a token → 401 {error:'unauthorized'}.

## Plan & billing: Paywall, Stripe checkout/portal (BOUNDARY), plan picker re-entry

**Entry:** Account → Commerce → 'Subscription & billing' row opens <Paywall reason='manage'> (src/components/paywall.tsx). Re-entry paths: Studio welcome plan CTA (src/app/studio.tsx sets reason 'subscription_required' after sign-in, and claims the welcome grant on close via POST /api/creator/onboarding) and Eve go-live gating (src/components/eve/eve-home.tsx sets 'subscription_required'/'brand_limit'). Server: /api/creator/subscription, /api/creator/billing/checkout, /api/creator/billing/portal, src/lib/billing.ts. LIVE STRIPE KEYS — hard stop before any payment submission.

### PAY-1 — Paywall renders comp entitlements: Advanced = 'Current plan', 3 tiers + 3 packs [P0]

  1. Tap 'Subscription & billing' (subtitle should read 'Advanced plan · view plans & top up credits' for comp)
  2. Wait for load; inspect the sheet
- **Expected:** Title 'Plans & credits' (reason=manage). Tiers Starter $10/mo (500 cr, 1 brand, in-app store), Pro $50/mo (3,000 cr, 3 brands, website+domain), Advanced $175/mo (12,000 cr, unlimited brands). Advanced card shows outlined 'Current plan'; Starter/Pro buttons read 'Switch' (entitlements.active=true). Packs: 500/$5, 1,500/$15, 5,000/$50 with no discount line (comp creditRateMultiplier=1). 'Manage billing in Stripe ↗' button visible (active=true). Footer says web-purchase copy, and in __DEV__ an 'IAP status: …' diagnostic line appears.

### PAY-2 — GET /api/creator/subscription payload correctness for comp [P0]

- **Methods:** api
- **Steps:**
  1. API: GET http://localhost:8081/api/creator/subscription with claudetest Bearer
- **Expected:** 200 with entitlements {plan:'advanced', status:'comp', active:true, maxBrands:9999, monthlyCredits:0, website:true, creditRateMultiplier:1, currentPeriodEnd:null}, brandCount:number, canLaunch:true, tiers[3], creditPacks[3]. No token → 401.

### PAY-3 — Subscribe/'Switch' → Stripe Checkout session — STOP AT THE STRIPE PAGE [P0]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui, api
- **⛔ Boundary:** HARD STOP: LIVE Stripe keys. Never enter card details, never tap Pay/Subscribe on the Stripe page. Note the acceptable side effects: a live-mode Checkout Session AND (first time) a real Stripe Customer for claudetest are created by ensureCustomer() — no charge occurs; optionally delete the stray customer in the Stripe dashboard afterwards. Verify instead: the {url} response shape, that the URL host is checkout.stripe.com, and that abandoning it changes no entitlements (re-GET /api/creator/subscription).
- **Steps:**
  1. In the Paywall tap 'Switch' on Starter
  2. iapReady() is false in the simulator dev build (StoreKit products unavailable — confirm via the __DEV__ 'IAP status' line, e.g. 'connect failed'/'0/6 products'), so it falls through to POST /api/creator/billing/checkout {kind:'subscription', plan:'starter'}
  3. The in-app browser (expo-web-browser) opens the returned checkout.stripe.com URL
  4. VERIFY the page renders 'Nano Crew' + the Starter price, then CLOSE the browser sheet immediately
  5. Confirm the Paywall re-loads entitlements on return (still comp/advanced — nothing changed)
- **Expected:** Checkout URL is returned (200 {url}) and opens; closing the sheet leaves plan unchanged. Spinner shows on the tapped button only while busy; double-taps blocked by the busy guard.

### PAY-4 — Buy credit pack → Stripe payment-mode checkout — STOP AT THE STRIPE PAGE [P0]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui, api
- **⛔ Boundary:** Same live-Stripe hard stop as PAY-3: never submit payment. The credit-grant webhook path is therefore untestable here — verify only session creation + the no-grant-on-abandon invariant.
- **Steps:**
  1. Tap 'buy' on the 500-credit pack
  2. IAP is unavailable in sim (creditProductId exists but iapOn=false) → POST /api/creator/billing/checkout {kind:'credit_pack', packId:'pack_500'}
  3. Verify the Stripe page shows 'Nano Crew — 500 credits' at $5.00, then CLOSE the sheet
  4. Re-check GET /api/creator/credits: balance unchanged, no 'topup' ledger row
- **Expected:** Payment-mode session created with unit_amount 500 (multiplier 1 for comp). Abandoning grants nothing — credits are only granted by the platform-api webhook after real payment.

### PAY-5 — 'Manage billing in Stripe' portal: comp with no Stripe customer → 409 note [P1]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui, api
- **Steps:**
  1. Tap 'Manage billing in Stripe ↗'
  2. API twin: POST http://localhost:8081/api/creator/billing/portal with claudetest Bearer
- **Expected:** createBillingPortalSession finds no subscriptions.stripeCustomerId row for claudetest → 409 {error:'no_billing_account'} → Paywall shows the note 'No billing account yet — subscribe first.' No browser opens. (ensureCustomer in PAY-3 does NOT persist a customer id to the DB, so this stays 409 even after PAY-3.)

### PAY-6 — Paywall load-failure path and malformed-payload guard (no crash) [P1]

  2. Observe; restart the server, close and reopen the Paywall
- **Expected:** Loading spinner resolves to note 'Could not load plans. Please try again.' with data=null — the tiers/creditPacks Array.isArray guard means an error object can never reach render (the old crash). Reopen after restart loads normally.

- **Expected:** (a) 401 unauthorized; (b) 400 'kind required'; (c) 400 'plan required'; (d) 400 'packId required'; (e) 502 with 'unknown pack' (thrown in createCreditPackCheckout, mapped by the catch). Portal without auth → 401.

### PAY-8 — Missing Stripe price config maps to 503 + friendly copy (config-off variant) [P2]

  2. GET /api/creator/credits — find one ledger row {delta:+1000, reason:'subscription_grant'} (refId onboard_welcome:<creatorId>) and balance +1000
  3. POST the same body again → expect NO second grant, balance unchanged
  4. UI re-entry: from Studio welcome, choose a plan card (onChoose('subscribe')) while signed in → Paywall opens with reason 'subscription_required' (title 'Launch your store'); close it → studio.tsx claims the same grant (already claimed → no-op)
- **Expected:** Exactly one subscription_grant ledger row per account ever (unique refId guard). Paywall title/subtitle vary correctly by reason: manage='Plans & credits', subscription_required='Launch your store', brand_limit='You've reached your brand limit'.

  2. Tap 'Free up a slot — delete a brand'
- **Expected:** Title 'You've reached your brand limit', subtitle about upgrading, and the onFreeSlot button rendered above the tiers; tapping calls the callback (eve-home/studio close the paywall).

Real IAP purchase + /api/creator/billing/iap-verify are manual-device-only (TestFlight build with App Store Connect products) and even there must stop before confirming the Apple payment sheet.

## Credits: balance, ledger, price list, and the comp exemption end-to-end

**Entry:** Server: GET /api/creator/credits (src/app/api/creator/credits+api.ts) backed by src/lib/credits.ts + src/lib/comp.ts. UI: balance strings render in the spend surfaces (go-live-composer.tsx line 276, scene-short-composer.tsx line 260, brand-deck, studio-composer) — the Account screen itself links to top-ups via the Paywall. claudetest@nanocrew.dev is confirmed present in COMP_EMAILS in .env.local.

### CRED-1 — GET /api/creator/credits: balance + costs + videoModels + ledger (≤20, desc) [P0]

- **Methods:** api, simulator-ui
- **Steps:**
  1. API: GET http://localhost:8081/api/creator/credits with claudetest Bearer
  2. Validate: costs equals CREDIT_COSTS (video_voiceover 25, video_veo 400, design_generate 8, logo_generate 8, merge 8, design_edit 8, tryon 6, model_shots 25, preview_shots 16, revision 60)
  3. Validate ledger rows are newest-first, each {delta, reason, balanceAfter, createdAt}, max 20
- **Expected:** 200. On the very first call for a fresh account the route ensures the credit account and the ledger contains a {delta:+300, reason:'signup_bonus', balanceAfter:300} row (SIGNUP_BONUS). Repeat calls are idempotent (no duplicate bonus).

### CRED-2 — COMP EXEMPTION END-TO-END: a paid AI spend does not debit claudetest [P0]

- **Methods:** api, simulator-ui
- **Risk:** Spends real Gemini image API cost (~$0.04) on the platform account — one call is the agreed price of proving the exemption. Keep to a single generation.
- **Steps:**
  1. API: GET /api/creator/credits — record balance B and the ledger row count
  2. POST /api/generate {prompt:'minimal line-art fox logo', purpose:'design'} with claudetest Bearer (normally debits design_generate=8)
  3. Wait for 200 with the generated image payload
  4. GET /api/creator/credits again
- **Expected:** Balance still exactly B and NO new negative ledger row — debitCredits() returns early for comp creators (src/lib/credits.ts line 97 → isCompCreator) so neither the guarded UPDATE nor the ledger insert runs. The generation itself succeeds (comp ≠ blocked). UI variant: run one generation from the Design tab and confirm any visible 'balance: N credits' string is unchanged after.

### CRED-3 — Comp account can never hit the 402 credit gate (even at low balance) [P0]

- **Methods:** unit, api
- **Steps:**
  1. Unit: call debitCredits('<comp-creator-id>', 999999, 'design_generate') against a test double where isCompCreator resolves true and balance is 0
  2. Assert it resolves to the balance and throws nothing
  3. API sanity: as claudetest request an expensive op guard (e.g. POST /api/video body with a valid design) — expect it never returns 402 regardless of balance
- **Expected:** The comp early-return precedes the balance guard, so InsufficientCreditsError is unreachable for comp accounts.

### CRED-4 — Non-comp 402 shape: insufficient_credits with needed/balance (unit) [P1]

- **Methods:** unit, api
- **Steps:**
  1. Unit-test debitCredits for a non-comp creator with balance 5 and amount 8: expect InsufficientCreditsError {needed:8, balance:5} and NO balance change / no ledger row (the guarded UPDATE matched nothing)
  2. Unit-test the route mapping (generate/video/model-shots catch blocks): the error becomes 402 {error:'insufficient_credits', needed, balance}
  3. Optional live check requires a throwaway NON-comp account (see DEL-2's disposable account before deleting it): drain nothing — a fresh account has 300 credits, so call POST /api/video (video_veo=400) once → expect 402 and balance still 300
- **Expected:** Exact 402 JSON contract the composers parse ('Not enough credits — need X, you have Y. Top up in Account.' in FinalizeSheet).

### CRED-5 — Debit atomicity: concurrent spends cannot drive balance negative (unit) [P2]

- **Methods:** unit
- **Steps:**
  1. Unit/integration test against a test DB: seed balance 10, fire two concurrent debitCredits(…, 8) calls
  2. Assert exactly one succeeds (balance 2, one ledger row) and the other throws InsufficientCreditsError
- **Expected:** The single guarded `UPDATE … WHERE balance >= amount` prevents the check-then-decrement race described in credits.ts.

### CRED-6 — Balance display surfaces read the same number the API returns [P1]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui, api
- **Steps:**
  1. GET /api/creator/credits → note balance
  2. In the app open a spend surface that prints it (Studio → a brand → Go-Live composer, or the scene-short composer)
  3. Compare the rendered 'balance: N credits' text
- **Expected:** Rendered N equals the API balance; after CRED-2's comp-exempt generation it is unchanged.

### CRED-7 — Credits endpoint auth: 401 without/with-garbage token [P1]

- **Methods:** api
- **Steps:**
  1. GET /api/creator/credits with no Authorization header, then with 'Bearer garbage'
- **Expected:** 401 {error:'unauthorized'} both times; no credit account side effects.

## Server-side rate limiting on paid AI endpoints

**Entry:** src/lib/rate-limit.ts (fixed window, rate_limits table, fail-open) via guardRate in the paid routes. Cheapest trip wire: POST /api/creator/model-videos guards `model-video:<creatorId>` at 4 per 600s BEFORE body validation, so invalid bodies increment the bucket without any AI spend.

### RATE-1 — 429 after the window cap, with retryAfter + Retry-After header (zero AI cost) [P0]

- **Methods:** api
- **Risk:** Pollutes the production rate_limits row for claudetest for up to 10 minutes — run this AFTER any model-video tests other surfaces need, or wait out the window.
- **Steps:**
  1. API: POST http://localhost:8081/api/creator/model-videos with claudetest Bearer and body {} — repeat 4 times; each returns 400 'productId required' (guard already counted them)
  2. Send a 5th identical request within 10 minutes
- **Expected:** 5th returns 429 {error:'rate_limited', retryAfter:<1..600>} with a matching Retry-After header. Requests 1–4 never reached any paid API (validation 400s).

### RATE-2 — Comp accounts are still rate-limited (comp exempts credits, not abuse caps) [P1]

- **Methods:** api
- **Steps:**
  1. Observe RATE-1 ran as claudetest (a comp account) and still produced the 429
- **Expected:** guardRate executes before any comp/credit logic in every paid route, so COMP_EMAILS does not bypass rate limits.

### RATE-3 — Window expiry resets the bucket [P2]

- **Methods:** api, unit
- **Steps:**
  1. After RATE-1's 429, wait until retryAfter elapses (≤10 min) — or unit-test rateLimit('t', 2, 1) with a 1s window
  2. Send one more invalid-body request
- **Expected:** Back to 400 (allowed; fresh window with count=1). Unit variant asserts the expired branch re-inserts count 1 with windowStart now().

### RATE-4 — Fail-open: a limiter DB error never blocks the request (unit) [P2]

- **Methods:** unit
- **Steps:**
  1. Unit-test rateLimit with the db mock throwing on select
  2. Assert it resolves true (and guardRate returns null) instead of throwing
- **Expected:** Only RateLimitError propagates; infrastructure errors are swallowed per the design comment.

## Payouts (Stripe Connect) row

**Entry:** Account → Commerce → payout row (title varies: 'Set up payouts' / 'Finish payout setup' / 'Payouts active' from GET /api/creator/connect). Tap → POST /api/creator/connect → Linking.openURL of the Stripe-hosted onboarding link. Files: account.tsx openPayouts, src/app/api/creator/connect+api.ts, src/lib/connect.ts. LIVE Stripe — creating a Connect account is a persistent live-mode object.

### PAYOUT-1 — Fresh-account payout status renders 'Set up payouts' with ↗ [P1]

- **Methods:** api, simulator-ui
- **Steps:**
  1. API: GET /api/creator/connect as claudetest — expect {connected:false, chargesEnabled:false, needsOnboarding:true} if no Connect account exists
  2. UI: verify the row title 'Set up payouts', subtitle 'Get paid when your brand sells', trailing '↗', not tinted
- **Expected:** Row state mirrors the GET payload; the '✓/Payouts active' tinted variant appears only when chargesEnabled is true.

### PAYOUT-2 — Tapping the payout row — STOP BEFORE the Stripe onboarding form [P1]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui, api
- **⛔ Boundary:** HARD STOP at the Stripe-hosted form: never enter identity/bank details and never submit — this is a LIVE Express account onboarding. Note the side effect: the POST itself creates a persistent live Connect account bound to claudetest (no money movement); flag it for cleanup in the Stripe dashboard rather than repeating the tap.
- **Steps:**
  1. Tap the payout row once
  2. POST /api/creator/connect fires; if it returns {url}, Safari opens the Stripe-hosted Express onboarding page
  3. VERIFY the page loads (connect.stripe.com), then close Safari immediately and return to the app
  4. Re-GET /api/creator/connect: connected may now be true with chargesEnabled false → row should now read 'Finish payout setup'
- **Expected:** URL opens or, if Connect isn't enabled on the platform account, the row surfaces 'Payouts aren't available yet — Connect is being set up.' (503 branch) / 'Payouts aren't available yet.' inline error.

### PAYOUT-3 — Connect endpoint error mapping (503 config, 502 other, 401 auth) [P2]

- **Methods:** api, unit
- **Steps:**
  1. POST /api/creator/connect with no token → 401
  2. Unit: with STRIPE_SECRET_KEY unset the route returns 503 'payments are not configured yet'; a /connect|platform|sign up for/i Stripe error maps to the friendly 503 copy, anything else 502
- **Expected:** Status/copy mapping exactly as in connect+api.ts lines 34 and 41–45.

## Push notifications: registration lifecycle (PUSH_ENABLED)

**Entry:** On sign-in, src/hooks/use-auth.ts calls registerForPush (src/lib/push.ts, PUSH_ENABLED=true): permission → getExpoPushTokenAsync → POST /api/creator/push-token. Server: src/app/api/creator/push-token+api.ts (POST upsert / DELETE scoped). Deep-link handling: attachReviewDeepLink mounted in src/app/_layout.tsx.

### PUSH-1 — Simulator sign-in: push registration is a silent best-effort no-op [P1]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui, manual-device-only
- **Steps:**
  1. Fresh-install the app in the simulator, sign in as claudetest
  2. Watch for the iOS notification-permission alert; grant it if shown
  3. Confirm the app never blocks, spins, or errors on the Account screen
  4. API: check no bogus row was written — the device_tokens table gains nothing because getExpoPushTokenAsync fails on the simulator (no APNs) and the catch swallows it
- **Expected:** Sign-in completes normally; push failure is invisible (the try/catch in registerForPush). Real token minting + receiving a push are manual-device-only (EAS dev/prod build on a physical iPhone).

### PUSH-2 — POST /api/creator/push-token: upsert semantics with a synthetic token [P1]

- **Methods:** api
- **Steps:**
  1. API: POST /api/creator/push-token with claudetest Bearer and {token:'ExponentPushToken[claudetest-qa-1]', platform:'ios'} → expect {ok:true}
  2. POST the exact same body again → still {ok:true} (idempotent; lastSeenAt bumped, creator re-pointed)
  3. Clean up with DELETE /api/creator/push-token?token=ExponentPushToken[claudetest-qa-1] → {ok:true}
- **Expected:** Upsert on the token unique key; no duplicate rows; delete removes it. (The synthetic token can never receive a push — Expo's push service just rejects it — safe to create/delete.)

### PUSH-3 — Token validation: 400 unless it starts with 'ExponentPushToken' [P1]

- **Methods:** api
- **Steps:**
  1. POST bodies: {}, {token:'abc'}, {token:'expo-push-token'} — each with valid auth
  2. DELETE without the ?token param
- **Expected:** All POSTs → 400 'valid Expo token required'; the DELETE → 400 'token required'. No auth → 401 on both methods.

### PUSH-4 — DELETE is scoped to the caller (no cross-creator notification DoS) [P1]

- **Methods:** api, unit
- **Steps:**
  1. Seed a token for claudetest via PUSH-2
  2. From a SECOND authed identity (the throwaway account from DEL-2, before deleting it — or a unit test on the where clause) DELETE ?token=<claudetest's token>
  3. As claudetest, verify the token still exists (re-POST returns ok and, if DB access is available, the row was never removed)
- **Expected:** The delete matches token AND creatorId, so another creator's DELETE silently removes nothing (still {ok:true} but no row change).

### PUSH-5 — revision_ready push deep-links to Studio review (cold + warm) [P2]

- **Methods:** manual-device-only, unit
- **Steps:**
  1. Manual device with a real token: send a test push via Expo's push tool with data {kind:'revision_ready', slug:'<store>', name:'<brand>'}
  2. Tap it warm (app open) and cold (app killed)
- **Expected:** Both route to /studio with reviewSlug/reviewName params (attachReviewDeepLink handles getLastNotificationResponseAsync for cold start). Simulator can only unit-test the route(data) mapping.

## Sign out + account deletion

**Entry:** Danger zone at the bottom of the signed-in Account screen: neutral 'Sign out' button (supabase.auth.signOut) and red underlined 'Delete account' link → native Alert → DELETE /api/me (src/app/api/me+api.ts) then signOut. PRODUCTION DB — deletion cascades creators → stores/designs/orders/subscriptions/credits/device tokens + best-effort Supabase auth identity removal.

### OUT-1 — Sign out returns to auth screen and clears per-session state [P0]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui
- **Steps:**
  1. Signed in as claudetest, tap 'Sign out'
  2. Verify the auth (join hero) branch renders and the tab still works
  3. Sign back in and confirm stores/plan/payout rows repopulate (the session effect re-runs all four bootstrap fetches)
- **Expected:** Session → null flips the branch; stores=[], isAdmin=false, plan=null, payouts=null are reset by the effect's early return. Re-sign-in restores everything. No crash, no stale ADVANCED badge on the logged-out view.

### OUT-2 — Known gap: sign-out does NOT unregister the push token [P2]

- **Methods:** api, simulator-ui
- **Risk:** Documented divergence between push-token+api.ts's stated intent and the client; worth a follow-up ticket.
- **Steps:**
  1. Seed a synthetic token via PUSH-2, sign out in the UI
  2. API: verify the device_tokens row still exists (nothing in the client calls DELETE /api/creator/push-token on sign-out)
- **Expected:** Row persists — the server DELETE exists 'e.g. on sign-out' per its comment but no client call site does; on a shared device the previous user could keep receiving creator pushes until another account signs in on it (the upsert re-points the token). Log as a product observation, not a test failure.

### DEL-1 — Delete account: Alert copy + Cancel is a true no-op (claudetest) [P0]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui, api
- **⛔ Boundary:** NEVER tap 'Delete' while signed in as claudetest@nanocrew.dev — this is the production DB and the comp account other test surfaces depend on (and it's pinned in .env.local COMP_EMAILS). The confirm path is exercised only in DEL-2.
- **Steps:**
  1. Tap 'Delete account'
  2. Verify the native Alert: title 'Delete account?', body 'This permanently deletes your account, brands, designs, and data. This cannot be undone.', buttons Cancel / Delete (destructive-red)
  3. Tap CANCEL
  4. Verify still signed in; GET /api/me and /api/creator/credits still 200 with unchanged data
- **Expected:** Cancel dismisses with zero network traffic (deleteAccount only runs from the Delete button's onPress). Account fully intact.

### DEL-2 — Full deletion end-to-end — ONLY with a purpose-made disposable account [P1]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui, api
- **⛔ Boundary:** Deletion is only ever run against the disposable account created for this test — never claudetest, never any real creator. The throwaway must have no store, no Stripe activity, and no Printful products before deleting (nothing to orphan).
- **Risk:** Production Supabase: creating+deleting one auth user leaves at most an auth audit trail. If email confirmation is enforced on prod signups, use a claudetest+suffix alias so the confirmation email is receivable.
- **Steps:**
  1. Create ONE throwaway email account in the app (e.g. claudetest+del@nanocrew.dev, satisfying AUTH-4's form incl. terms checkbox) — this account is also the non-comp fixture for CRED-4/PUSH-4, so run those first
  2. As the throwaway: GET /api/me (creates the creators row), GET /api/creator/credits (grants the 300 signup bonus)
  3. Tap Delete account → Delete
  4. Verify: app lands on the signed-out auth view; the throwaway's old Bearer now gets 401 from /api/me; signing in with its password fails (auth identity removed when SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY is set); its creators/credit rows are gone (cascade)
- **Expected:** DELETE /api/me returns {ok:true}, then signOut flips the UI. All creator-owned rows cascade away. Note the code's own caveat: live Printful sync products / active Stripe subs are NOT auto-removed — the throwaway must never have had either.

### DEL-3 — DELETE /api/me auth + failure paths [P1]

- **Methods:** api, simulator-ui, unit
- **Steps:**
  1. API: DELETE http://localhost:8081/api/me with no token → 401
  2. UI failure path: stop the dev server, tap Delete → Delete on the throwaway (before DEL-2's real run) or simulate via a unit test of deleteAccount()
  3. Observe the inline error
- **Expected:** 401 unauthorized without a token. On a failed request the screen shows 'Could not delete account' in red, the user stays signed in, and busy resets (no half-deleted UI state).

  2. Read each plan card: struck base price, discounted '1st mo' price, blurb

Pro (· POPULAR, accent border/glow): $60 struck → $51. Advanced: $199 struck → $169.15. (Apple prices used because Platform.OS==='ios'; DISCOUNT=0.15, Math.round on cents.)

### WELC-4 — Annual toggle swaps banner and yearly pricing [P2]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui
- **Steps:**

- **Expected:** Paywall (reason subscription_required) opens once, unprompted. On close, a POST /api/creator/onboarding with {path:'subscribe'} is logged; server responds 200 with planActive:false for a free-plan account and no welcome grant.

  3. Sign in on /account, go to Studio → paywall opens; close it
  4. Terminate + relaunch again, go to Studio
- **Expected:** Paywall opens on the first post-sign-in Studio visit (intent read from AsyncStorage). After the intent is consumed (key removed + intentHandledRef), later launches show no paywall.

(account.tsx forgotPassword → sendPasswordReset in src/lib/oauth.ts, redirect nanocrew://reset-password). src/app/reset-password.tsx phases: verifying → ready|invalid → done; native builds the recovery session via Linking.getInitialURL + createSessionFromUrl.

  2. Watch the screen phases
  3. Tap 'Back to sign in'
- **Expected:** Brief 'Verifying your reset link…' spinner → 'This reset link is invalid or has expired. Request a new one from the sign-in screen.' (createSessionFromUrl throws 'No session in callback'). Back button router.replace's to /account. A Cancel link is also present.

### RST-4 — Already-signed-in visit skips straight to the password form [P1]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui
- **⛔ Boundary:** Do NOT submit a new password for claudetest@nanocrew.dev — it would invalidate TEST_ACCOUNT_PASSWORD in .env.local for every other suite. Verify the form renders + validation (RST-5), then Cancel. Full submission only on a throwaway (RST-6).

  2. xcrun simctl openurl booted "nanocrew://reset-password"
  3. Observe the phase, then tap Cancel

### RST-5 — New-password form validation [P1]

  2. (a) Enter '12345' in both fields → Update password
  3. (b) Enter 'abcdef' / 'abcdeg' → Update password
