### RST-6 — Full reset round-trip on a throwaway account [P1]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui, manual-device-only
- **Risk:** Requires inbox access for nanocrew.dev plus-aliases (Joe). PKCE: the reset must be REQUESTED from the same app install that opens the link — requesting from the simulator and opening via simctl openurl on that same simulator keeps the code verifier in AsyncStorage. simctl openurl of an https Supabase link opens Safari first; the redirect must hop to the nanocrew:// scheme — if it stalls in Safari, copy the final nanocrew:// URL and openurl it directly.
- **Steps:**
  1. With throwaway claudetest+rst@nanocrew.dev (created in SUP-2) signed OUT, request a reset from the Account screen
  2. Retrieve the email from the claudetest inbox (plus-alias), copy the Supabase action link
  3. Open it on the simulator: xcrun simctl openurl booted "<link URL>" (the link 302s through Supabase and deep-links nanocrew://reset-password with the code)
  4. Set a new 6+ char password, confirm, tap Update password
  5. Tap Continue; sign out; sign in with the NEW password, then confirm the OLD one fails
- **Expected:** ready phase after link verification → 'Password updated / Your password has been changed. You're signed in.' → Continue lands on the signed-in /account. New password works; old returns invalid credentials.

### RST-7 — Warm-app link open (known fragility: getInitialURL only) [P2]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui
- **Risk:** src/app/reset-password.tsx line 49 — no Linking.addEventListener('url') subscription.
- **Steps:**
  1. With the app ALREADY RUNNING in the foreground signed out, open a fresh valid reset link via simctl
  2. Observe the phase reached
- **Expected:** reset-password.tsx only reads Linking.getInitialURL() (the cold-launch URL) — a warm open may deliver the URL as an event instead, leaving no initial URL → 'invalid' phase despite a valid link. Record actual behavior; if it fails warm this is a confirmed defect (expo-router may still pass params, making it work — verify).

### RST-8 — updateUser failure surfaces the server error [P2]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui
- **Steps:**
  1. Reach the ready phase via a recovery link, wait past the token TTL (or reuse an already-consumed link), submit a valid new password
- **Expected:** Red error from supabase.auth.updateUser (e.g. token expired / session missing) shown inline; phase stays 'ready'; busy cleared.

## Session persistence + token plumbing (use-auth, apiFetch, server verify)

**Entry:** src/hooks/use-auth.ts (getSession + onAuthStateChange, AsyncStorage-backed via src/lib/supabase.ts), src/lib/api.ts (apiFetch Bearer attach, GET-only 502/503/504 retry, readJson/ApiError), src/lib/auth.ts (local ES256 JWT verify). Dev API base: http://localhost:8081.

### SES-1 — Session survives app termination and relaunch [P0]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui
- **Steps:**
  1. Sign in as claudetest@nanocrew.dev
  2. xcrun simctl terminate booted com.nanocrew.app
  3. Relaunch from the springboard (or npm run ios without uninstalling)
  4. Open the Account tab
- **Expected:** Profile renders directly — no login form, no flash of the signed-out gate longer than the loading spinner (useAuth loading → getSession from AsyncStorage). Studio shows Eve home, not the gate.

### SES-2 — Cold-start authed bootstrap makes clean 200s [P1]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui
- **Steps:**
  1. After SES-1 relaunch, watch the dev-server request log while Account loads
- **Expected:** GET /api/me → 200 (never 401), plus /api/platform/admin, /api/creator/connect, /api/creator/subscription with Bearer headers. If the stored access token had expired, supabase-js refreshes it first (autoRefreshToken) — no user-visible failure.

### SES-3 — Bearer token attach + 401 without it (direct API) [P0]

- **Methods:** api
- **Steps:**
  1. TOKEN=$(curl -s "$EXPO_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL/auth/v1/token?grant_type=password" -H "apikey: $EXPO_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_PUBLISHABLE_KEY" -H 'Content-Type: application/json' -d '{"email":"claudetest@nanocrew.dev","password":"<TEST_ACCOUNT_PASSWORD>"}' | jq -r .access_token)
  2. curl -s http://localhost:8081/api/me → expect 401
  3. curl -s http://localhost:8081/api/me -H "Authorization: Bearer $TOKEN" → expect 200
- **Expected:** No header → 401 {"error":"unauthorized"}. Valid token → 200 {creator:{id,email,name},stores:[…]}.

### SES-4 — Tampered / expired / wrong-shape tokens are rejected locally [P1]

- **Methods:** api, unit
- **Steps:**
  1. Take $TOKEN, flip one character in the signature segment → curl /api/me with it
  2. Use a token from a previous day (expired exp) if available
  3. Send 'Bearer not.a.jwt' and 'Bearer abc'
- **Expected:** All → 401 unauthorized (src/lib/auth.ts verifies ES256 signature, exp, aud='authenticated', iss locally; malformed tokens fail parsing).

### SES-5 — apiFetch retries GETs only on 502/503/504 [P2]

- **Methods:** unit
- **Risk:** No jest/vitest configured — follow the existing tsx-script test pattern (package.json test:lipsync).
- **Steps:**
  1. Write an ad-hoc test (repo pattern: src/lib/__tests__/, run with npx tsx) stubbing global.fetch
  2. Case A: GET returns 503, 503, 200 → assert 3 calls, final 200, delays ~400/1200ms
  3. Case B: GET returns 503 three times → assert the 503 Response is returned after 2 retries
  4. Case C: POST returns 503 → assert exactly 1 call, 503 returned
  5. Case D: POST fetch throws → assert it propagates on the first attempt; GET fetch throws twice then resolves → returned
- **Expected:** Matches src/lib/api.ts: RETRY_STATUS {502,503,504}, RETRY_DELAYS_MS [400,1200], retryable only when method resolves to GET.

### SES-6 — readJson maps bodies to ApiError correctly [P2]

- **Methods:** unit
- **Steps:**
  1. Unit-test readJson with synthetic Responses:
  2. (a) 402 body {"error":"insufficient credits"} → ApiError message 'insufficient credits', status 402, body parsed
  3. (b) 502 HTML '<html>bad gateway' → ApiError 'Request failed (502)', body = raw text
  4. (c) 200 valid JSON → returns object; (d) 200 empty body → returns null
- **Expected:** All four contracts hold per src/lib/api.ts readJson.

### SES-7 — verifyToken hardening (alg pinning, claims) [P2]

- **Methods:** unit
- **Steps:**
  1. Unit test src/lib/auth.ts getUserFromRequest with SUPABASE_JWKS env set to a generated P-256 JWK:
  2. reject alg 'none' and HS256 tokens; reject missing kid; reject exp in the past; reject aud !== 'authenticated'; reject wrong iss; accept a validly-signed token and extract id/email/name/phone/terms_version from user_metadata
- **Expected:** Only the fully valid ES256 token yields an AuthedUser; every other case returns null (→ 401 at routes).

### SES-8 — Push registration on sign-in is best-effort on simulator [P2]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui, manual-device-only
- **Steps:**
  1. Watch the console/dev log while signing in on the simulator (no real push support)
- **Expected:** registerForPush fires after the session lands (use-auth.ts) and fails/no-ops quietly — no crash, no user-facing error. Real push-token registration is device-only.

**Entry:** src/app/index.tsx (Redirect → /studio), src/components/app-tabs.tsx (Eve/Design/Market/Account), per-tab gates in src/app/studio.tsx (Meet Eve gate) and src/app/design.tsx (line 1654 gate); Market has no gate. Scheme 'nanocrew' from app.json; drive with xcrun simctl openurl booted.

### GATE-1 — Studio signed-out gate [P0]

## GET /api/me — profile bootstrap + creators upsert

**Entry:** src/app/api/me+api.ts GET: verifies the Bearer token (src/lib/auth.ts), upserts the creators row (capturing name/phone/terms_version from user_metadata, coalesce = never overwrite), returns {creator, stores}. Called by account.tsx and studio.tsx on sign-in. Dev URL: http://localhost:8081/api/me.

### ME-1 — 401 without / with invalid Authorization [P0]

- **Methods:** api
- **Steps:**
  1. curl -si http://localhost:8081/api/me
  2. curl -si http://localhost:8081/api/me -H 'Authorization: Bearer garbage'
  3. curl -si http://localhost:8081/api/me -H 'Authorization: Basic abc'
- **Expected:** All three → HTTP 401 {"error":"unauthorized"} (only 'Bearer ' prefixed, verifiable tokens pass).

### ME-2 — 200 with the comp test account's token [P0]

- **Methods:** api
- **Steps:**
  1. Obtain $TOKEN per SES-3
  2. curl -s http://localhost:8081/api/me -H "Authorization: Bearer $TOKEN" | jq
- **Expected:** {creator:{id:<uuid>, email:'claudetest@nanocrew.dev', name:<string|null>}, stores:[{id,name,slug,status,logoUrl,ogImageUrl,siteAssets,deploymentUrl,customDomain}...]} — stores scoped to this creator only (tenant scoping via accessibleStoreIds).

### ME-3 — Upsert is idempotent and never overwrites recorded fields [P1]

- **Methods:** api
- **Steps:**
  1. Call GET /api/me 3 times in a row with the same token
  2. Compare creator.name and stores each time
- **Expected:** Identical responses; no duplicate creators row (PK conflict → coalesce update); terms acceptance timestamp is not re-stamped for an existing row (coalesce keeps the original).

### ME-4 — First-ever call creates the creators row from token metadata [P1]

- **Methods:** api
- **Steps:**
  1. Using the SUP-2 throwaway's token (freshly signed up, never called /api/me): curl GET /api/me
  2. Inspect creator.name in the response
- **Expected:** 200 with creator.name = the signup Full name (user_metadata.name), email = the throwaway address; row created with terms_version '2026-06-18'. stores = [].

## DELETE /api/me — account deletion (known bug: auth identity survives)

**Entry:** Account tab signed in → red 'Delete account' link → native Alert confirm → apiFetch DELETE /api/me → supabase.auth.signOut(). Route: src/app/api/me+api.ts DELETE — deletes productLikes + creators (cascades stores/designs/orders/credits), then best-effort deletes the Supabase auth user ONLY if SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY is set. CONFIRMED IN THIS ENV: .env.local defines SUPABASE_SECRET_KEY but NOT SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY (the name the route reads at line 83), so the auth-identity delete silently never runs.

### DEL-1 — Deletion removes DB data but the auth identity survives (confirm the bug) [P0]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui, api
- **⛔ Boundary:** THROWAWAY ACCOUNTS ONLY, created for this test. NEVER run deletion against claudetest@nanocrew.dev, josephsardella@gmail.com, or any creator with stores/orders — this is the PRODUCTION database and the cascade permanently destroys stores, designs, orders, subscriptions, and credits; the route explicitly does NOT clean up live Printful products or Stripe subscriptions.
- **Risk:** The surviving auth identity means deleted users can silently resurrect accounts — App Store data-deletion compliance gap. Also leaves an orphaned Supabase auth user per deleted throwaway.
- **Steps:**
  1. Create + sign in as throwaway claudetest+del1@nanocrew.dev (SUP-2), note the creator id from GET /api/me
  2. Account tab → tap 'Delete account' → Alert 'Delete account?' with body 'This permanently deletes your account, brands, designs, and data. This cannot be undone.' → tap the destructive 'Delete'
  3. Observe: request succeeds and the app signs out to the auth form
  4. Now sign in AGAIN with the same email/password
  5. Call GET /api/me with the fresh token
- **Expected:** BUG CONFIRMATION: the re-sign-in SUCCEEDS (the Supabase auth user was never deleted because SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY is unset) and GET /api/me silently re-creates a brand-new creators row (same id, stores now empty, name re-captured from token metadata). If the service key were configured, the second sign-in would fail with invalid credentials — that is the fixed-state assertion.

### DEL-2 — Alert cancel aborts deletion [P1]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui
- **Steps:**
  1. Signed in as a throwaway, tap 'Delete account', then tap 'Cancel' in the Alert
- **Expected:** No DELETE request in the dev-server log; still signed in; all data intact (GET /api/me unchanged).

### DEL-3 — DELETE requires auth [P1]

- **Methods:** api
- **Steps:**
  1. curl -si -X DELETE http://localhost:8081/api/me
- **Expected:** 401 {"error":"unauthorized"} — no data touched.

### DEL-4 — Repeat DELETE is idempotent; tokens outlive deletion (bug side-effect) [P2]

- **Methods:** api
- **Steps:**
  1. After DEL-1's first deletion, reuse the SAME (pre-deletion) access token: curl -X DELETE http://localhost:8081/api/me -H "Authorization: Bearer $OLD_TOKEN"
- **Expected:** 200 {"ok":true} — deleting the already-gone row is a no-op, no 500. Note the old JWT still authenticates until exp because the auth identity survives (record as part of the bug's blast radius).

### DEL-5 — Client error path when the route fails [P2]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui
- **Steps:**
  1. Stop the dev server (Ctrl-C on npx expo start), then tap Delete → confirm in the app
- **Expected:** Red 'Could not delete account' error on the Account screen; user remains signed in; busy cleared. Restart the dev server afterwards.

## POST /api/creator/onboarding — welcome-path credit grants

**Entry:** src/app/api/creator/onboarding+api.ts. Client trigger: studio.tsx paywall-close after the subscribe intent (PIPE-1). Direct: curl -X POST http://localhost:8081/api/creator/onboarding -H "Authorization: Bearer $TOKEN" -H 'Content-Type: application/json' -d '{"path":…}'. Grants: free/subscribe ensure the credit account (+300 SIGNUP_BONUS once); subscribe additionally grants 1000 WELCOME_CREDITS once, only when a paid plan is genuinely active (getEntitlements), guarded by ledger refId 'onboard_welcome:<creatorId>'.

### ONB-1 — 401 without a token [P0]

- **Methods:** api
- **Steps:**
  1. curl -si -X POST http://localhost:8081/api/creator/onboarding -d '{"path":"free"}'
- **Expected:** 401 {"error":"unauthorized"}.

### ONB-2 — 400 on missing/invalid/malformed path [P0]

- **Methods:** api
- **Steps:**
  1. With $TOKEN, POST bodies: '{}', '{"path":"bogus"}', 'not-json', and no body at all
- **Expected:** All → 400 {"error":"path must be 'subscribe', 'free', or 'shop'"} (req.json().catch(null) absorbs the parse failure).

### ONB-3 — 'free' grants the 300-credit signup bonus exactly once [P0]

- **Methods:** api
- **Steps:**
  1. Use a FRESH throwaway token (account that has never touched credits)
  2. POST {"path":"free"} → note balance
  3. POST {"path":"free"} again → compare
- **Expected:** First: {ok:true, path:'free', planActive:false, balance:300}. Second: identical balance 300 (ensureCreditAccount onConflictDoNothing — no double grant).

### ONB-4 — 'shop' creates no credit account [P0]

- **Methods:** api
- **Steps:**
  1. Use a second fresh throwaway token
  2. POST {"path":"shop"} → inspect response
  3. Then POST {"path":"free"} → inspect balance
- **Expected:** shop → {ok:true, path:'shop'} with NO balance/planActive fields (early return, no credit account). The follow-up free call then returns balance 300, proving the shop call created nothing.

### ONB-5 — 'subscribe' without an active paid plan grants no welcome credits [P1]

- **Methods:** api
- **Steps:**
  1. With a free-plan account's token (claudetest if its plan is 'free', else a throwaway): POST {"path":"subscribe"}
  2. Repeat once
- **Expected:** {ok:true, path:'subscribe', planActive:false, balance:<signup bonus only, e.g. 300>} both times — the 1000-credit WELCOME_CREDITS grant is withheld until getEntitlements shows an active/trialing paid plan (a bare CTA tap can't mint credits).

### ONB-6 — 'subscribe' with an active paid plan grants 1000 exactly once (refId guard) [P1]

- **Methods:** api, unit
- **⛔ Boundary:** If no paid plan is active, STOP — do not purchase a subscription to create the precondition (LIVE Stripe keys / real App Store billing). Instead verify the guard by code inspection plus a unit test of the refId-dedupe query, and mark the live-grant leg blocked-pending-a-safe-staging-plan.
- **Steps:**
  1. Check claudetest's plan first: curl GET http://localhost:8081/api/creator/subscription with $TOKEN
  2. ONLY IF a paid plan is already active/trialing: POST {"path":"subscribe"} twice and diff the balances
- **Expected:** Balance rises by exactly 1000 on the first call (ledger refId 'onboard_welcome:<id>'), unchanged on the second. planActive:true in both responses.

### ONB-7 — Client fires the subscribe POST on paywall close (end-to-end wiring) [P2]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui
- **Steps:**
  1. Run PIPE-1 while tailing the dev-server log
  2. Confirm the request body and auth header of the POST fired from studio.tsx line 240
- **Expected:** Exactly one POST /api/creator/onboarding {path:'subscribe'} with the session's Bearer token, fired only when the paywall was opened by the welcome intent (pendingSubscribeGrantRef) — closing a paywall opened any other way (e.g. Account → Subscription & billing) fires nothing.

# Area: Eve's conversational surfaces — Studio tab gate/entry, EveHome (guide + interview), keyboard chat, voice-intent router API, digest, Gemini Live session infra, vision bus

## Studio entry, gates, and surface machine (src/app/studio.tsx)

**Entry:** The Eve tab is the default tab: launch with `npm run ios` (dev server = npx expo start; API routes at http://localhost:8081). Key files: src/app/studio.tsx, src/lib/eve-bus.ts, src/components/screen-fade.tsx. Sign-in state drives three branches: signed-out gate, loading spinner, EveHome default.

### STUDIO-1 — Signed-out gate renders over dimmed Eve [P0]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui
- **Steps:**
  1. Fresh install (or Account tab -> sign out) so useAuth().session is null
  2. If the first-launch Welcome modal appears (fresh AsyncStorage), dismiss it via its close/onRequestClose path first
  3. Land on the Eve tab (/studio)
- **Expected:** Scrim (EVE_SCRIM) dims the background Eve; EveGlyph, 'FROM IDEA TO BRAND IN SECONDS', 'Meet Eve' title, body copy, gold 'Create an account' button, 'I already have one — log in' link, and 'Free to explore…' footer all render. No mic permission prompt fires while signed out.

### STUDIO-2 — Signed-out CTAs navigate to /account [P1]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui
- **Steps:**
  1. From the signed-out gate tap 'Create an account'
  2. Go back to the Eve tab, tap 'I already have one — log in'
- **Expected:** Both taps router.navigate to the Account tab. Pressed state scales the primary button (0.98) and dims glow.

### STUDIO-3 — First-launch Welcome modal shows once and persists dismissal [P1]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui
- **⛔ Boundary:** For the 'subscribe' branch, stop when the Paywall sheet renders. Do NOT proceed into any Stripe payment sheet or submit payment — .env.local carries LIVE Stripe keys. Verify only that the paywall opened with reason 'subscription_required' and that closing it fires the POST /api/creator/onboarding {path:'subscribe'} claim call (observe in dev-server logs).
- **Steps:**
  1. Delete the app from the simulator (clears AsyncStorage), reinstall, launch signed-out
  2. Verify the full-screen Welcome modal appears above the tab bar
  3. Choose 'shop' -> expect navigation to /market and modal gone
  4. Kill and relaunch the app
- **Expected:** Welcome shows only when nc_welcome_seen is unset AND signed out. After any choice (or onRequestClose) nc_welcome_seen='1' is written; relaunch never shows it again. 'login' routes to /account. 'subscribe' stores nc_onboard_intent='subscribe' and routes to /account; after sign-in the Paywall opens once (intentHandledRef) and the intent key is removed.

### STUDIO-4 — Signed-in loading spinner until /api/me resolves [P1]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui
- **Steps:**
  1. Sign in as claudetest@nanocrew.dev on the Account tab
  2. Switch to the Eve tab and watch the first render
- **Expected:** ActivityIndicator (color #cdd1d9) over the scrim while loading || !voiceResolved; then EveHome mounts. No flash of the signed-out gate.

### STUDIO-5 — /api/me network failure fails soft (no deck, EveHome still mounts) [P1]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui
- **Risk:** With the dev server down, the live-voice token mint also fails — expect the error banner from LIVE-4 as a side effect; that is correct behavior, not a defect.
- **Steps:**
  1. Sign in, then kill the dev server (Ctrl-C on npx expo start) — or toggle Mac Wi-Fi off — before opening the Eve tab
  2. Open the Eve tab
- **Expected:** The catch leaves hasStore=false; finally sets voiceResolved=true, so EveHome renders (no summon pill / BrandDeck). No crash, no red screen. EveHome's own /api/me also fails -> guidance falls back to venusGuide({stores:[]}) greeting.

### STUDIO-6 — Summon pill / top-edge swipe reveals BrandDeck and silences Eve [P0]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui
- **Risk:** The summon pan gesture requires activeOffsetY 12 — a slow drag may not trigger; use a deliberate flick. Pill only renders when hasStore=true.
- **Steps:**
  1. Signed in with >=1 store (claudetest should have one; if not, run BUILD-5 first)
  2. Verify the small pill (44x4) near the top of the Eve tab
  3. Tap the pill (hitSlop is generous) -> BrandDeck slides over Eve
  4. Close the deck; this time swipe DOWN from the top edge (translationY > 30 or fast flick)
- **Expected:** Both gestures set deckShown=true. While the deck is shown, EveHome receives open=false (open = focused && !deckShown) so the live session STOPS (watch Metro logs for the [live] teardown; her audio stops). Closing the deck restarts the session (state connecting -> listening).

  2. Complete a brand build (BUILD-5) so emitEveEvent({kind:'store-created'}) fires
  3. Return to the guide

- **Methods:** simulator-ui
- **Steps:**
  1. Signed in, session live (Eve greeted you)
  2. Switch to the Market tab; wait 2s; switch back
- **Expected:** focused=usePathname().startsWith('/studio') goes false -> open=false -> live.stop() (Metro shows [live] teardown; audio stops mid-sentence if she was speaking). Returning re-starts a FRESH session (she greets again — live.messages reset).

### STUDIO-12 — Design overlay keeps EveHome mounted (she keeps listening) [P0]

## Guide view — returning creator (src/components/eve/eve-home.tsx)

**Entry:** Signed in as claudetest with >=1 store, Eve tab focused, deck closed -> EveHome view='guide'. The guide is voice-first: subtitles top, one CTA bottom. Mic permission is requested once on summon (hasStore path).

### GUIDE-1 — Guide greeting renders from venusGuide before she speaks [P1]

  2. Before/while the session connects, read the center subtitle

- **Steps:**

  2. Reopen the Eve tab signed in with a store — the OS mic dialog appears (AudioModule.requestRecordingPermissionsAsync)
  3. Tap Don't Allow
  4. Tap 'View your digest'
- **Expected:** micOk=false -> the guide voice session NEVER starts (voiceView requires micOk===true) — no token mint in server logs, no error banner. The digest CTA still opens the modal. Re-entering the tab does not re-prompt (micOk latched non-null).

### GUIDE-3 — Mic granted -> guide session connects and Eve speaks the central greeting (audio OUT works in the sim) [P0]

- **Risk:** Persona is eveCentralInstruction(name, storeNames) — she should NOT run the first-time self-introduction. Each connect consumes a paid Gemini Live session (comp account, not billed but real usage).
- **Steps:**

  2. Wait through state connecting -> listening
  3. Listen and watch the subtitle
- **Expected:** POST /api/voice-live-token hits the dev server; ws opens; EVE_CENTRAL_GREETING nudge makes her speak a one-sentence greeting ALOUD (Gemini audio plays through the simulator/Mac speakers — no mic input needed since the greeting is text-nudged). Subtitle mirrors her words; eve-stage bus flips the background avatar to 'talking' while speaking.

### GUIDE-4 — 'View your digest' opens the modal AND briefs the live session [P0]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui, manual-device-only
- **Risk:** sendContext is a no-op if the session isn't connected — the modal must still work with mic denied (GUIDE-2).

  2. Watch the modal: loading spinner then headline + 3 tiles (Orders / Revenue / Views · 30d) + suggestion
  3. On a real device, then ask aloud 'how is <brand> doing?'
- **Expected:** openDigest fetches /api/creator/stats once, renders buildDigest; live.sendContext(digestBriefing(rows)) pushes the real per-brand figures silently (turnComplete:false — she does not reply unprompted). On device her follow-up answer quotes the actual numbers and declines anything time-sliced ('LIMITS' clause).

- **Risk:** Unit-testable by extracting the buildReady predicate (view gate + regex + turn floor) into a pure function; today it is inline in eve-home.tsx.

  2. Watch for any '✓ Build my brand' pill
- **Expected:** buildReady effect early-returns unless view==='interview' — no Build pill ever appears in the guide regardless of turn count or cue-like phrases.

- **Methods:** simulator-ui
- **Risk:** Deleting brands mutates the production DB. Prefer creating a throwaway state or accept and re-create via BUILD-5.

### GUIDE-7 — Backgrounding the app stops the session; foreground restarts [P1]

  2. Wait 3s; reopen the app
- **Expected:** AppState listener sets appActive=false -> live.stop() (audio halts). Foreground -> appActive=true -> live.start() fresh (she greets again; messages reset).

## Brand interview — voice UI (eve-home.tsx interview view)

**Entry:** From the first-brand guide, tap '🎙 Build your brand' (startVoice). Interview shows InterviewTopics checklist, hint line, Pause pill, subtitles, header icons (‹ tools · pause · ⌨).

### INT-1 — Mic denied at interview entry auto-falls back to keyboard mode [P0]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui
- **Steps:**
  1. Reset mic permission (xcrun simctl privacy booted reset microphone <bundle-id>)
  2. No-store account state; tap '🎙 Build your brand'
  3. Deny the OS dialog
- **Expected:** keyboardMode=true is set BEFORE entering the interview; the full-screen ChatInterview appears; error banner reads 'No microphone access — you can type your answers, or enable the mic in Settings.' The session still starts (keyboardMode bypasses the pause gate) so typed turns get replies.

### INT-2 — Interview with mic granted: session, hint states, topics checklist [P0]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui
- **Risk:** In the sim the mic streams silence — she will greet then sit in 'listening'. Use topic taps or ⌨ mode to drive turns.
- **Steps:**
  1. Grant mic; tap '🎙 Build your brand'
  2. Watch the hint line cycle: '[ connecting… ]' -> '[ listening — just talk ]' -> when she speaks '[ Eve is speaking — tap to pause ]'
  3. Verify the InterviewTopics strip renders (name, products, style, colors, logo, vibe)
- **Expected:** First-brand persona (liveSystemInstruction, firstTime=true) — she introduces herself in one sentence then greets. Topics check off as the transcript covers them (tap a topic -> onAsk sends that question via live.sendText and she answers it aloud).

### INT-3 — Pause / resume gates the voice session [P1]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui
- **Risk:** Known design quirk to confirm, not fix: pausing tears down the whole Live session, so resume loses conversational context (live.messages reset by start()). Verify buildReady also resets visually if messages clear.
- **Steps:**
  1. Mid-interview (voice mode), tap the '❚❚ Pause' pill while she is speaking
  2. Verify audio stops and the pill turns '▶ Resume' (accent color), hint '[ paused — tap to resume ]'
  3. Tap Resume
- **Expected:** Pause: player.pause(), state->idle, and the session-gate effect calls live.stop() (paused && !keyboardMode). Resume: live.start() reconnects (fresh session — she greets again). Header pause icon (❚❚/▶) mirrors the pill.

### INT-4 — Pause and keyboard icons hidden appropriately [P2]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui
- **Steps:**
  1. In voice mode verify header shows ‹ tools, ❚❚, ⌨
  2. Toggle ⌨
- **Expected:** In keyboardMode the header pause and ⌨ icons disappear (the chat surface covers the screen; pause is voice-only). ‹ tools remains reachable via the chat's own ‹ Back.

### INT-5 — ‹ tools backs out to the guide and resets interview state [P1]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui
- **Steps:**
  1. Enter the interview, exchange >=1 turn (typed is fine)
  2. Tap '‹ tools'
- **Expected:** resetToGuide: view='guide', heard/line cleared, keyboardMode off, brand null. For a no-store creator the guide session does NOT start (hasStore false -> voiceView false) — no orphaned audio. Re-entering starts a fresh interview.

### INT-6 — buildReady latch: cue phrase after >=3 user turns unlocks '✓ Build my brand' [P0]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui, unit
- **Risk:** Regex: /\b(ready to build|ready to (create|launch|go)|build your (brand|store|site|shop)|(everything|all)\s+(i|we)\s+need|got everything|let'?s build|time to build|shall we build)\b/i — unit-test the copy against sample lines ('are you ready to build your brand?' yes; 'we will build trust' — check 'build your brand' absent -> no).
- **Steps:**
  1. In ⌨ mode answer her questions with 3+ substantive typed turns (brand name, products, style)
  2. Keep going until she says a wrap-up line containing e.g. 'ready to build your brand' / 'got everything' / "let's build"
  3. Watch the header/orb area
- **Expected:** The moment her latest assistant message matches the cue regex AND userTurns>=3, the gold '✓ Build' button appears (chat header) / '✓ Build my brand' pill (voice view). It stays latched (buildReady early-return) even if she keeps talking.

### INT-7 — buildReady safety net at 6 user turns without a cue [P1]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui, unit
- **Steps:**
  1. Fresh interview in ⌨ mode; send 6 short evasive answers ('hm', 'not sure', …) regardless of her replies
- **Expected:** After the 6th committed user turn the Build button appears even though she never said the cue.

### INT-8 — Subtitles: 'you >' echo and her line, always on [P1]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui
- **Steps:**
  1. In voice view, drive a turn via a topic tap (InterviewTopics onAsk)
  2. Watch the captions block at the bottom
- **Expected:** heard shows 'you > <the sent text>' (max 2 lines); her streaming reply fills line (max 3 lines) and clears/replaces per turn.

### INT-9 — Error banner is dismissible [P1]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui
- **Steps:**
  1. Cause any error (e.g. kill the dev server then enter the interview -> token mint fails)
  2. Tap the red banner
- **Expected:** Banner shows the message + 'tap to dismiss'; tapping clears it. It sits above bottomPad and never blocks the Build pill permanently.

### INT-10 — Mic-busy modal (active phone call) with Try again [P2]

- **Methods:** manual-device-only
- **Risk:** Cannot be triggered in the simulator (no CallKit contention). isInsufficientPriority() itself is unit-testable with fake error shapes (numeric code, string code, '!pri' in message).
- **Steps:**
  1. On a REAL device: start a phone/FaceTime call, then open the interview
  2. Expect the 'MICROPHONE IN USE / You're on a call' modal
  3. End the call, tap 'Try again'
- **Expected:** AudioSessionBusyError (iOS 561017449) -> audioBusy modal, NOT the generic banner. 'Try again' clears paused state and live.start() reconnects successfully. 'Not now' just dismisses.

## Keyboard chat interview (src/components/chat-interview.tsx)

**Entry:** From the interview voice view tap ⌨ (or arrive via mic-denied fallback INT-1). Full-screen chat over the studio; her voice is muted; same Live session underneath (live.sendText).

### CHAT-1 — Chat surface renders with empty state then her greeting bubble [P1]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui
- **Steps:**
  1. Enter ⌨ mode at the start of a fresh interview before any turns commit
- **Expected:** 'Eve is connecting…' placeholder when messages empty and not streaming; header shows ‹ Back · Eve · 🎙 Voice; composer with 'Message Eve…' placeholder. Her greeting streams in as an assistant bubble (transcript still flows while muted).

### CHAT-2 — Send a typed turn: bubbles, thinking dots, streaming reply [P0]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui
- **Steps:**
  1. Type 'I want to start a streetwear brand called Ashfall' and tap send
  2. Watch the transcript area
- **Expected:** User bubble (accent, right) appears instantly (sendText pushes to transcript optimistically); state->thinking shows the '· · ·' bubble; her reply streams as a live bubble (showStreaming) then commits as a message without duplication (the last-message identity check). Auto-scroll keeps the newest visible. NO audio plays (muted).

### CHAT-3 — Send disabled on empty/whitespace and while thinking [P1]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui
- **Steps:**
  1. Tap send with an empty input; type spaces only and tap; send a real message then immediately try to send again while '· · ·' shows
- **Expected:** Send button at 0.4 opacity and inert in all three cases; input clears natively (inputRef.clear) only on an accepted send.

### CHAT-4 — Keyboard mode truly mutes: no mic pickup, no playback, immediate quiet on toggle [P1]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui, manual-device-only
- **Steps:**
  1. On a real device: while she is mid-sentence in voice mode, tap ⌨
- **Expected:** setMuted(true) clears queued buffers — her voice cuts off at once; speech-level envelope resets (avatar mouth closes); the mic callback drops frames (talking near the phone produces no user turns). In the sim, verify no audio plays for any chat reply.

### CHAT-5 — Entering keyboard mode clears pause so typed turns never hang [P0]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui
- **Risk:** Watch for a race: start() is async (token mint + ws). A message typed in the first ~2s may be dropped because session is null — sendText catches silently. Note actual behavior.
- **Steps:**
  1. In voice mode tap Pause (session stops)
  2. Tap ⌨
  3. Send a message
- **Expected:** toggleKeyboard resets paused=false; the session-gate effect ('keyboardMode || !paused') restarts the session; the typed turn gets a streamed reply rather than vanishing into a dead session.

### CHAT-6 — 🎙 Voice returns to the orb view and unmutes [P1]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui
- **Steps:**
  1. From the chat tap 🎙 Voice
- **Expected:** keyboardMode=false -> chat unmounts, voice layout returns, live.mute(false) — her next reply is audible. The Live session is NOT restarted (same transcript continues).

### CHAT-7 — ‹ Back exits the interview to the guide [P2]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui
- **Steps:**
  1. From the chat tap ‹ Back
- **Expected:** onExit=resetToGuide: chat closes, guide view, transcript refs cleared.

### CHAT-8 — ✓ Build replaces the Voice button once canBuild [P1]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui
- **Steps:**
  1. Reach buildReady in chat (INT-6/7)
  2. Verify the header right slot
- **Expected:** 🎙 Voice is replaced by the gold '✓ Build' button; while finalizing it shows a spinner at 0.5 opacity and is disabled.

### CHAT-9 — Composer sits above the keyboard and above the tab bar [P2]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui
- **Steps:**
  1. Focus the input (software keyboard: I/O -> Keyboard -> Toggle Software Keyboard if needed)
  2. Blur it; check both positions; type 6+ lines to grow the multiline input
- **Expected:** bottomInset switches between kb+8 and BottomTabInset+insets.bottom; input grows to maxHeight 120 then scrolls internally; focused border glows accentCool.

## Finalize -> BrandReview -> createStore (+ /api/extract-brand, /api/say)

**Entry:** Build pill in either interview mode -> live.finalize() -> POST /api/extract-brand -> BrandReview (src/components/brand-review.tsx) -> createStore POST /api/store -> launch fanfare via POST /api/say.

### BUILD-1 — Finalize happy path: brand extracted, session stopped, BrandReview shows [P0]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui, api
- **Risk:** Paid Gemini text call — comp account so not billed, but rate-limited 20/min (extract:<uid>).
- **Steps:**
  1. Complete an interview in ⌨ mode with clear essentials (name 'Ashfall', products 'hoodies and tees', style 'dark minimal streetwear', colors 'black and white')
  2. Tap ✓ Build; watch the spinner
- **Expected:** POST /api/extract-brand {messages} returns a BrandResult; the Live session stops (no more audio/turns); BrandReview renders the extracted name/products/palette for editing. The transcript is retained in messages.current for the store call.

### BUILD-2 — Finalize failure keeps the interview alive with an actionable error [P1]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui
- **Steps:**
  1. Reach buildReady, then kill the dev server
  2. Tap ✓ Build
- **Expected:** catch -> error 'Could not build the brand — try again.'; finalizing spinner clears; the session is NOT stopped (only success stops it); you can keep chatting and retry after restarting the server.

### BUILD-3 — /api/extract-brand direct API: 401 / 400 / 429 / happy [P1]

- **Methods:** api
- **Risk:** The 429 loop burns ~20 flash calls and pollutes the prod rate_limits row for the test user for up to a minute — run it last.
- **Steps:**
  1. Mint a token: source .env.local && curl -s "$EXPO_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL/auth/v1/token?grant_type=password" -H "apikey: $EXPO_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_PUBLISHABLE_KEY" -H 'Content-Type: application/json' -d "{\"email\":\"$TEST_ACCOUNT_EMAIL\",\"password\":\"$TEST_ACCOUNT_PASSWORD\"}" | jq -r .access_token
  2. No auth: curl -s -o /dev/null -w '%{http_code}' -X POST http://localhost:8081/api/extract-brand -H 'Content-Type: application/json' -d '{"messages":[]}' -> expect 401
  3. Authed empty body: {"messages":[]} -> expect 400 invalid body
  4. Authed happy: 6-turn fabricated transcript covering name/products/style -> expect {brand:{name,designStyle,products,...}}
  5. Loop 21 authed calls inside 60s -> expect a 429 with retryAfter + Retry-After header
- **Expected:** Statuses as listed; the 429 body is {error:'rate_limited',retryAfter:N}.

### BUILD-4 — createStore happy path: store created, fanfare speech, events [P0]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui
- **⛔ Boundary:** This WRITES to the production Supabase DB (a real store row) and provisions a Printful-backed shop for the test account. That is store/product provisioning, NOT an order — allowed — but STOP before anything that would place a Printful ORDER or open a Stripe payment sheet. Note the created slug so it can be cleaned up (delete via the brand console) after the run.
- **Risk:** The playSpeech watchdog (load poll 25x100ms, play nudges at i=4/9) exists because silent play() races on iOS — if no audio, check Metro for the watchdog dropping to idle rather than assuming the API failed.
- **Steps:**
  1. From BrandReview (BUILD-1), review/edit fields, tap the create CTA
  2. Wait for creation; listen
- **Expected:** POST /api/store {brand, transcript} succeeds; created slug set; logoUrl may render; emitEveEvent('store-created') -> Studio deck refetch (STUDIO-10); then POST /api/say returns base64 WAV and playSpeech plays '<Brand> is online. Head to the Design tab…' in her Kore voice (audible in the sim); state flips to 'speaking' during playback and the background avatar animates. onFinished returns to the guide with hasStore=true.

### BUILD-5 — createStore 402 -> paywall (subscription_required / brand_limit) [P1]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui, api
- **⛔ Boundary:** Verify the paywall RENDERS with the right reason and close it. Do NOT tap through to any Stripe checkout/payment sheet — live Stripe keys. API-level: assert only the 402 status + error body.
- **Steps:**
  1. Use an account whose plan disallows another brand (or claudetest at its brand limit)
  2. Complete BrandReview and tap create
- **Expected:** 402 body {error:'brand_limit'} -> Paywall opens with reason 'brand_limit'; any other 402 -> 'subscription_required'. creating spinner clears; BrandReview stays so nothing is lost.

### BUILD-6 — createStore network failure surfaces an error and preserves the review [P1]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui
- **Steps:**
  1. In BrandReview, kill the dev server, tap create
- **Expected:** error banner 'Failed to create store' (or the thrown message); creating=false; BrandReview still shows the editable brand for a retry.

### BUILD-7 — Fanfare guards: closed/paused surface never plays orphan audio [P2]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui
- **Steps:**
  1. Immediately after tapping create, background the app (Cmd-Shift-H) before the store call returns
  2. Foreground after ~10s
- **Expected:** playSpeech checks openRef/pausedRef before and after load — no audio plays into a hidden surface; state settles at idle; the store itself IS created (verify in the deck).

### BUILD-8 — /api/say direct: happy + auth [P2]

- **Methods:** api
- **Steps:**
  1. Authed: curl -s -X POST http://localhost:8081/api/say -H "Authorization: Bearer $TOKEN" -H 'Content-Type: application/json' -d '{"text":"Test line."}' | jq '.audio | length'
  2. Unauthed same call -> 401
- **Expected:** Happy returns {audio:<base64 WAV>} with nonzero length (44-byte header + PCM); unauthed 401.

## Voice-intent router — /api/eve/route API battery (src/app/api/eve/route+api.ts)

**Entry:** POST http://localhost:8081/api/eve/route with Bearer <claudetest token> (mint per BUILD-3 step 1). Body: {turn, recent:[{role,text}], stores:[{name,slug,hasSite}], interviewActive}. Standard fixture: STORES=[{"name":"Urban Threads","slug":"urban-threads","hasSite":true},{"name":"Ashfall","slug":"ashfall","hasSite":true}]. The endpoint is PRECISION-over-recall: every failure path must return {intent:'none'}, never a 5xx to the client flow.

### ROUTE-1 — 401 without a bearer token [P0]

- **Methods:** api
- **Steps:**
  1. curl -s -o /dev/null -w '%{http_code}' -X POST http://localhost:8081/api/eve/route -H 'Content-Type: application/json' -d '{"turn":"hi"}'
- **Expected:** 401 {error:'unauthorized'}.

### ROUTE-2 — 400 on missing/empty turn [P1]

- **Methods:** api
- **Steps:**
  1. Authed POST with bodies: {} ; {"turn":""} ; {"turn":"   "}
- **Expected:** All three -> 400 {error:'invalid body'} (turn trims to empty). Malformed JSON body also -> 400.

### ROUTE-3 — 429 after 60 calls in a minute (eve-route:<uid>, 60/60s) [P1]

- **Methods:** api
- **Risk:** Burns ~60 flash calls + writes the prod rate_limits row; blocks further routing for the test user up to 60s. Run in isolation. Fixed-window is 'soft' — a couple extra 200s under burst is acceptable per design.
- **Steps:**
  1. Loop: for i in $(seq 1 62); do curl -s -o /dev/null -w '%{http_code}\n' -X POST http://localhost:8081/api/eve/route -H "Authorization: Bearer $TOKEN" -H 'Content-Type: application/json' -d '{"turn":"hello"}'; done
- **Expected:** First ~60 return 200; then 429 {error:'rate_limited',retryAfter:N} with Retry-After header. Window resets after 60s.

### ROUTE-4 — create-brand positives [P0]

- **Methods:** api
- **Steps:**
  1. Authed POSTs with stores=STORES, interviewActive=false, turns:
  2. "let's build another brand"
  3. "I want to start a new label"
  4. "can we create a second brand together, something for gym wear"
- **Expected:** Each returns {intent:'create-brand'} (no extra fields).

### ROUTE-5 — edit-site positives incl. named store -> storeSlug [P0]

- **Methods:** api
- **Steps:**
  1. Same fixture, turns:
  2. "I want to edit my site" (two candidates -> see ROUTE-6)
  3. "let's change the homepage on Urban Threads"
  4. "can we update the hero on the Ashfall store"
- **Expected:** Named turns return {intent:'edit-site',storeSlug:'urban-threads'} / {...,'ashfall'} — slug must be from the provided list, matched from the spoken NAME.

### ROUTE-6 — edit-site ambiguous-store -> ask, no slug [P0]

- **Methods:** api
- **Steps:**
  1. stores=STORES (both with sites), turn "I want to edit my website"
- **Expected:** {intent:'edit-site'} with NO storeSlug and ask set to a one-line question naming Urban Threads and Ashfall (<=200 chars).

### ROUTE-7 — Slug-invention guard: model may not target stores the caller doesn't own [P0]

- **Methods:** api
- **Steps:**
  1. stores=STORES, turn "edit the Nike site for me"
  2. Also: stores=[] , turn "I want to edit my site"
- **Expected:** Any storeSlug not in the caller's list is stripped server-side (stores.some check) — response has intent edit-site (or none) but NEVER a foreign/invented slug. With stores=[] there is nothing to match; storeSlug absent.

### ROUTE-8 — new-design positives incl. the meme branch idea capture [P0]

- **Methods:** api
- **Steps:**
  1. Turns:
  2. "make me a meme about mondays"
  3. "new tee design with a chrome skull"
  4. "design me a graphic of a koi fish in neon"
- **Expected:** Each {intent:'new-design', idea:'<short concept>'} — idea present and <=300 chars (e.g. contains 'monday', 'chrome skull'). The meme/plain split is CLIENT-side (/\bmeme\b/i on the raw turn), so the API contract is identical for both.

### ROUTE-9 — write-post positive -> topic [P1]

- **Methods:** api
- **Steps:**
  1. Turn "write a blog post about our summer drop"
- **Expected:** {intent:'write-post', topic:'<short topic>'} (topic <=200 chars).

### ROUTE-10 — digest positives incl. contextual bare 'yes' [P0]

- **Methods:** api
- **Steps:**
  1. Turns (no recent): "how am I doing" ; "any sales?" ; "show my numbers" ; "how's Urban Threads performing this month"
  2. Contextual: recent=[{"role":"assistant","text":"Would you like your digest?"}], turn "yes"
- **Expected:** All -> {intent:'digest'}. The contextual bare 'yes' resolves via recent turns.

### ROUTE-11 — done positives [P1]

- **Methods:** api
- **Steps:**
  1. Turns: "that's all for now" ; "we're done, goodbye Eve" ; "thanks Eve, talk later"
- **Expected:** Each {intent:'done'}.

### ROUTE-12 — Near-miss battery — every one MUST return none (precision guard) [P0]

- **Methods:** api
- **Risk:** LLM classification at temperature 0 is stable but not deterministic across model updates — run the battery 2x; flag flaky items.
- **Steps:**
  1. interviewActive=false, stores=STORES; assert intent==='none' for EVERY turn:
  2. "my brand is called Ashfall" (interview content, not create-brand)
  3. "I love building brands" (chit-chat)
  4. "maybe someday I'll do another brand" (vague wish)
  5. "my website should feel airy and minimal" (style talk, not edit-site)
  6. "I saw a really cool website yesterday"
  7. "I like bold designs" (not new-design)
  8. "the logo design should be simple"
  9. "how are you doing today, Eve?" (about HER, not the store — not digest)
  10. "I should really post more on social" (not write-post)
  11. "I'm done with the logo talk — what's next?" (topic pivot, not done)
  12. "sales matter a lot to me long-term" (abstract, not digest)
- **Expected:** Eleven consecutive {intent:'none'} responses. Any hit here is a false positive that would yank a creator out of conversation — file each as a P0 bug with the exact turn.

### ROUTE-13 — interviewActive strictness: interview content stays none; explicit redirects still route [P0]

- **Methods:** api
- **Steps:**
  1. interviewActive=true, stores=STORES; assert none for: "call it Ashfall" ; "hoodies first, then tees" ; "black and white palette" ; "the site should have a dark hero" ; "let's make the vibe really street"
  2. Then assert routing still fires for the explicit redirect: "actually forget this, I want to edit my site instead" -> edit-site
- **Expected:** All interview-content turns -> none; the explicit redirect -> edit-site (with ask, both stores have sites).

### ROUTE-14 — Fail-open paths return {intent:'none'}, never a 5xx [P2]

- **Methods:** api, unit
- **Risk:** Env edit affects every other Gemini feature while unset — do this in a quiet window and restore immediately.
- **Steps:**
  1. Temporarily unset GOOGLE_GENAI_API_KEY and GEMINI_API_KEY in .env.local, restart the dev server, send a clear create-brand turn
  2. Restore the keys after
- **Expected:** 200 {intent:'none'} (missing key short-circuit). Same contract if the model call throws non-transiently (catch -> NONE). The client (routeTurn) treats it as no-op.

### ROUTE-15 — Input clamps: 600-char turn, 6 recent, 12 stores, 300-char texts [P2]

- **Methods:** api
- **Steps:**
  1. Send a 5,000-char turn -> expect 200 (clamped to 600, likely none)
  2. Send 50 recent messages and 30 stores -> expect 200, no error
  3. Send stores with 500-char names -> expect 200 (names clamped to 80)
- **Expected:** All clamped server-side; никогда a 400/500 for oversize; response shape unchanged.

## Intent routing — client wiring in EveHome (routeTurn / surface transitions)

**Entry:** eve-home.tsx routeTurn effect: fires per committed user turn from live.messages, ONLY when hasStore && !brand. In the simulator, turns can be committed by TYPING (interview ⌨ mode) or topic taps — but a RETURNING creator can only enter the interview by voice ('create-brand' routed), so several guide-level wirings are device-only. Files: eve-home.tsx, eve-bus.ts, design-bus.ts.

### WIRE-1 — First-brand creator NEVER routes (gate: hasStore) [P0]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui
- **Steps:**
  1. No-store account state; enter the interview in ⌨ mode
  2. Type clear commands: 'show my numbers', 'edit my site', 'that's all, goodbye'
  3. Watch the dev-server logs for POST /api/eve/route
- **Expected:** Zero /api/eve/route calls (the effect marks turns consumed without routing). She answers conversationally; no surface changes, no digest modal.

### WIRE-2 — Returning creator, typed mid-interview redirect switches to edit-site [P1]

- **Methods:** manual-device-only, api
- **Risk:** TESTABILITY GAP worth flagging to Joe: for hasStore creators the interview is voice-entry-only (guide CTA becomes 'View your digest'), so this wiring cannot be reached simulator-only without a code shim. The API half is covered by ROUTE-13.
- **Steps:**
  1. Requires a returning creator INSIDE the interview: on device say "let's build another brand" from the guide (or temporarily add a dev button calling enterInterview)
  2. In ⌨ mode type 'actually forget this, I want to edit my site instead'
- **Expected:** Route call carries interviewActive:true; on an edit-site hit with exactly one site-bearing store -> onGo({state:'developing',payload:{slug,url,name}}) — EveDeveloping replaces the surface. With two -> she asks which (sendContext).

### WIRE-3 — edit-site auto-target when exactly one store has a live site [P1]

- **Methods:** manual-device-only, unit
- **Risk:** siteUrlFor is a pure exported-in-file function — extractable for a tsx unit test (github.com placeholder must yield null).
- **Steps:**
  1. On device (guide, voice): account with one site-bearing store; say 'I want to edit my website'
- **Expected:** No slug needed — the single withSites store is targeted; surface transitions to EveDeveloping with its url/slug. siteUrlFor prefers customDomain, else a non-github deploymentUrl; never fabricated.

### WIRE-4 — edit-site on a named brand WITHOUT a live site -> gentle spoken decline [P1]

- **Methods:** manual-device-only
- **Steps:**
  1. On device: name a real brand that has no deployment ('let's edit the <name> site')
- **Expected:** No surface change; live.sendContext tells her that brand has no live site — she says so about THAT brand and suggests launching from the Studio (never 'did you mean <other>').

### WIRE-5 — edit-site with NO site-bearing stores -> spoken suggestion to launch first [P2]

- **Methods:** manual-device-only
- **Steps:**
  1. On device: account whose stores all lack sites; say 'edit my website'
- **Expected:** sendContext('none of your brands has a live site yet…'); she answers gently; no navigation.

### WIRE-6 — new-design (non-meme) opens Eve's design overlay with the idea [P0]

- **Methods:** manual-device-only
- **Risk:** Design generation is a paid AI call (credit-gated; comp account exempt from billing). Confirm the credit balance is untouched afterwards (Account tab) — that IS the comp-gating test.
- **Steps:**
  1. On device (guide): say 'make me a new tee design with a chrome skull'
- **Expected:** onGo({state:'design',payload:{idea}}) — the translucent EveDesign popup spawns OVER EveHome (STUDIO-12); she stays live and comments; the idea string seeds the generation.

### WIRE-7 — new-design meme branch leaves Eve and opens /design with the design-bus command [P1]

- **Methods:** manual-device-only
- **Steps:**
  1. On device: say 'make me a meme about mondays'
- **Expected:** Because /\bmeme\b/i matches the RAW turn: onRequestClose() fires, sendDesignCommand({kind:'open-generate',prompt:idea,meme:true}) queues, router.push('/design') — the Design tab opens in meme mode with the prompt prefilled. Eve's session stops (tab blur).

### WIRE-8 — digest intent opens the modal AND briefs her with real figures [P1]

- **Methods:** manual-device-only
- **Steps:**
  1. On device (guide): say 'how am I doing?'
- **Expected:** Digest modal opens (loading -> tiles); await openDigest() resolves rows THEN sendContext(digestBriefing(rows)) — she speaks the headline from the actual numbers and can answer 'how's <brand> doing?' per-brand. Ask 'how much did I make last week?' -> she declines (LIMITS clause) instead of inventing.

### WIRE-9 — write-post stub: spoken redirect to the Studio composer [P2]

- **Methods:** manual-device-only
- **Steps:**
  1. On device: say 'write a blog post about our launch'
- **Expected:** No navigation; sendContext makes her say in one sentence that voice post-writing lands soon and posts live in the Studio composer for now.

### WIRE-10 — done intent closes/resets Eve's surface [P2]

- **Methods:** manual-device-only
- **Steps:**
  1. On device: say 'that's all for now, goodbye Eve'
- **Expected:** onRequestClose -> setEve(null): for the default surface this is a state reset (EveHome remains the tab default); no crash, no dangling audio.

### WIRE-11 — Late route resolution after leaving the surface is dropped (routingAlive) [P2]

- **Methods:** unit
- **Steps:**
  1. Code-level: the route fetch retries transient failures 2x2 with backoff (seconds) inside /api; client-side the response can land after EveHome unmounted (home->developing swap)
  2. Unit: extract routeTurn's post-await guard or simulate by resolving a mocked fetch after routingAlive.current=false
- **Expected:** No onGo/onRequestClose/router.push fires after unmount — the 'if (!routingAlive.current) return' guard holds. No 'update on unmounted component' warnings.

### WIRE-12 — Routing failure is silent (dev server down mid-conversation) [P1]

- **Methods:** manual-device-only
- **Steps:**
  1. On device with the session live, kill the dev server (the Gemini socket is DIRECT to Google so the conversation survives)
  2. Say a clear command ('show my numbers')
- **Expected:** routeTurn's catch swallows the network error — the conversation continues; the command simply doesn't execute (costs a repeat, never an error banner).

### WIRE-13 — No burst-routing of the finished interview when hasStore flips true [P1]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui
- **Steps:**
  1. Complete a FIRST brand end-to-end (interview -> build -> created)
  2. Watch dev-server logs during/after createStore
- **Expected:** Because routedUsers is marked consumed while !hasStore/brand, the flip to hasStore=true does NOT replay old interview turns through /api/eve/route — in particular a closing 'that's everything' never classifies 'done' and never closes the surface.

## Digest engine (src/lib/eve-digest.ts + modal)

**Entry:** Pure functions buildDigest/digestBriefing consumed by EveHome's digest modal and briefing (GUIDE-4 / WIRE-8). Data from GET /api/creator/stats. Unit tests runnable as a tsx script matching the existing pattern (package.json test:lipsync).

### DIG-1 — buildDigest headline branches (unit) [P1]

- **Methods:** unit
- **Steps:**
  1. Write a tsx test invoking buildDigest with: [] ; [{orders:0,revenueCents:0,views30d:0,status:'draft',...}] ; views-only ([{...views30d:42}]) ; orders ([{orders:3,revenueCents:8700}]) ; singulars (1 order, 1 view)
- **Expected:** 'You're just getting started.' for []; 'Your store is set up — time to get eyes on it.' for zero-everything; '42 views in 30 days — no orders yet.'; '3 orders · $87 all-time.'; singular forms '1 order' / '1 view'.

### DIG-2 — money() formatting (unit) [P2]

- **Methods:** unit
- **Steps:**
  1. Assert money(0)='$0', money(150)='$1.50', money(8700)='$87', money(123456)='$1,235' (>=1000 rounds), money(99999)='$999.99'
- **Expected:** Whole dollars drop cents; >= $1000 rounds with thousands separators; fractional cents keep 2dp.

### DIG-3 — Suggestion ladder (unit) [P1]

- **Methods:** unit
- **Steps:**
  1. Cases: no stores; stores but none 'live'; live + 0 views; live + views + 0 orders; live + orders
- **Expected:** In order: 'Let's build your first brand…' / 'Get a brand live to start selling…' / 'No views yet…' / '<n> views, no sales yet…' / 'Momentum's good…'.

### DIG-4 — digestBriefing content contract (unit) [P1]

- **Methods:** unit
- **Steps:**
  1. Assert the empty-store string mentions no brands + invent nothing
  2. For 2 stores assert: TOTALS line with brand count/orders/revenue/views; BY BRAND per-store line with status; the LIMITS clause naming all-time vs 30-day and forbidding estimates
- **Expected:** All three sections present and correctly pluralized; per-brand values match inputs exactly (money() formatted).

### DIG-5 — Digest modal lifecycle: open, cache, close paths [P1]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui
- **Steps:**
  1. In the guide tap 'View your digest' -> spinner -> content
  2. Close via the Close pill; reopen immediately
  3. Close via tapping the dark backdrop; verify tapping INSIDE the card does not close it
- **Expected:** First open fetches /api/creator/stats (one request in server logs); reopen renders instantly from digestStores cache (NO second fetch); backdrop closes, card tap doesn't (nested Pressable eats it).

### DIG-6 — /api/creator/stats failure -> graceful empty digest [P1]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui
- **Risk:** Slight lie risk: a creator WITH stores sees the empty-state headline when the fetch fails — verify wording acceptability with Joe; it currently reads as if they have no brands.
- **Steps:**
  1. Kill the dev server; tap 'View your digest'
- **Expected:** catch -> buildDigest([]) renders 'You're just getting started.' with $0/0/0 tiles rather than a stuck spinner; briefing (if session live) is the honest empty-state string.

## Gemini Live session infrastructure (src/lib/live-voice.ts, src/hooks/use-live-voice.ts, /api/voice-live-token)

**Entry:** LiveVoiceSession: token mint -> iOS audio session -> AudioContext graph -> ws to Gemini Live (direct, ephemeral token). States: connecting/listening/speaking/thinking/idle/error. Simulator: audio OUT and all state transitions observable; mic IN is silence.

### LIVE-1 — /api/voice-live-token happy path [P0]

- **Methods:** api
- **Steps:**
  1. curl -s -X POST http://localhost:8081/api/voice-live-token -H "Authorization: Bearer $TOKEN" | jq
- **Expected:** {token:'auth_tokens/...', model:'gemini-2.5-flash-native-audio-preview-12-2025'}. Token allows 2 uses, expires 30 min, session must start within 2 min.

### LIVE-2 — Token endpoint 401 / 429 / 502 [P1]

- **Methods:** api
- **Risk:** The 429 run temporarily locks the TEST USER out of starting voice sessions (~60s) — sequence after UI voice tests. Env swap breaks all Gemini features while active.
- **Steps:**
  1. No auth -> expect 401
  2. 21 authed calls in 60s -> expect 429 (live:<uid>, 20/min) with Retry-After
  3. 502 path: temporarily set an invalid GOOGLE_GENAI_API_KEY, restart server, call once, restore
- **Expected:** 401 {error:'unauthorized'}; 429 {error:'rate_limited',retryAfter}; 502 {error:<mint failure msg>}.

### LIVE-3 — Token failure surfaces as a UI error state, not a hang [P1]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui
- **Steps:**
  1. Kill the dev server; open a surface that starts a session (guide with mic granted, or interview)
- **Expected:** start() throws (fetch fails) -> hook catch -> error banner (message from the throw), state 'error'. hint shows connecting only briefly; the 15s watchdog is NOT needed (failure is immediate).

### LIVE-4 — 15s connect watchdog fails to a retryable error [P1]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui
- **Risk:** Timing-fiddly; /etc/hosts edit needs sudo and MUST be reverted.
- **Steps:**
  1. Simulate a mint that succeeds but a ws that never opens: turn Mac Wi-Fi OFF immediately after the token log line appears ([live] token status=200), or blackhole generativelanguage.googleapis.com in /etc/hosts
  2. Wait 15s
- **Expected:** "Eve couldn't connect — tap to try again." banner; state error; session fully stopped (audio session released). Restoring network + re-entering the surface reconnects cleanly.

### LIVE-5 — State machine transitions on a healthy session [P0]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui
- **Steps:**
  1. Open the guide (mic granted); watch the hint/subtitle and Metro logs ([live] ws open, setupComplete -> greeting)
  2. Observe: connecting -> (setup) -> her greeting audio -> speaking -> listening
- **Expected:** onState sequence matches; entity state maps thinking/listening/speaking; when she finishes (turnComplete) state returns to listening; eve-stage bus mirrors talking/silence.

### LIVE-6 — sendText round-trip: optimistic commit + streamed audio reply [P0]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui
- **Steps:**
  1. Interview ⌨ mode: send 'my brand is called Ashfall'
  2. Watch live.messages and streaming state
- **Expected:** User turn pushed to transcript immediately; state 'thinking'; outputTranscription streams venusText; on turnComplete the assistant turn commits. In VOICE mode the same reply is audible; in keyboard mode silent.

### LIVE-7 — sendContext is invisible: no reply, no transcript churn [P1]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui, manual-device-only
- **Steps:**
  1. With the guide session live, tap 'View your digest' (fires sendContext) and observe: no new bubble, she does NOT start talking
  2. Then (device) ask a follow-up and confirm the context informed her answer
- **Expected:** turnComplete:false — nothing visible happens until the next committed user turn, which is answered WITH the injected figures. No state flips to thinking on the send itself.

### LIVE-8 — sendImage: she sees a settled design (vision path end-to-end) [P1]

- **Methods:** manual-device-only, simulator-ui
- **Risk:** ~1.3k input tokens per image — settled images only; verify it is NOT re-sent per edit/frame (single listener call per settle).
- **Steps:**
  1. Trigger the design overlay with an idea (device voice, or API-drive the generation then use the overlay)
  2. When the artwork settles, watch Metro for the vision listener firing and (device) listen for her unprompted-by-you comment when next prompted
- **Expected:** eve-vision-bus publishes {url,note}; EveHome fetches the image (Cloudinary shrunk to w_512 jpg), base64s it, live.sendImage(...) with turnComplete:false; no crash on large images (chunked base64). Her next reply references what the design looks like.

### LIVE-9 — Mute flushes queued audio instantly [P1]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui, manual-device-only
- **Steps:**
  1. On device (or sim during her long greeting): while she is mid-sentence, tap ⌨
- **Expected:** setMuted(true): clearBuffers, playEndsAt=0, speech level reset — silence within ~100ms; transcript continues streaming.

### LIVE-10 — Single-session guard: two starts can never talk at once [P0]

- **Methods:** unit, simulator-ui
- **Risk:** This code has a documented history of doubled sessions after fast unmount/remount — the deck-toggle stress is the regression probe.
- **Steps:**
  1. Unit: instantiate two LiveVoiceSession objects (mock fetch/AudioManager), start A, then start B; assert A.closed and activeLiveSession===B
  2. In-app corollary: rapid-toggle the brand deck open/closed 5x while she connects; listen for doubled voices
- **Expected:** start() stops any OTHER active session first; every await in start() re-checks this.closed (token mint, audio activate, ws connect) so a stale start aborts without grabbing the mic. In-app: only one voice ever.

### LIVE-11 — Barge-in interruption flushes her audio [P1]

- **Methods:** manual-device-only
- **Steps:**
  1. REAL DEVICE: while she is mid-sentence, start talking over her
- **Expected:** serverContent.interrupted -> queue cleared, mic reopens (playEndsAt=0), state listening; her mouth (avatar) closes immediately; no self-interruption loop.

### LIVE-12 — Half-duplex echo gate: she never hears herself [P1]

- **Methods:** manual-device-only
- **Steps:**
  1. REAL DEVICE at full speaker volume: let her speak long replies; stay silent
  2. Watch the transcript for phantom 'user' turns echoing her words
- **Expected:** Mic frames are dropped until playEndsAt+250ms; voiceChat mode gives AEC. No phantom user turns; she never responds to her own voice.

### LIVE-13 — WS close mid-session degrades to idle, not error spam [P1]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui
- **Steps:**
  1. With a session live, toggle Mac Wi-Fi off for 10s, back on
- **Expected:** onclose (not closed by us) -> state idle. The gating effect may restart when conditions still hold — verify a clean reconnect or an honest idle, never a crash loop.

### LIVE-14 — Unmount teardown releases the iOS audio session [P1]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui
- **Steps:**
  1. Cycle: guide (session live) -> Market tab -> guide, 5 times
  2. Watch Metro for resume() hangs or 'audio session activate attempt' retries growing
- **Expected:** stop() sets AudioSessionActivity(false) each teardown; every reconnect activates first-try (retry log absent or rare); no accumulating leak that wedges resume().

## Eve's eyes — vision bus (src/lib/eve-vision-bus.ts)

**Entry:** Module-level one-way bus: EveDesign (overlay) publishes showEve({url,note}); EveHome subscribes and forwards via live.sendImage. Deliberately unqueued — sights are DROPPED when she isn't live. Pure helpers small()/toBase64()/imageForEve() are unit-testable via a tsx script.

### VIS-1 — showEve with no listener is a silent no-op (drop-by-design) [P1]

- **Methods:** unit
- **Steps:**
  1. Unit: call showEve({url:'https://x/y.png'}) with no registered listener; assert no throw
  2. Register a listener, unregister it, call again — assert the listener is not invoked
- **Expected:** No queueing, no error; unregister only clears when the stored fn matches (a second register isn't clobbered by the first's unsubscribe).

### VIS-2 — small() applies the Cloudinary shrink transform only where valid [P2]

- **Methods:** unit
- **Steps:**
  1. Unit asserts: 'https://res.cloudinary.com/d/image/upload/v1/a.png' -> contains '/upload/c_limit,w_512,q_auto,f_jpg/'
  2. Non-Cloudinary URL unchanged; Cloudinary URL WITHOUT '/upload/' unchanged; data: URI unchanged
- **Expected:** Exactly one replacement on the first '/upload/'; all other inputs pass through byte-identical.

### VIS-3 — imageForEve: data URI short-circuit and fetch path [P1]

- **Methods:** unit
- **Steps:**
  1. Unit: imageForEve('data:image/png;base64,AAAA') -> {mimeType:'image/png',base64:'AAAA'} with NO network call
  2. Mock fetch 200 with bytes + content-type 'image/jpeg; charset=x' -> mimeType 'image/jpeg', base64 round-trips
  3. Mock 404 -> null; empty body -> null; fetch throw -> null; missing content-type -> 'image/jpeg' default
- **Expected:** All failure shapes return null (never throw) — a failed sight must never break the conversation.

### VIS-4 — toBase64 chunking survives large images [P2]

- **Methods:** unit
- **Steps:**
  1. Unit: encode a 1MB Uint8Array (past the 0x8000 chunk size many times); compare against Buffer.from(bytes).toString('base64')
- **Expected:** Identical output; no 'Maximum call stack size exceeded' (the apply-arg-limit bug this chunking exists for).

### VIS-5 — End-to-end: a settled design reaches the live session; a dropped one doesn't [P1]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui, manual-device-only
- **Risk:** Design generation is a paid, credit-gated call — comp account keeps it unbilled; confirm credits unchanged afterwards.
- **Steps:**
  1. With the session LIVE, get a design to settle in the EveDesign overlay (device voice 'make me a tee design…', or drive EveDesign directly)
  2. Verify in Metro that sendImage fired (add a temporary log or watch for the sendClientContent image turn)
  3. Repeat with the session STOPPED (pause / deck open): settle a design
- **Expected:** Live: one sendImage per settle with the note steering her read. Not live: registerEveVisionListener's callback still runs in EveHome but live.sendImage no-ops on a null session — dropped silently, and she does NOT react to it minutes later when the session restarts (no queue).

# Area: Design tab (src/app/design.tsx) — canvas, all panels, generation/edit/merge/composite/mockup/tryon/video endpoints, designs & compositions CRUD, blanks catalogue, site-assets mode, credit/rate-limit gating, and the design command bus (Eve/deep links)

## Entry, auth gate, and brand/collection setup

**Entry:** Tab bar > Design (route /design, src/app/design.tsx). First mount loads /api/blanks and /api/me, then opens the full-screen setup modal (brand step -> collection step). Sign in as claudetest@nanocrew.dev. NOTE: app talks to https://api.nanocrew.app (EXPO_PUBLIC_API_URL in .env.local) backed by the PRODUCTION Supabase DB.

### DES-1 — Signed-out gate [P0]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui
- **Steps:**
  1. Sign out from Account tab
  2. Tap the Design tab
- **Expected:** Gate screen renders: 'Design your products' title, 'Create an account' button and 'I already have one — log in' link; both navigate to /account. No setup modal, no API calls with a token.

### DES-2 — First-entry setup: single brand auto-advances to collection, default collection auto-picked [P0]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui
- **Risk:** chooseBrand falls through silently on /api/catalogues failure — user is left on the collection step; verify that path with network off.
- **Steps:**
  1. Sign in as claudetest@nanocrew.dev (ensure the account has exactly one brand)
  2. Open the Design tab
  3. Observe the setup modal
- **Expected:** Modal opens; with one brand it skips straight to the collection step, then auto-selects the 'first-drop' collection (or first non-'Web Assets' collection, or creates 'First drop' via POST /api/catalogues) and lands on the canvas. Chip top-left shows the short collection name.

### DES-3 — Multi-brand step: brand cards, back link, 'Add a brand image' quick link [P1]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui
- **Steps:**
  1. Use an account with 2+ brands (or add a second store to claudetest)
  2. Open Design; on the brand step inspect each card
  3. Pick a brand, then tap '‹ Brands' on the collection step
- **Expected:** Cards show OG/hero/logo image or tileColor+initials fallback; card without an image shows '＋ Add a brand image' which jumps directly into Site-assets mode for that brand. '‹ Brands' back link renders only when brands.length > 1.

### DES-4 — No-brands empty state [P1]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui
- **Risk:** Creates a real creator row + 300-credit signup bonus in production; keep the account for the 402 tests (CRD-2).
- **Steps:**
  1. Sign in with a fresh account that has no stores (creates a real row in the prod DB — use a disposable email)
  2. Open Design
- **Expected:** Brand step shows 'Create a brand to get started…' and a 'Create a brand in Studio' button that closes the modal and navigates to /studio.

### DES-5 — /api/me failure on entry surfaces the picker, not a silent 'no brands' [P1]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui
- **Steps:**
  1. Enable airplane mode on the Mac (or block api.nanocrew.app) before opening Design
  2. Open the Design tab
- **Expected:** Setup modal still opens on the brand step (retryable by re-entering); console warns '[design] failed to load brands'. It must NOT show the 'Create a brand in Studio' empty state as if the account had no brands.

### DES-6 — Create a new collection (presets + name + season) [P0]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui, api
- **Steps:**
  1. Long-press the top-left collection chip (350ms) to open the setup at the collection step
  2. Tap 'Summer' preset — name prefills 'Summer 2026'; tap 'Drop' — name clears
  3. Verify 'Create collection' is disabled with an empty/whitespace name
  4. Enter a name and tap Create
- **Expected:** POST /api/catalogues {name, season, storeSlug} creates the row; sheet closes; the new collection becomes active with an empty canvas and empty designs dock; empty collection auto-opens the product picker.

### DES-7 — Collection chip tap vs long-press [P1]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui
- **Steps:**
  1. In a product collection, tap the chip
  2. Close the picker, long-press the chip
  3. Switch to Site assets mode, tap the chip
- **Expected:** Tap in collection mode opens the full-screen ProductPicker; long-press always opens the brand/collection setup at the collection step; tap in asset mode opens the setup (not the picker).

### DES-8 — Canvas state persistence (nodes, scale, colour) across app restarts [P0]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui, api
- **Steps:**
  1. Add 2 products and 1 design node; move them; pinch-resize one; apply a colourway via the product detail sheet
  2. Wait >1s (debounced save is 600ms), kill the app from the app switcher
  3. Relaunch, sign-in persists, reopen Design and the same collection
- **Expected:** PUT /api/canvas/:catalogueId saved the node set; on reload GET /api/canvas/:id rehydrates positions, integer-percent scale, colorImage/selectedColor, groups (member groupIds rewritten to fresh row ids), and composition nodes with previewUrl/status.

### DES-9 — Canvas API auth/ownership [P0]

- **Methods:** api
- **Steps:**
  1. Obtain a bearer token for claudetest (POST {SUPABASE_URL}/auth/v1/token?grant_type=password with the anon key), then curl the dev server or api.nanocrew.app
  2. GET /api/canvas/<own-catalogue-id> without Authorization header
  3. GET and PUT /api/canvas/<someone-elses-catalogue-id> with the token
  4. PUT /api/canvas/<own-id> with body {} (no nodes)
- **Expected:** No token -> 401 {error:'unauthorized'}; foreign catalogue -> TenantError status (403/404), no data leak; missing nodes -> 400 'nodes is required'; valid PUT returns {ok:true,count}.

### DES-10 — Rapid collection switching doesn't cross-pollute canvases [P2]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui
- **Risk:** Race — repeat several times; a failure shows A's designs in B's dock.
- **Steps:**
  1. From the setup sheet pick collection A, immediately long-press the chip and pick collection B before A finishes loading
- **Expected:** The catalogueRef guard drops A's late response; only B's designs/nodes render; saving writes to B's canvas id only.

  1. Open 'More options', set effort to Max (4)

  3. Shorten the prompt to 'panda', tap Enhance (disabled when prompt empty)
- **Expected:** GET /api/idea?effort=4 returns {idea} into the prompt; POST /api/enhance {prompt,effort} replaces it with a rich prompt; failures are silently ignored (prompt untouched) — verify by blocking network mid-tap.

### GEN-9 — Staged review: Apply change, Regenerate, Discard [P0]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui
- **Steps:**
  1. With a staged image, type 'add the text SALE' and tap Apply change
  2. Then tap ↻ Regenerate
  3. Then tap Discard
- **Expected:** Apply change re-generates using the staged https URL as reference with 'Keep the overall composition…' appended (meme wrapper NOT re-applied on re-roll); Regenerate re-runs the original prompt; Discard returns to the form with prompt/options intact; Close resets everything including prefill.

### GEN-10 — Model refusal (422) surfaces an actionable message [P1]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui, api
- **Risk:** Refusals are probabilistic — retry a few prompts; the server itself retries 3x before 422.
- **Steps:**
  1. Prompt with a named-likeness likely to trigger the provider's RECITATION refusal (e.g. a famous character by name)
  2. Generate
- **Expected:** 422 comes back with the human message ('The image model wouldn't render this one — …describe the look in your own words'); the sheet shows it in the error slot, not 'Generation failed'; on a non-comp account the debit is refunded (ledger shows debit + refund).

### GEN-11 — Network failure during generation [P1]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui
- **Steps:**
  1. Start a generation, immediately enable airplane mode
- **Expected:** fetch rejects; sheet shows 'Generation failed' (error state), busy clears, Generate re-enabled; no stuck spinner.

### GEN-12 — /api/generate contract: 401 / 400 / 403-tenant [P0]

- **Methods:** api
- **Steps:**
  1. POST /api/generate with no Authorization header
  2. POST with token but body {} (no prompt, no image)
  3. POST with a valid prompt + catalogueId belonging to another creator
- **Expected:** 401 unauthorized; 400 'prompt or image is required'; foreign catalogue -> TenantError status (403/404) BEFORE any credit debit or model call.

### GEN-13 — Content-safety pre-screen (422 before spend) [P1]

- **Methods:** unit, api
- **Risk:** Prefer unit — API probes write prohibited text into production logs.
- **Steps:**
  1. Unit: run assertSafePrompt (src/lib/content-safety.ts) against the narrow prohibited categories via a tsx script
  2. Optionally one API probe with a clearly-prohibited phrase
- **Expected:** ContentSafetyError surfaces as 4xx with its message and NO credit debit and NO model call (check ledger unchanged on a non-comp account).
