## Design command bus: Eve commands and deep links

**Entry:** src/lib/design-bus.ts (module queue + listener registered by design.tsx). Senders today: deep links translated in design.tsx (?action=generate&prompt&meme, ?edit=<designId>, ?panel=), Eve home voice intent 'new-design' with meme (src/components/eve/eve-home.tsx:312), Eve design surface handoff buttons (src/components/eve/eve-design.tsx:185,190). 'ingest-design' currently has NO in-app sender (Venus-future path).

### BUS-1 — Deep link -> open-generate with meme prefill [P0]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui
- **Steps:**
  1. App running and signed in: run `xcrun simctl openurl booted "nanocrew:///design?action=generate&prompt=a%20frog%20meme&meme=1"`
  2. Observe the Design tab
- **Expected:** Design opens, dock expands, GenerateModal opens with prompt 'a frog meme' and the Meme toggle ON; the user still has to press Generate (external actors suggest, user decides). Closing the sheet clears the prefill; re-opening via FAB shows a clean form. The deep link handles once per mount (deepLinkHandled).

### BUS-2 — Deep link -> open-editor on a design (?edit=) [P0]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui
- **Steps:**
  1. Get a real designId from GET /api/canvas/:catId
  2. Run `xcrun simctl openurl booted "nanocrew:///design?edit=<designId>"` while ON the Design tab with designs loaded
  3. Repeat with the app cold-started via the link (designs not yet loaded)
  4. Repeat with a nonexistent designId
- **Expected:** Loaded: DesignEditor opens on that design immediately. Cold: command queues in pendingCmdsRef and fires once designs hydrate. Nonexistent id: queued forever, silent no-op (no crash) — note as accepted behavior.

### BUS-3 — ?panel= expands the dock (and ?slot= does nothing) [P1]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui
- **Steps:**
  1. Open nanocrew:///design?panel=web with the dock collapsed
  2. Try panel=products and panel=content
  3. Try a ?slot= param
- **Expected:** panel=products|web|content sets dockCollapsed=false; any other value (and slot=) is ignored — no slot param is implemented in design.tsx despite older docs mentioning it.

### BUS-4 — Eve voice 'make a meme' routes to open-generate (meme:true) [P1]

- **Methods:** manual-device-only, simulator-ui
- **Risk:** eve-home matches /\bmeme\b/i on the transcribed turn — verify non-meme design requests go to Eve's own design surface instead.
- **Steps:**
  1. Voice path is manual-device-only (simulator has no mic input): on a device, open Eve and say 'make me a meme about Mondays'
  2. Simulator equivalent: exercise the same bus command via the BUS-1 deep link (design.tsx translates deep links into identical DesignCommand objects — one code path)
- **Expected:** Eve closes, router pushes /design, generator opens prefilled with the spoken idea and meme ON.

### BUS-5 — Eve design surface handoff: 'Open in Design' / 'Keep on canvas' (+ unmounted queue flush) [P1]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui
- **Risk:** Eve's design surface persists rows into a resolved catalogue — the design must exist in the CURRENT Design-tab collection for show-design/open-editor to find it; if Eve used a different collection the command queues forever. Watch for this mismatch.
- **Steps:**
  1. From Eve's design popup (reachable via Eve home in keyboard/typed mode — sendText path) generate a design, then tap 'Open in Design'
  2. Repeat with 'Keep on canvas'
  3. Do this while the Design screen has never been mounted this session
- **Expected:** 'Open in Design' sends open-editor -> Design mounts, editor opens on the design; 'Keep on canvas' sends show-design -> node added and camera flies to it. Commands sent before Design mounts queue in the module-level bus and flush on mount (registerDesignCommandListener flush).

### BUS-6 — ingest-design command (no in-app sender — dev-invoked) [P1]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui, unit
- **Steps:**
  1. Temporarily add a dev trigger (or use the React DevTools console) calling sendDesignCommand({kind:'ingest-design', url:'https://<hosted>.png', prompt:'external meme', openEditor:true})
  2. Also send one with a data: URL
  3. Send one before any collection is chosen
- **Expected:** Hosted URL: POST /api/designs {url} persists, row lands first in dock, dock expands, node added (addToCanvas default true), editor opens (openEditor:true) since the image is hosted. data: URL: posted as dataUrl and uploaded server-side. No catalogue yet: queued, executes after the collection is picked. API failure: silent no-op (nothing lands).

### BUS-7 — design-bus unit semantics [P2]

- **Methods:** unit
- **Steps:**
  1. tsx script (pattern of npm run test:lipsync): import sendDesignCommand/registerDesignCommandListener
  2. Send 2 commands before registering; register and assert both flush in order; send a 3rd and assert direct delivery; unregister and assert re-queueing
- **Expected:** Queue drains FIFO on register; listener nulling on unregister only when it is the current listener.

## Design editor (double-tap edit) and /api/edit

**Entry:** Double-tap a design node on the canvas (onNodeEdit in design.tsx -> src/components/designer/DesignEditor.tsx), or bus open-editor/deep link ?edit=. Endpoint: POST /api/edit (8cr).

### EDT-1 — Open gating: hosted designs only [P1]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui
- **Steps:**
  1. Double-tap a hosted design node
  2. Double-tap a node whose design is still generating or has a data:/missing image
- **Expected:** Hosted: selection haptic + editor opens showing the image. Non-hosted: nothing happens (silent skip).

### EDT-2 — Edit modes and iterative editing [P0]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui, api
- **Steps:**
  1. Mode 'Retouch': 'make the background a night sky' -> Apply
  2. Verify the result becomes the new stage image and 'Saved to your designs — keep editing…' appears; the new design also lands in the dock immediately
  3. Switch to 'Words' and swap lettering; then 'Remix idea'; then 'Custom'
  4. Tap Done
- **Expected:** Each Apply POSTs /api/edit {designId:<latest result>, catalogueId, instruction, mode} — the SOURCE is the previous result (iteration); every success is a NEW design row (non-destructive, prompt 'Edit — …'); mode changes the server-side instruction framing; Done closes without losing saved iterations.

### EDT-3 — Editor error paths [P1]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui
- **Steps:**
  1. Apply with the network blocked
  2. Apply as a non-comp account with balance < 8 (see CRD-2)
- **Expected:** Network: inline 'Edit failed', busy clears. 402: 'Not enough credits — need 8. Top up in Account.'; the input's text is preserved for retry (error clears on typing).

### EDT-4 — /api/edit contract: validation, ownership, refusal [P0]

- **Methods:** api
- **Steps:**
  1. POST {} -> 400 'designId, catalogueId, instruction required'
  2. POST with another creator's designId + your own catalogueId
  3. POST with a foreign catalogueId
  4. POST with no token
- **Expected:** 400 on missing; foreign design -> 404 'design not found' (store-scoped select); foreign catalogue -> TenantError; no token -> 401. Rate bucket `gen:` max 20/60s (see CRD-4).

### EDT-5 — Bus-prefilled instruction opens in Custom mode [P2]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui
- **Steps:**
  1. Send open-editor with an instruction (dev-invoke sendDesignCommand({kind:'open-editor',designId,instruction:'make it blue'}))
- **Expected:** Editor opens with mode=Custom and the instruction prefilled; a plain open (double-tap) starts at Retouch with an empty input.

## Try-on flow (photo permission + selfie) and /api/tryon

**Entry:** UI lives in the hidden feed (src/app/feed.tsx startTryOn, line ~217) — reach it via deep link `xcrun simctl openurl booted nanocrew:///feed` (tab hidden in v1). Requires at least one PUBLISHED product in the feed. Endpoint: POST /api/tryon {selfie: dataURL, productId} — selfie never persisted, render hosted in nanocrew/tryon.

### TRY-1 — Happy path with a simulator stock photo as the selfie [P1]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui
- **Steps:**
  1. Deep-link to /feed signed in; ensure a published product card renders
  2. Tap the try-on control
  3. Allow the photo-library permission dialog
  4. Pick one of the simulator's stock people photos
  5. Wait for the render
- **Expected:** Busy state, then the try-on result image renders (Cloudinary URL): the person from the photo wearing the product. Comp account: tryon has NO debit at all (rate-limited only) — balance unchanged.

### TRY-2 — Permission denied is a silent return [P2]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui
- **Steps:**
  1. Reset permission: `xcrun simctl privacy booted reset photos`
  2. Tap try-on and DENY
- **Expected:** Flow silently aborts — no error copy, no busy state (known UX gap; document). Subsequent taps do not re-prompt until permission is reset in Settings.

### TRY-3 — Signed-out try-on [P2]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui
- **Steps:**
  1. Sign out, deep-link to /feed, tap try-on
- **Expected:** Modal state shows 'Sign in to try things on.' — no picker, no API call.

### TRY-4 — /api/tryon contract: validation, published-only IDOR guard, rate limit [P0]

- **Methods:** api
- **Steps:**
  1. POST with no token -> 401
  2. POST {selfie:'https://…', productId} (not a data URL) -> 400
  3. POST {selfie:<tiny valid data URL>} with a DRAFT (unpublished) product's uuid
  4. POST with a random uuid
  5. Burn the bucket: 15 malformed POSTs (missing productId) in <60s, then one more
- **Expected:** 401; 400 'selfie (data URL) and productId required'; draft product -> 404 'product not found' (isPublished filter blocks UUID-guessing of drafts); random uuid -> 404; 16th request -> 429 {error:'rate_limited', retryAfter} with Retry-After header (bucket tryon:<uid> 15/60).

### TRY-5 — Privacy: the selfie itself is never stored [P2]

- **Methods:** api
- **Steps:**
  1. Run TRY-1, then inspect: GET /api/canvas for design rows, and confirm the response image URL is under nanocrew/tryon (transient render folder)
- **Expected:** No design/db row contains the selfie; only the generated render is hosted.

## Product video endpoint (/api/video)

**Entry:** POST /api/video {productId, mode:'voiceover'|'veo', force?} — feed/product video generation for a published product the creator owns. No Design-tab UI button today; exercise via direct authed fetch.

### VID-1 — Voiceover mode happy path + cache [P1]

- **Methods:** api
- **Risk:** Real ElevenLabs + ffmpeg spend (~$0.01) on production keys; slow (tens of seconds).
- **Steps:**
  1. POST /api/video {productId:<own product with imageUrl>} as claudetest
  2. POST again without force
- **Expected:** First call debits 25 (comp'd -> no-op), generates the Ken-Burns+voiceover clip, saves products.videoUrl and returns {videoUrl, mode:'voiceover'}; second call returns {videoUrl, cached:true} with NO regeneration and no debit.

### VID-2 — Veo premium mode [P2]

- **Methods:** api
- **Risk:** ~$1.20 REAL Google spend per clip on the production key — run once or skip; credits are comp'd but the upstream bill is real.
- **Steps:**
  1. POST {productId, mode:'veo', force:true} — AT MOST ONCE
- **Expected:** 400-credit debit (comp'd), Veo 3 clip generated and saved; on generation/upload failure the debit is refunded (verify ledger on a non-comp account only).

### VID-3 — Validation and ownership [P1]

- **Methods:** api
- **Steps:**
  1. POST {} -> 400 'productId required'
  2. POST with an unknown productId -> 404
  3. POST with a product owned by another store -> 403 'not your product'
  4. POST with an owned product that has no imageUrl -> 400 'product has no image'
- **Expected:** Statuses as listed; the ownership check runs BEFORE any debit.

## Credit gating and rate limiting (cross-cutting)

**Entry:** src/lib/credits.ts (debit/grant, CREDIT_COSTS, isCompCreator no-op), src/lib/rate-limit.ts (DB-backed fixed window, guardRate). claudetest@nanocrew.dev is in COMP_EMAILS -> debitCredits returns early, so 402 paths need a NON-comp account (see DES-4 scratch account) or unit verification. Buckets: gen:<uid> (generate 40/60, composite 40/60, edit 20/60, merge 20/60 — SAME bucket, different maxima), ai:<uid> (idea+enhance 60/60), pf:<uid> (mockup+preview-shots 60/60), tryon:<uid> (15/60).

### CRD-1 — Comp account is never charged [P0]

- **Methods:** api, simulator-ui
- **Steps:**
  1. GET /api/creator/credits (note balance)
  2. Run one generate, one edit, one merge, one preview-shots via the UI
  3. GET /api/creator/credits again and inspect the ledger endpoint if exposed
- **Expected:** Balance identical; no debit ledger rows for claudetest — confirms COMP_EMAILS wiring and that testing paid AI in the simulator doesn't bill.

### CRD-2 — 402 insufficient_credits (requires a non-comp account) [P0]

- **Methods:** api, simulator-ui
- **Risk:** Draining a prod-DB account costs real upstream API dollars; alternatively verify the guard at unit level (debitCredits guarded UPDATE ... WHERE balance >= amount) with a tsx script against a throwaway row — but that writes to the production DB; prefer the scratch-account route.
- **Steps:**
  1. Use the scratch account from DES-4 (signup grants 300 credits)
  2. Drain the balance: 37 generates would cost real Gemini spend — instead prefer draining via cheap failures is impossible (refunds) — so either run ~12 generates (96cr) and then preview-shots/model-shots to approach 0, or accept the cost of ~37 generates (~$1.45 upstream)
  3. With balance < 8: POST /api/generate with a valid prompt
  4. In the UI: run an edit (Apply edit · 8) and a Combine-sheet preview (·16)
- **Expected:** API: 402 {error:'insufficient_credits', needed:8, balance:<n>} and NOTHING charged (atomic guarded update). UI: DesignEditor and preview-shots surfaces show 'Not enough credits — need N. Top up in Account.'; GenerateModal shows the raw 'insufficient_credits' string (data.error passthrough) — log as a copy bug.

### CRD-3 — Refund on failed paid generation [P1]

- **Methods:** api
- **Steps:**
  1. Non-comp account with sufficient balance: trigger a hard model refusal on /api/generate (see GEN-10)
  2. Inspect the credit ledger
- **Expected:** A -8 debit row followed by a +8 'refund' grant row; net balance unchanged. Same pattern for edit/merge/preview-shots/video failures.

### CRD-4 — 429 rate limiting via zero-cost malformed bursts [P0]

- **Methods:** api
- **Risk:** Uses the production rate_limits table keyed to the test user — self-expiring, no cleanup needed. Do not run bursts against endpoints that debit before validation (none do — guardRate always precedes debit).
- **Steps:**
  1. Script 40 POSTs to /api/generate with body {} (each 400s AFTER guardRate increments — free, no model call) within 60s, then send request 41
  2. While the gen bucket is at >=20, POST a VALID-shaped /api/edit and /api/merge
  3. Burn ai: with 61 GET /api/idea calls; pf: with 61 malformed /api/mockup posts; then one more each
  4. Wait out the 60s window and confirm recovery
- **Expected:** 41st generate -> 429 {error:'rate_limited', retryAfter} + Retry-After header. Because edit/merge share the gen:<uid> bucket at max 20, they 429 while generate still passes between counts 20-39 — verify this asymmetry. All buckets reset after the window; UI surfaces show the raw 'rate_limited' string where errors pass through (log as copy polish).

### CRD-5 — Limiter fails open [P2]

- **Methods:** unit
- **Steps:**
  1. Code-level/unit: point rateLimit at an unreachable DB (or stub db.select to throw) in a tsx script
- **Expected:** rateLimit returns true (never blocks the feature on limiter outage); RateLimitError is the only throw path.

# Area: Eve design popup + brand build & site pipeline (eve-design.tsx, design-bus.ts, eve-developing.tsx, brand-deck.tsx, /api/store, /api/extract-brand, /api/generate, /api/edit, /api/creator/{plan-site-edits,revise,revisions,build-site,stores/[slug]/publish}, provisioning via src/lib/provision.ts)

## Eve design popup — entry, catalogue resolve, idle state

**Entry:** src/app/studio.tsx renders <EveDesign> as a translucent overlay when eve.state==='design'. Reached ONLY via voice-intent routing in src/components/eve/eve-home.tsx routeTurn() case 'new-design' (spoken 'make me a design of X'), or programmatically via summonEve({state:'design',payload:{idea}}) from src/lib/eve-bus.ts. There is NO typed path from the guide view — on the Simulator, add a temporary dev button or one-line summonEve() call in studio.tsx (revert after) to open it. Component: src/components/eve/eve-design.tsx.

### DSGN-1 — Entry with an idea payload auto-generates on mount [P0]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui, manual-device-only
- **Risk:** Spoken entry cannot be driven in the Simulator (no mic input). The dev summon shim is a temporary code edit — revert before commit. Each auto-generate is a real Gemini image call (~$0.04, comped credits).
- **Steps:**
  1. Sign in as claudetest@nanocrew.dev in the Simulator (account must own >=1 store so the guide/intent path is realistic)
  2. Open the popup with an idea: temporarily call summonEve({ state: 'design', payload: { idea: 'a chrome skull tee' } }) from a dev shim in src/app/studio.tsx (or speak 'design me a chrome skull tee' on a real device)
  3. Observe the popup mounts over EveHome with the translucent scrim (styles.designScrim rgba(6,8,15,0.78)); Eve's orb glows through
  4. Observe phase: line shows 'Working on it…', VenusBubble placeholder then busy veil + ActivityIndicator
  5. Wait ~5-15s for POST /api/generate to return
- **Expected:** Generation starts without any tap; on success the image renders center-stage (contentFit contain), line reads 'Here you go. Tell me a tweak, or open it up to edit yourself.', '✓ Keep' + 'Open in Design ›' buttons appear, send button label becomes '✦ 8'. EveHome stays mounted underneath (deep is only 'developing' in studio.tsx line 78) so the live session keeps running.

### DSGN-2 — Entry without an idea lands in idle and asks [P0]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui
- **Steps:**
  1. Summon with no payload: summonEve({ state: 'design' })
  2. Observe initial line 'What shall I make?' and phase 'idle' after resolveCatalogue completes
  3. Confirm no VenusBubble-replacing image; the 120px orb shows in the stage
  4. Confirm placeholder text is 'Describe the design — "a chrome skull tee"' and the send button shows '✦' (no cost number)
  5. Confirm send is disabled (opacity 0.5) until non-whitespace text is typed
- **Expected:** No API generation fires on mount (verify no POST /api/generate in Metro/server logs). Typing then submitting runs generate(); empty/whitespace submit is a no-op.

### DSGN-3 — Catalogue resolution reuses the primary store's first catalogue [P1]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui, api
- **Steps:**
  1. Via api: GET /api/catalogues with claudetest's bearer token; record the first catalogue id (stores auto-create 'First drop' at creation — src/app/api/store+api.ts line 148)
  2. Open the popup and generate once
  3. GET /api/catalogues again and diff
- **Expected:** No new catalogue is created; the generated design row (GET /api/designs?catalogueId=<first>) lands in the existing first catalogue. catalogueRef caches so a second generate does not re-hit GET /api/catalogues (verify one request in server logs).

### DSGN-4 — Catalogue creation fallback when the store has zero catalogues [P2]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui, api
- **Risk:** Setup is invasive on the prod DB — prefer running against a freshly-built throwaway brand from the BUILD flow before its catalogues are touched by anything else.
- **Steps:**
  1. Setup (api): delete all catalogues for the test store via DELETE /api/catalogues (check route supports it) or use a store created without one
  2. Open the popup; watch server logs for POST /api/catalogues {name:'Designs'}
  3. Generate a design
- **Expected:** A catalogue named 'Designs' is created exactly once and the design persists into it.

### DSGN-5 — Edge: creator with NO store — generation succeeds server-side but UI errors (id missing) [P2]

- **Methods:** api, unit
- **Risk:** In practice routeTurn gates 'new-design' behind hasStore, so this is defense-in-depth; still reachable via a stale summon. Real Gemini spend per attempt.
- **Steps:**
  1. Use a store-less account (secondary throwaway signup) via api: GET /api/catalogues → expect 404 'store not found'; POST /api/catalogues → also fails (tenant lookup)
  2. POST /api/generate with prompt but catalogueId omitted → 200 { image } WITHOUT id
  3. In eve-design.tsx generate(): `if (!r.ok || !d.image || !d.id) throw` → failFrom(200,d) → generic 'That didn’t work — try rephrasing.'
- **Expected:** API returns the image but no id; the popup shows the generic error and phase 'error' even though the model call succeeded. For a NON-comp account the 8-credit debit is NOT refunded on this client-side rejection (server saw success) — flag as a latent bug/UX gap.

### DSGN-6 — ‹ back exits to Eve home from every phase [P1]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui
- **Risk:** eve-design.tsx has no AbortController/alive-flag on generate(); a late setPhase after unmount is a known-possible warning — record if seen.
- **Steps:**
  1. From idle: tap '‹ back' → popup unmounts, EveHome guide visible, scrim gone
  2. From ready (with a design): tap '‹ back' → same; confirm the design row still exists via GET /api/designs (nothing is deleted on exit)
  3. From busy: tap '‹ back' mid-generation → popup unmounts; confirm no crash when the in-flight fetch resolves after unmount (watch for setState-on-unmounted warnings in Metro logs)
- **Expected:** Exit always returns to EveHome; abandoning mid-generation logs no red-box. Note: the fetch continues server-side and the design persists (and comped credit debit stands) — expected behavior.

## Eve design popup — generate (happy + error paths)

**Entry:** eve-design.tsx generate() → POST /api/generate (src/app/api/generate+api.ts) with {prompt, catalogueId, background:'filled', aspectRatio:'1:1'}. Same endpoint as the Design tab.

### GEN-1 — Happy path typed generation with full busy-state UI [P0]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui, api
- **Risk:** ~5-15s latency typical; Nano Banana occasionally retries internally (up to 3 attempts server-side).
- **Steps:**
  1. Open popup in idle; type 'a minimalist mountain line-art tee' and press return (or the ✦ button)
  2. During flight: verify input is disabled (editable={!busy}), send button at 0.5 opacity, busy veil + ActivityIndicator over the stage, line 'Working on it…', caption orb 'speaking' animation
  3. On success: image renders; line 'Here you go…'; '✓ Keep' and 'Open in Design ›' visible; send label now '✦ 8'; placeholder switches to 'Describe a tweak — "bigger type, night sky"'
- **Expected:** Phase idle→busy→ready with all listed UI transitions. Server log shows [pipeline:generate] ok. Response is a Cloudinary https URL (not a data: blob).

### GEN-2 — Design persists as a row in the resolved catalogue [P0]

- **Methods:** api
- **Steps:**
  1. After GEN-1, api: GET /api/designs (or the catalogue-scoped listing the Design tab uses) with the bearer token
  2. Find the newest row
- **Expected:** Row exists with the exact prompt text, storeId of the primary store, catalogueId from DSGN-3, and the same URL rendered in the popup.

### GEN-3 — showEve vision handoff on a settled design (Eve 'sees' it) [P1]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui, manual-device-only
- **Risk:** Session state races (popup opened before live connects) should degrade silently — verify no error surfaces.
- **Steps:**
  1. With EveHome's live session connected (guide voice for a returning creator; Simulator audio OUTPUT works), generate a design in the popup
  2. eve-design.tsx calls showEve({url, note}) only on SUCCESS (never mid-generation) — verify via a log breakpoint in src/lib/eve-vision-bus.ts
  3. EveHome's registerEveVisionListener fetches the image (imageForEve) and calls live.sendImage
- **Expected:** One image is sent to the live session per settled design (cost ~$0.004 per doc comment). If the session is disconnected, the publish is fire-and-forget and nothing crashes. Her audible one-sentence reaction is observable through Simulator audio output; her HEARING your reply is manual-device-only.

### GEN-4 — Model refusal → 422 with actionable message; retry works [P1]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui, api
- **Steps:**
  1. Type a prompt the provider reliably declines, e.g. a named celebrity likeness ('a photorealistic portrait of <famous person> as a tee print')
  2. Server: generate+api.ts detects RECITATION/SAFETY finishReason → refunds (no-op for comp) → 422 with refusalMessage
  3. UI: failFrom falls through to d.error → the refusal text shows in the red error line; phase 'error' (no prior design) so no Keep/Open buttons
  4. Type a rephrased safe prompt and submit again
- **Expected:** Error text is the model-limitation message ('The image model wouldn’t render this one…'), NOT a scary server error; the input stays usable and the retry generates normally. api: confirm status 422 + reason field.

### GEN-5 — 401 unauthorized mapping [P1]

- **Methods:** api, simulator-ui
- **Steps:**
  1. api: POST /api/generate with no Authorization header → expect 401 {error:'unauthorized'}
  2. UI mapping: failFrom(401) → 'Sign in to create designs.' — the popup itself is auth-gated (studio.tsx requires session) so simulate by letting the token expire or api-only
  3. Also verify eve-design.tsx generate() early-guard: with token undefined it sets the same message without any network call
- **Expected:** 401 from the route; client renders 'Sign in to create designs.' and phase 'error'.

### GEN-6 — 402 insufficient_credits — REQUIRES a non-comp account (comp bypasses the gate) [P0]

- **Methods:** api, simulator-ui
- **⛔ Boundary:** Do NOT top up the secondary account through the live Stripe paywall — stop at the 402/paywall display. Verify the error rendering and the untouched balance instead of completing any purchase.
- **Risk:** This 402 is IMPOSSIBLE to trigger as claudetest — any test report claiming it passed on the comp account is wrong. Throwaway account lives in the production DB; name it obviously (e.g. claudetest+broke@…) and note it for cleanup.
- **Steps:**
  1. First prove the comp bypass (api): as claudetest, GET /api/creator/credits, run one generate, GET again — balance UNCHANGED (debitCredits returns early via isCompCreator, src/lib/credits.ts:97)
  2. Create a secondary throwaway account (fresh email, NOT in COMP_EMAILS) → signup grants 300 credits
  3. Drain it: 37 generates at 8cr leaves 4 (~$1.50 real Gemini spend) — cheaper alternative: have Joe zero the balance directly in Supabase (prod DB — one-row update on credit_accounts)
  4. In the popup as that account, submit a generate
  5. api double-check: POST /api/generate → 402 {error:'insufficient_credits', needed:8, balance:<n>}
- **Expected:** UI shows 'Not enough credits — need 8. Top up in Account.' (failFrom 402 branch, needed interpolated); phase 'error'; nothing was charged (atomic guarded UPDATE never matched).

### GEN-7 — 429 rate limit (40/60s on gen:<id>) — cheap burst without model spend [P1]

- **Methods:** api, simulator-ui
- **Risk:** Rate limits DO apply to comp accounts (guardRate has no comp exemption) — claudetest works for this. The fixed window is DB-backed (rate_limits table, prod DB) — the bucket self-expires in 60s.
- **Steps:**
  1. guardRate runs BEFORE body validation in generate+api.ts, so: api-script 40 POSTs to /api/generate with an INVALID body ({}) — each counts against the bucket then 400s with zero Gemini cost
  2. 41st request with a VALID body within the same 60s window
  3. Inspect response: 429, JSON {error:'rate_limited', retryAfter:<sec>}, header Retry-After
  4. In the popup, submit during the window
  5. Wait retryAfter seconds; submit again
- **Expected:** 429 shape as above; UI shows failFrom fallthrough → literal 'rate_limited' text (poor copy — log as a UX observation, not a failure); after the window expires the generate succeeds.

### GEN-8 — Network failure mid-generate [P1]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui
- **Steps:**
  1. Start a generate, then kill the dev server (or toggle the Mac's network off — the Simulator shares it)
  2. Observe the catch path: error message set from the fetch error, phase 'error' (no current) — input remains
  3. Restore network/server; submit the same prompt again
- **Expected:** Graceful red error line, no crash, no stuck busy veil; retry succeeds. If a design already existed (fail during a re-generate after error-retry), phase returns to 'ready' and the prior image is preserved.

### GEN-9 — Busy-state input guards [P1]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui
- **Steps:**
  1. Submit a generate; while busy, try typing (input editable={!busy} → keyboard input blocked) and tap the send button repeatedly (disabled={busy||!input.trim()})
  2. Also verify submit() early-returns when phase==='busy'
- **Expected:** Exactly one POST /api/generate in server logs; no queued duplicate requests.

## Eve design popup — typed tweak loop (edit) + undo + EDIT_COST

**Entry:** eve-design.tsx edit() → POST /api/edit (src/app/api/edit+api.ts) with {designId, catalogueId, instruction, mode:'custom', background:'filled'}; local stackRef holds the undo history.

### EDIT-1 — Happy tweak replaces the image and grows the stack [P0]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui, api

  3. Verify the header now shows '↶ undo' (stack length 2)
  4. Run a second tweak ('add small stars')
- **Expected:** Each tweak is one POST /api/edit; the shown image updates each time; stack grows to 3; the send button reads '✦ 8' throughout the loop.

### EDIT-2 — Edits are non-destructive server-side [P0]

  1. api: after EDIT-1, list designs for the catalogue
  2. Confirm the ORIGINAL design row still exists unchanged, plus one new row per tweak with prompt 'Edit — <instruction>'
- **Expected:** N tweaks → N+1 rows; no row mutated or deleted.

### EDIT-3 — Undo reverts locally without server calls [P1]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui, api

  2. Tap undo again → original image, and the undo control DISAPPEARS (stack length 1 guard, canAct && stackRef.length>1)
  3. Verify server logs show NO network call for either undo; api: all design rows still exist

### EDIT-4 — EDIT_COST display mirrors the server constant [P1]

- **Methods:** unit, simulator-ui
- **Risk:** EDIT_COST is module-local and not exported — the unit check must read CREDIT_COSTS and grep/parse eve-design.tsx, or export the const.
- **Steps:**
  1. Code assert (unit-style tsx script per repo pattern): import EDIT_COST semantics — eve-design.tsx hardcodes 8; compare against CREDIT_COSTS.design_edit in src/lib/credits.ts
  2. UI: idle send button shows '✦' only; after a design exists it shows '✦ 8'
- **Expected:** 8 === CREDIT_COSTS.design_edit. The display constant is a mirror (comment says server is source of truth) — add a drift check to catch a future server-side price change.

### EDIT-5 — Edit failure preserves the current design and allows retry [P1]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui

  1. With a ready design, kill the network and submit a tweak
  2. Observe error line ('Edit failed' or the fetch message), phase back to 'ready' (NOT 'error' — edit's catch always sets ready), current image unchanged, Keep/Open buttons still present
  3. Restore network and retry the same instruction
- **Expected:** The prior design is never lost on a failed edit; retry succeeds and pushes onto the stack.

### EDIT-6 — 402 on edit (non-comp account) [P1]

- **Methods:** api, simulator-ui

  2. api: POST /api/edit → 402 {error:'insufficient_credits', needed:8, balance}

### EDIT-7 — 429 shared-bucket quirk: edit limits at 20 while generate allows 40 on the SAME bucket [P2]

- **Methods:** api
- **Steps:**
  1. api: fire 20 invalid-body POSTs to /api/edit (bucket gen:<id>, max 20 — guardRate precedes validation) within 60s
  2. 21st valid /api/edit → expect 429
  3. Immediately POST a valid /api/generate (same gen:<id> bucket but max 40)

### EDIT-8 — API validation and tenancy on /api/edit [P1]

- **Methods:** api
- **Steps:**
  1. api with claudetest token: POST missing instruction → 400 'designId, catalogueId, instruction required'
  2. POST with a designId belonging to another creator's store (or a random uuid) + own catalogueId → 404 'design not found'
  3. POST with another creator's catalogueId → TenantError status (403/404)
  4. POST with prohibited content in instruction (per src/lib/content-safety.ts narrow set) → ContentSafetyError status, no debit
- **Expected:** Each request fails with the listed status; no design rows created; no credits moved (comp anyway, but verify no ledger rows).

### EDIT-9 — Silent no-op guard when catalogueRef is null [P2]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui, unit
- **Steps:**
  1. Reproduce only in the DSGN-5 no-store edge: with current set but catalogueRef null, submit a tweak
  2. edit() early-returns before setPhase('busy')
- **Expected:** Nothing happens — no spinner, no error. Input text was already cleared by submit(), so the typed instruction is silently LOST. Log as a UX gap.

## Design-bus handoffs (Keep / Open in Design) + queued command semantics

**Entry:** eve-design.tsx keepOnCanvas()/openInDesign() → sendDesignCommand (src/lib/design-bus.ts) → onHandoff closes the popup and router.push('/design'); src/app/design.tsx registers the listener on mount (line 1415) and retries commands whose designs haven't loaded (pendingCmdsRef, lines 1341-1424).

### BUS-1 — Open in Design — COLD (Design tab never mounted this session) [P0]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui
- **Risk:** Race-prone by design: the retry effect only fires on catalogue/designs.length changes — if the new design isn't in the first page of the fetched list the command can strand. Watch for a silently-ignored handoff.
- **Steps:**
  1. Fresh app launch → Eve tab → open popup → generate a design
  2. Tap 'Open in Design ›'
  3. Popup closes, router pushes /design; the open-editor command was QUEUED before the listener existed
  4. design.tsx mounts → listener registers → queue flushes → design likely not yet in the fetched list → command parks in pendingCmdsRef → the designs-loaded effect replays it
- **Expected:** Within a couple seconds of the Design tab settling, the design EDITOR opens on exactly the design generated in the popup (match by image URL). No crash if designs load slowly.

### BUS-2 — Keep — show-design lands the design on the canvas [P0]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui
- **Steps:**
  1. Generate in the popup, tap '✓ Keep'
  2. On the Design tab: the design should be added as a canvas node and the camera should fly to it (focusNode(addNode(...)), design.tsx line 1355)
- **Expected:** Design visible on canvas, focused; NOT opened in the editor (that's open-editor only).

### BUS-3 — Warm handoff — listener already registered dispatches immediately [P1]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui
- **Steps:**
  1. Visit the Design tab first (mount + load designs), then switch to the Eve tab
  2. Generate + 'Open in Design ›'
  3. Because expo-router keeps the tab mounted, sendDesignCommand hits the live listener synchronously
- **Expected:** Editor is already open (or opens instantly) when the Design tab appears — no visible delay vs BUS-1.

### BUS-4 — design-bus module semantics (pure unit) [P1]

- **Methods:** unit
- **Steps:**
  1. Write a standalone tsx test (repo pattern: src/lib/__tests__/venus-formants.test.ts, run via npx tsx) importing src/lib/design-bus.ts
  2. Assert: (1) send-before-register queues and register flushes in FIFO order; (2) send-after-register dispatches synchronously; (3) the unregister fn clears the listener only if it is still the registered one; (4) a second register replaces the first and drains any new queue; (5) commands sent after unregister queue again
- **Expected:** All five assertions pass; module state resets predictably between listeners.

### BUS-5 — Unknown designId never crashes, just strands [P2]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui, unit
- **Steps:**
  1. unit/simulator: dispatch sendDesignCommand({kind:'open-editor', designId:'nonexistent'}) with the Design tab mounted
  2. design.tsx pushes it to pendingCmdsRef and retries on each designs change
- **Expected:** No crash, no error toast; the command is simply never fulfilled. Acceptable, but confirm repeated retries don't loop hot (the retry effect only fires on dependency changes).

Voice entry ('edit my site' → routeTurn 'edit-site') is manual-device-only. Requires a brand with a REAL deploymentUrl/customDomain (build one via the BUILD flow first).

### DEV-4 — Typed edit capture, review list, remove [P0]

  2. Pen disarms after one stroke; scroll the page — the mark stays anchored to the section (SCROLL_SCRIPT sync)

### DEV-6 — Submit → forge revision (structural edits) → review handoff [P0]

## Forge revision server routes (plan-site-edits, revise, revisions, approve/decline)

**Entry:** src/app/api/creator/plan-site-edits+api.ts, revise+api.ts, revisions+api.ts, revisions/[id]/approve+api.ts + decline+api.ts. api method: authed fetch against the local dev server with claudetest's Supabase bearer token (password-grant via the Supabase auth REST endpoint; creds noted in .env.local around line 79).

### API-1 — plan-site-edits happy shape + caps [P0]

- **Methods:** api
- **Risk:** LLM-classified — assert structurally (keys, caps, slot validity), not exact strings. Each call is one gemini-2.5-flash request.
- **Steps:**
  1. POST {messages:[{role:'user',text:'change the hero to a beach at sunset'},{role:'assistant',text:'got it'},{role:'user',text:'and make the about section a 3-photo gallery'}]}
  2. Assert 200 {images, edits, assets}: beach → images[0] slot 'hero' with a merged vivid prompt; gallery → an entry in edits plus up to 3 asset prompts
  3. Send >6 image requests / >20 edits in one transcript and assert the slices (6/20/8) hold
  4. Assert greetings/'yes do it' turns produce no entries
- **Expected:** Buckets match the SYSTEM contract; invalid slots filtered; console log [pipeline:plan] present.

### API-2 — plan-site-edits error paths: 401 / 400 / 429 / 502 [P1]

- **Methods:** api
- **Steps:**
  1. No token → 401
  2. {messages:[]} → 400 'invalid body'
  3. Burst: 30 invalid-body POSTs (bucket plan-edits:<id>, 30/60s; guardRate precedes parsing) then 1 valid → 429 with retryAfter
  4. 502 transient path: not reliably forceable — verify the message mapping ('Busy — try again in a moment.' vs 'Could not plan the edits.') by code review or a temporary invalid GOOGLE_GENAI model override (skip on shared env)
- **Expected:** Statuses and shapes exactly as coded; 429 carries Retry-After.

### API-3 — revise validation, tenancy, and normalization [P0]

- **Methods:** api
- **Risk:** A 'building' row ENQUEUES a real forge job — for pure validation tests use image-only bodies (no forge) or a bogus... no: slug must be owned and real. Prefer image-only shapes for validation-focused runs, and reserve one real requestMd submit for API-4.
- **Steps:**
  1. POST without storeSlug → 400; with an unknown or another-creator slug → 404
  2. POST {storeSlug, editPlan:{images:[]}} with no requestMd and no images → 400 'requestMd or editPlan.images required'
  3. POST with 12 annotations → only 8 stored; transcript of 100 turns → last 60 kept, each text clipped to 2000 chars; 25 plan edits → 20 kept
  4. Assert response counts {images, edits, total} computed server-side match the clipped inputs
  5. Image-only body (images with generated/placed flags, no requestMd) → status 'approved', branch 'direct/<ts>'; requestMd body → status 'building', branch 'revision/<ts>'
- **Expected:** All caps and both submit shapes behave exactly as revise+api.ts codes them; a durable store_revisions row exists per submit.

### API-4 — Revision lifecycle: building → ready (preview) via the forge worker [P0]

- **Methods:** api, simulator-ui
- **Risk:** Duration 3-15 min; real cost: one headless Claude session on the droplet (order ~$0.5-3) + Vercel preview build. Requires the forge droplet + worker to be up (ssh nanocrew-forge to check if a job never starts).
- **Steps:**
  1. Submit one real forge revision (DEV-6 or api POST /api/creator/revise with a small requestMd like 'Make the footer copyright year 2026')
  2. Poll GET /api/creator/revisions?storeSlug=<slug> every ~30s
  3. Expect status building → ready with a previewUrl (Vercel branch deploy) within ~3-15 min
  4. Open the previewUrl in Safari and eyeball the change on the revision/<ts> branch deploy — brand main is untouched
- **Expected:** Branch-based revision discipline holds (never on main); the preview reflects the requested edit; failure would surface as a failed status rather than vanishing.

### API-5 — approve gating: only 'ready' merges; decline mirror [P0]

- **Methods:** api, simulator-ui
- **⛔ Boundary:** Approving deploys to the PRODUCTION storefront of the test brand — fine for our throwaway brand, but never run these routes against a real creator's slug.
- **Steps:**
  1. While API-4's revision is still 'building': POST /api/creator/revisions/<id>/approve → 409 'cannot approve a building revision'
  2. Unknown id → 404; a revision on a store the user isn't a member of (use the secondary account's token against claudetest's revision id) → 403
  3. Once 'ready': approve → 200 {status:'approved'} → merge to main → production deploy updates the live site (verify in Safari after the deploy, ~1-3 min)
  4. On a second ready revision: POST decline → status flips per decline+api.ts and no merge happens
- **Expected:** State machine enforced server-side; approve merges exactly once; declined branches never reach main.

## Brand build pipeline — interview → extract-brand → BrandReview → POST /api/store → provisioning → live site

**Entry:** Eve tab guide '🎙 Build your brand' CTA (store-less creator) → interview view; ⌨ header toggle gives the typed ChatInterview (Simulator path — mic streams silence). Finalize → POST /api/extract-brand → BrandReview (src/components/brand-review.tsx) → 'Create my store' → POST /api/store (src/app/api/store+api.ts) → provisionStorefront (src/lib/provision.ts) → forge → Vercel.

### BUILD-1 — Typed interview end-to-end on the Simulator (buildReady gate) [P0]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui, manual-device-only
- **Risk:** Gemini Live session flakiness (connecting/reconnects) is the main env dependency; if typed turns hang, check live.state and the audio-busy modal. Voice interruption/VAD behavior is manual-device-only.
- **Steps:**
  1. Precondition: claudetest has NO store (or delete the previous test brand first) — the '🎙 Build your brand' CTA only shows for store-less creators; for a returning creator, 'create another brand' is voice-routed (manual-device-only) — plan test order accordingly
  2. Tap the CTA; grant mic (Simulator will stream silence — fine); tap ⌨ to enter keyboard mode (mutes mic)
  3. Type answers to Eve's questions: brand name, products, style, colors, logo direction, site wishes — one per turn
  4. Assert the '✓ Build my brand' pill is ABSENT before 3 user turns; appears after ≥3 turns + Eve's 'ready to build' cue, or unconditionally at 6 turns (safety net)
  5. Tap '✓ Build my brand' (finalize)
- **Expected:** Live session responds to typed turns (sendText into the same Gemini Live session); buildReady latches per the regex/floor logic in eve-home.tsx lines 201-209; finalize shows the spinner then lands on BRAND COMPILED (BrandReview).

### BUILD-2 — extract-brand API contract + error paths [P0]

- **Methods:** api
- **Steps:**
  1. Happy (api): POST /api/extract-brand with a plausible 8-turn transcript → 200 {brand} with name, tagline, designStyle in the 5-value enum, designSystem.palette of 5 roled hexes, products, logo.direction, siteNotes
  2. 401 no token; 400 {messages:[]}
  3. 422: single gibberish message ('asdf') → 'could not extract a brand yet' (model-dependent — accept 200-with-invented-brand as a logged observation if it occurs)
  4. 429: 20 invalid-body posts (extract:<id> bucket 20/60) then a valid one
  5. Transcript cap: send 60 messages → only last 40 considered (verify via prompt-size effects or code-trust; mark inspected)
- **Expected:** Statuses and BrandResult shape as coded; transient upstream errors map to the friendly 502 message.

### BUILD-3 — BrandReview editing mutates what createStore sends [P0]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui, api
- **Steps:**
  1. On BRAND COMPILED: edit the name (append '-QA'), tweak the tagline, change one palette swatch via the hex picker, switch the website template in the horizontal picker
  2. Tap 'Create my store'
  3. api: GET /api/me → the new store's name/tagline reflect the edits; the stores row's designSystem palette contains the changed hex; brandProfile.transcript captured (≤80 turns)
- **Expected:** Whatever was approved is exactly what POST /api/store received — edits are not lost.

### BUILD-4 — createStore happy path (comp = website plan): store row, catalogue, logo, building state, fanfare [P0]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui, api
- **⛔ Boundary:** The LIVE-Stripe Connect account creation is an acceptable non-payment side-effect but STOP there: never proceed into Connect onboarding, payouts, or any Stripe money movement. AUTO_FIRST_DROP is not set in .env.local (verified) so no automatic Printful product generation fires — re-verify the env before running; if it were '1', first-drop would spend Gemini + create Printful products.
- **Risk:** Creates durable prod artifacts: DB rows, Cloudinary logo/OG assets, a GitHub repo, a Vercel project, a Stripe Connect account. See BUILD-8 cleanup.
- **Steps:**
  1. After BUILD-3's Create: expect the created/launch state in the UI and the /api/say fanfare audio ('<brand> is online…') through Simulator output
  2. api verify: store slug slugified from name (collision suffix -2 if rerun); catalogues has 'First drop — <first product>'; logoUrl (Cloudinary) or null-tolerated; ogImageUrl built; status 'building' (comp entitlements are website:true so provisioning fires)
  3. Verify Studio dashboard refetch (emitEveEvent store-created → BrandDeck shows the brand)

- **Expected:** One store row + one catalogue + provisioning job enqueued (store_revisions row with branch '__provision__'); creation returns even if logo generation failed (logo null beats failure).

### BUILD-5 — Provisioning lifecycle: building → ready, durable progress UI, live storefront [P0]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui, api
- **Risk:** Real cost: logo + hero Nano Banana images, an authorBrandBrief gemini-2.5-pro call, a headless Claude forge session (~$1-5), GitHub repo + Vercel project creation. Requires the forge droplet up and provisioning env present (GITHUB_TOKEN/OWNER, VPS_HOST/USER, VERCEL_TOKEN) — provisioning SILENTLY SKIPS if missing: if status never leaves 'building' with no store_revisions job row, check env/droplet first, not the app.

  1. Open StudioComposer → Edit-site tab: animated progress bar + elapsed timer + phase label derived from durable signals (store.status + the provision store_revisions row), surviving app restarts (~6s poll)
  2. Poll api GET /api/me (or /api/creator/stores/<slug>) until status 'ready' and deploymentUrl is a real Vercel URL (not github.com)
  3. Open the deploymentUrl in Safari: template matches the chosen designStyle (minimal/bold/elegant/extravagant/street per provision.ts TEMPLATE_BY_STYLE), palette/typography honor the review, hero image present (pre-generated via lib/hero-image.ts — never blank), CTA styled, store wired (catalogue endpoints respond)
- **Expected:** Build completes in roughly 5-20 minutes; the site is presentable-with-temporary-imagery per BUILD_FLOW.md step 2; killing/relaunching the app mid-build re-derives 'building' (durable, not a local flag).

- **Methods:** api, simulator-ui
- **⛔ Boundary:** Stop at the paywall display — do not start a live Stripe checkout/trial.
- **Steps:**
  1. As the secondary NON-comp free account: complete an interview (or api-POST /api/store with a canned BrandResult) → 402 {error:'subscription_required', plan, maxBrands, brandCount}
  2. UI: the Paywall sheet opens with the subscription pitch (eve-home.tsx createStore 402 branch)
  3. brand_limit: requires a paid non-comp account at its cap — verify by code-path reading or api against such an account if one exists; otherwise mark not-executable
  4. api validation: POST /api/store {} → 400 'invalid body'; missing brand.name → 400
- **Expected:** Free accounts cannot launch; comp (claudetest) can never see these 402s — any pass claim on claudetest is invalid.

### BUILD-7 — Slug collision suffixing (api) [P2]

- **Methods:** api
- **Risk:** Each POST fires full provisioning for comp (website:true) — TWO forge builds. To avoid the second build's cost, delete the duplicate store immediately (the forge job may still run; acceptable, or time the delete before the worker picks it up).
- **Steps:**
  1. api: POST /api/store twice with the same brand JSON (comp token)
  2. Second creation should return slug '<base>-2' (retry loop, up to 5 attempts)
- **Expected:** No 500 on collision; distinct slugs; both stores listed in /api/me. Delete the duplicate afterwards.

### BUILD-8 — build-site route for an existing brand: 409 site_exists / 422 no_design_system / 402 upgrade_required [P2]

- **Methods:** api
- **Steps:**
  1. api: POST /api/creator/build-site {storeSlug:<built brand>} → 409 {error:'site_exists', deploymentUrl}
  2. 402: secondary free account with any store-less... (they can't own a store) — verify by reading getEntitlements gate; mark api-not-executable without a Starter account
  3. 422: needs a store row with null designSystem (legacy shape) — construct only if such a row exists; otherwise code-inspect
  4. 400 missing storeSlug; 404 unknown slug
- **Expected:** All gates return the exact statuses without firing a doomed provision.

### BUILD-9 — Cleanup checklist for every brand built in testing [P0]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui, api
- **⛔ Boundary:** Deleting Supabase auth users and Stripe/GitHub/Vercel artifacts are Joe-performed manual steps (dashboard logins) — hand off with exact names/slugs rather than doing them via credentials.
- **Steps:**
  1. In-app: BrandDeck → composer → delete the brand (onDeleted flow) — removes the store from /api/me and the deck
  2. Verify the brand is NOT public: never call publish {listed:true} without immediately unlisting; confirm /api/market does not list it
  3. Manual cleanup (delete does NOT cascade to external systems): the per-brand GitHub repo (<GITHUB_OWNER>/<slug>), the Vercel project + deployments, Cloudinary assets under nanocrew/logos + nanocrew/designs + nanocrew/critique-shots, the Stripe Connect account (archive in the live Stripe dashboard), and any leftover store_revisions/forge branches
  4. Confirm the secondary throwaway auth users are noted for Joe to remove from Supabase Auth (prod)
- **Expected:** Prod DB and Market are left with no visible test residue; external artifacts are enumerated for manual removal.

## Publish / go-live phases (app-only listing)

**Entry:** POST /api/creator/stores/[slug]/publish (src/app/api/creator/stores/[slug]/publish+api.ts) — Studio → Settings → 'Marketplace → Open shop'. Lifecycle per docs/studio/BUILD_FLOW.md §4: needs an active plan + ≥1 published product; no website required.

### PUB-1 — Gates provable WITHOUT going live: empty shop 409 + building 409 + 404 + 401 [P0]

- **Methods:** api
- **Steps:**
  1. api on the fresh test brand (no published products): POST publish {listed:true} → 409 {error:'no_published_products'} — proves the plan gate passed (comp active) and the product gate blocks, with zero public side-effect
  2. While the brand is still status 'building': POST publish → 409 'still building — try again once it is ready'
  3. Unknown slug → 404; no token → 401
- **Expected:** All gates fire in order (build-state, plan, products); store stays isPublic:false throughout.

### PUB-2 — 402 subscription_required on publish (non-comp) [P2]

- **Methods:** api
- **Steps:**
  1. Not directly executable: a free account cannot own a store to publish. Verify via code that getEntitlements gates listed:true, and exercise it only if a lapsed-plan store exists
  2. Record as code-inspected if no fixture exists
- **Expected:** publish {listed:true} without an active plan → 402 {error:'subscription_required'}.

### PUB-3 — Full open-shop round trip on the throwaway brand (list then IMMEDIATELY unlist) [P1]

- **Methods:** api, simulator-ui
- **⛔ Boundary:** HARD STOP after listing verification: never open the public storefront checkout, never create a Stripe payment sheet, and NEVER let any flow reach Printful ORDER creation (/api/public/checkout is out of bounds). Product-catalog sync to Printful is the accepted ceiling; delete/unpublish the product afterwards.
- **Risk:** This briefly exposes a test brand in the live in-app Market — schedule at a low-traffic time and keep the window to seconds.
- **Steps:**
  1. Prereq: publish one product from the Design tab (this syncs a product to Printful's catalog — a PRODUCT, not an order; flag but acceptable)
  2. api: POST publish {listed:true} → 200 {ok, listed:true, status:'live'}
  3. Verify GET /api/market now includes the brand and nanocrew.app/b/<slug> resolves
  4. Within the same minute: POST publish {listed:false} → 200 {ok, listed:false}; store back to isPublic:false, status 'ready' (no custom domain)
  5. Verify /api/market no longer lists it
- **Expected:** Listing and delisting are symmetric; the first-open notifyPlatform fires once (best-effort). The production Market shows the test brand only for the seconds between the two calls.

## Cross-cutting: credit gating, comp exemption, rate limiting

**Entry:** src/lib/credits.ts (debit/grant/atomic guard), src/lib/comp.ts (COMP_EMAILS — claudetest@nanocrew.dev is listed per .env.local), src/lib/rate-limit.ts (DB-backed fixed window, fail-open).

### CRED-1 — Comp exemption is total for claudetest (the premise of this whole test setup) [P0]

- **Methods:** api
- **Steps:**
  1. api: GET /api/creator/credits → record balance
  2. Run 1 generate + 1 edit + 1 revise-adjacent generate via the popup
  3. GET /api/creator/credits again
  4. GET /api/creator/subscription → entitlements {plan:'advanced', status:'comp', active:true, website:true, maxBrands:9999}
- **Expected:** Balance is IDENTICAL before/after (debitCredits early-returns via isCompCreator); comp entitlements confirm the account can launch website brands without Stripe. This also formally documents why 402s must be tested elsewhere.

### CRED-2 — Debit-then-refund on failure for a non-comp account [P1]

- **Methods:** api
- **Risk:** Refund is fire-and-forget (void grant().catch) — allow a second before re-reading the balance.
- **Steps:**
  1. Secondary account with credits: record balance; POST /api/generate with a refusal-prone prompt → 422
  2. Record balance again
- **Expected:** Net balance unchanged (8 debited, 8 refunded with reason 'refund'); a SUCCESSFUL generate nets -8. Confirms the charge-before-model/refund-on-no-image discipline.

### CRED-3 — Atomic insufficient-credits guard under concurrency [P2]

- **Methods:** api
- **Steps:**
  1. Secondary account with balance set to exactly 8
  2. Fire 2 concurrent POST /api/generate requests (Promise.all in a script)
  3. Read balance + both statuses
- **Expected:** Exactly one 200/202-path and one 402; balance never negative (guarded UPDATE ... WHERE balance >= amount). The winner's spend may then refund if its model call fails — assert from the ledger, not just the end balance.

### CRED-4 — 429 window semantics: shape, Retry-After honesty, reset [P1]

- **Methods:** api
- **Risk:** The limiter fails OPEN on its own DB errors — if a burst unexpectedly all-passes, check server logs for rate_limits table errors before filing an app bug.
- **Steps:**
  1. Trip any bucket per GEN-7; record retryAfter
  2. Retry at retryAfter-2s → still 429; retry at retryAfter+2s → success (fresh window, count resets to 1)
  3. Verify the fixed-window (not sliding) behavior: after reset, a fresh burst is allowed immediately
- **Expected:** Retry-After is accurate to ±2s; fixed windows reset fully; comp accounts are NOT exempt from rate limits.
