# Area: Cross-cutting server + platform behavior (auth, tenant scoping, rate limits, credit gating, comp accounts, voice/AI utility routes, feed, admin gating, internal routes, deep links, error-shape, env)

## Test harness setup (token + base URL)

**Entry:** Prereqs for every api-method test below. Dev server: `npx expo start` (Metro serves expo-router API routes at http://localhost:8081/api/*). NOTE (dev-vs-deployed): .env.local sets EXPO_PUBLIC_API_URL to the DEPLOYED backend, so the SIMULATOR app's apiFetch (src/lib/api.ts apiUrl(), native branch) talks to PRODUCTION, not local Metro — api-method tests must curl http://localhost:8081 explicitly. Token via Supabase password grant. Files: src/lib/api.ts, src/lib/auth.ts, src/lib/supabase.ts, .env.local (EXPO_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL, EXPO_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_PUBLISHABLE_KEY, TEST_ACCOUNT_EMAIL/PASSWORD).

### SETUP-1 — Mint a bearer token for claudetest@nanocrew.dev (comp account) [P0]

- **Methods:** api
- **Risk:** Token expires ~1h — re-mint if 401s appear mid-session. This account is in COMP_EMAILS so credit spends are never billed.
- **Steps:**
  1. Read EXPO_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL, EXPO_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_PUBLISHABLE_KEY, TEST_ACCOUNT_PASSWORD from /Users/averagexjoe/code/nanocrew/.env.local.
  2. curl -s "$EXPO_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL/auth/v1/token?grant_type=password" -H "apikey: $PUBLISHABLE_KEY" -H 'Content-Type: application/json' -d '{"email":"claudetest@nanocrew.dev","password":"<TEST_ACCOUNT_PASSWORD>"}'
  3. Export access_token as $TOK. Confirm the JWT header decodes to alg=ES256 and a kid (auth.ts pins ES256 + requires kid).
- **Expected:** 200 with access_token, token_type=bearer, expires_in ~3600. $TOK is a 3-part ES256 JWT with aud=authenticated and iss=$SUPABASE_URL/auth/v1.

### SETUP-2 — Confirm local dev server is the one under test (not the deployed backend) [P1]

- **Methods:** api
- **Risk:** Both dev and deployed hit the SAME production Supabase DB + LIVE Stripe (.env.local), so writes are real either way.
- **Steps:**
  1. curl -s -o /dev/null -w '%{http_code}' http://localhost:8081/api/feed (public GET).
  2. Compare against the deployed EXPO_PUBLIC_API_URL/api/feed to confirm they are distinct hosts.
- **Expected:** localhost:8081 returns 200 JSON. Tester is consciously targeting local Metro for all api tests; simulator-ui tests exercise whatever EXPO_PUBLIC_API_URL points at.

## Auth middleware — getUserFromRequest token validation

**Entry:** src/lib/auth.ts getUserFromRequest() → verifyToken(). Reached by every authed route via `const user = await getUserFromRequest(req); if (!user) return 401`. Sample routes: GET /api/me, GET /api/creator/credits, POST /api/say, POST /api/report, DELETE /api/designs/[id]. Local ES256 verify against SUPABASE_JWKS (no network).

### AUTH-1 — Absent Authorization header → 401 on a sample of authed routes [P0]

- **Methods:** api
- **Steps:**
  1. curl -s -w '\n%{http_code}' http://localhost:8081/api/me
  2. Repeat with no auth header for: GET /api/creator/credits, POST /api/say (-d '{"text":"hi"}'), POST /api/report (-d '{"slug":"x","reason":"y"}'), POST /api/transcribe (-d '{"audio":"AAA"}'), DELETE /api/designs/00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000, POST /api/generate (-d '{"prompt":"hi"}').
- **Expected:** Every route → 401 {"error":"unauthorized"} (report uses same; feed/[id]/like uses {"error":"sign in to like"}). No DB write, no AI spend.

### AUTH-2 — Garbage / malformed token → 401 [P0]

- **Methods:** api
- **Steps:**
  1. curl with -H 'Authorization: Bearer not.a.jwt' against GET /api/me.
  2. Repeat with 'Bearer abc' (2 parts, split length !=3), and with a random 3-segment base64 blob 'eyJhbGciOiJFUzI1NiJ9.ey000.zzz'.
  3. Repeat with header 'Authorization: <token-without-Bearer-prefix>' (missing 'Bearer ').
- **Expected:** All → 401 unauthorized. verifyToken returns null on parts.length!=3, JSON parse failure, or missing Bearer prefix (header.startsWith('Bearer ') is false → token null).

### AUTH-3 — Expired token → 401 [P0]

- **Methods:** api
- **Risk:** Time-dependent; simplest is to reuse an hour-old token.
- **Steps:**
  1. Use a previously-minted $TOK, wait until past its exp (or decode exp and confirm it is in the past), then curl GET /api/me with it.
  2. Alternatively, hand-craft a token with exp in the past is not possible (signature check) — instead let a real token expire (~1h) and reuse it.
- **Expected:** 401 unauthorized. auth.ts line 113: `if (payload.exp <= now) return null`. Signature valid but expired still rejected.

### AUTH-4 — Signature-valid token with wrong audience/issuer → 401 (defense-in-depth) [P1]

- **Methods:** api, unit
- **Risk:** Hard to source a foreign valid token; acceptable to cover by code inspection + a unit test of verifyToken.
- **Steps:**
  1. If a token from a DIFFERENT Supabase project (or a non-'authenticated' aud, e.g. a service token) is available, curl GET /api/me with it.
  2. Otherwise verify by code-reading auth.ts lines 114-115 (aud must be 'authenticated', iss must equal ISSUER) — mark as inspection.
- **Expected:** 401 — aud!=='authenticated' or iss mismatch returns null even with a valid signature.

### AUTH-5 — alg confusion / 'none' rejected [P0]

- **Methods:** api
- **Steps:**
  1. Craft a JWT with header {"alg":"none"} and the same payload (no signature) → Bearer it to GET /api/me.
  2. Craft one with {"alg":"HS256","kid":"<real-kid>"} signed with a guessed secret → GET /api/me.
- **Expected:** Both → 401. auth.ts line 101 hard-pins header.alg==='ES256' && header.kid; anything else returns null (no HS/none downgrade).

### AUTH-6 — Valid token happy path bootstraps the creator row [P0]

- **Methods:** api, simulator-ui
- **Steps:**
  1. curl GET /api/me with $TOK.
  2. Confirm response has profile + stores[]; on first-ever call it also inserts the creators row and grants nothing here (credits are granted lazily by /api/creator/credits).
- **Expected:** 200 with { ...profile, stores: [...] }. creators row exists for claudetest with termsVersion backfilled (see /api/me insert onConflictDoUpdate).

## Internal service-key auth (x-internal-key)

**Entry:** src/lib/auth.ts getUserFromRequest() internal path (x-internal-key + x-internal-creator) and src/app/api/internal/release-payouts+api.ts (raw x-internal-key check). INTERNAL_API_KEY from .env.local.

### INT-1 — release-payouts rejects absent/garbage internal key [P0]

- **Methods:** api
- **⛔ Boundary:** STOP at the auth check. Do NOT POST with the REAL INTERNAL_API_KEY — a valid call scans the production orders table and executes LIVE Stripe transfers (releasePayout) to connected accounts. Only ever test the rejection path.
- **Risk:** This is money movement against LIVE Stripe. Never send the real key.
- **Steps:**
  1. curl -X POST http://localhost:8081/api/internal/release-payouts (no header).
  2. Repeat with -H 'x-internal-key: wrong'.
  3. Repeat with -H 'x-internal-key: ' (empty).
- **Expected:** All → 401 {"error":"unauthorized"}. safeEqual length-checks then timing-safe compares; empty INTERNAL_API_KEY also 401s.

### INT-2 — Internal creator-impersonation path requires BOTH key and creator id [P1]

- **Methods:** api, unit
- **⛔ Boundary:** Do not exercise the internal key against write/generation routes; /api/me GET is read-only and safe if you must confirm live.
- **Risk:** Internal path lets a holder of INTERNAL_API_KEY act as ANY creator id — high-value secret; confirm it's server-only and never in the app bundle (it is NOT EXPO_PUBLIC_).
- **Steps:**
  1. curl POST /api/me with -H 'x-internal-key: <realkey>' but NO x-internal-creator header — inspect only, do not run against a mutating route.
  2. Prefer code inspection: auth.ts lines 125-129 — internal path returns a user only when key matches AND x-internal-creator present; otherwise falls through to Bearer logic → 401 without a token.
- **Expected:** Missing x-internal-creator → no internal user minted → falls through → 401 if also no Bearer. The impersonated identity is exactly x-internal-creator (email 'internal@nanocrew').

## Per-creator tenant scoping (cross-tenant access)

**Entry:** src/lib/tenant.ts (assertDesignOwner/CompositionOwner/CatalogueOwner/ProductOwner, isStoreMember, storeForMember). Enforced by: DELETE /api/designs/[id], GET|PATCH|DELETE /api/compositions/[id], DELETE /api/creator/products/[id], POST /api/generate (catalogueId), GET|PATCH /api/creator/stores/[slug], POST /api/video (productId). TenantError carries the HTTP status (403 forbidden / 404 not found).

### TEN-1 — Forged/nonexistent resource id → 404 (not 500, not silent success) [P0]

- **Methods:** api
- **Risk:** assertProductOwner on a real other-owner product would then attempt Printful delete — do NOT target a real product you don't own; use forged ids only.
- **Steps:**
  1. curl -X DELETE http://localhost:8081/api/designs/00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000 -H "Authorization: Bearer $TOK".
  2. Repeat GET /api/compositions/00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000.
  3. Repeat DELETE /api/creator/products/00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000.
  4. Try a non-UUID id: DELETE /api/designs/not-a-uuid.
- **Expected:** Missing rows → 404 with {"error":"design not found"|"composition not found"|"product not found"}. Non-UUID may 404 or 500 depending on PG uuid cast — note which; a 500 from an uncast id is a finding.

### TEN-2 — Access a resource owned by ANOTHER creator → 403 forbidden [P0]

- **Methods:** api
- **⛔ Boundary:** For DELETE against a real other-owner resource, STOP before running — a successful forbidden check is fine, but if scoping were broken the DELETE would destroy another creator's data + call Printful. Prefer GET/read cross-tenant probes; only DELETE against forged ids.
- **Risk:** This is the core multi-tenant isolation guarantee — highest-value test in scope.
- **Steps:**
  1. Obtain a real design/composition/product id owned by a DIFFERENT creator (e.g. via GET /api/platform/admin as the admin account to list stores, or from seed data).
  2. As claudetest ($TOK), curl DELETE /api/designs/<other-owner-design-id>, GET /api/compositions/<other-owner-comp-id>, GET /api/creator/stores/<other-owner-slug>.
  3. For /api/generate, POST with catalogueId=<other-owner-catalogue-id>.
- **Expected:** 403 {"error":"forbidden"} from tenant helpers (row exists, creatorId!==user and not a collaborator). /api/creator/stores/[slug] returns 404 'not found' instead (it filters by creatorId in the query, so a non-owned slug looks absent). Note the 403-vs-404 inconsistency between helper-based routes and the stores/[slug] direct-filter route.

### TEN-3 — generate persists to a catalogue only if owned (ownership checked BEFORE spend) [P1]

- **Methods:** api
- **Steps:**
  1. As claudetest, POST /api/generate with body {"prompt":"test","catalogueId":"<other-owner-catalogue>"}.
  2. Confirm it 403s BEFORE the model call (generate+api.ts checks assertCatalogueOwner before debit/AI).
- **Expected:** 403 forbidden, no credit debit, no Nano Banana call. Order in generate+api.ts: rate-limit → safety → catalogue-owner check → apiKey → debit → model.

### TEN-4 — video route uses isStoreMember (403 'not your product') [P1]

- **Methods:** api
- **⛔ Boundary:** Do not run against a product you DO own with mode 'veo' — that triggers real Veo generation (dollars). Owner-path video generation is out of scope here.
- **Steps:**
  1. POST /api/video {"productId":"<other-owner-product>"} as claudetest.
  2. Confirm 403 before any debit/generation.
- **Expected:** 403 {"error":"not your product"}; debit + Veo/voiceover happen only after the membership check passes.

### TEN-5 — accessibleStoreIds / getCreatorStore returns only owned+collab stores [P1]

- **Methods:** api, simulator-ui
- **Steps:**
  1. GET /api/me as claudetest → note stores[].
  2. Confirm none of another creator's stores appear.
  3. If claudetest has zero stores, getCreatorStore-backed routes should 404 'no store for this creator' rather than leak another store.
- **Expected:** stores[] contains only claudetest-owned/collaborated stores; empty → 404 on getCreatorStore paths, never a fallback to someone else's store.

## Rate limiting (guardRate) per route

**Entry:** src/lib/rate-limit.ts guardRate(bucket,max,windowSec) → 429 {error:'rate_limited',retryAfter} + Retry-After header. Backed by rate_limits table (fixed window). Buckets keyed by user id. Enumerated call sites below. Note SHARED buckets with DIFFERENT maxes.

### RL-1 — Trip /api/say limit (say:<id>, 30/60s) with zero AI spend [P0]

- **Methods:** api
- **Risk:** Empty-body trick avoids real TTS cost while still exercising the limiter. Window is per-minute; wait 60s to reset.
- **Steps:**
  1. Fire 31 rapid POSTs to http://localhost:8081/api/say with $TOK and an EMPTY body ({}). guardRate runs BEFORE body-parse (say+api.ts: auth → guardRate → apiKey → parse), so each empty body 400s but still increments the counter.
  2. Bash: for i in $(seq 1 31); do curl -s -o /dev/null -w '%{http_code} ' -X POST http://localhost:8081/api/say -H "Authorization: Bearer $TOK" -H 'Content-Type: application/json' -d '{}'; done
- **Expected:** First 30 → 400 (text required), 31st → 429 with body {error:'rate_limited',retryAfter:N} and header Retry-After:N (1..60). No TTS/Gemini spend at all.

### RL-2 — Enumerate every guardRate route + limit (inventory + spot-trip) [P1]

- **Methods:** api, unit
- **Steps:**
  1. Verify limits by code (rate-limit.ts call sites): live:<id> 20/60 (voice-live-token); ai:<id> 60/60 (enhance, idea, creator/enhance-copy — SHARED); gen:<id> composite 40/60, generate 40/60, merge 20/60, edit 20/60 (SHARED bucket, MISMATCHED maxes); extract:<id> 20/60 (extract-brand); tryon:<id> 15/60; pf:<id> mockup 60/60, publish 30/60, creator/preview-shots 60/60 (SHARED, mismatched); say:<id> 30/60; plan-edits:<id> 30/60; model-video:<id> 4/600; scene-video:<id> 4/600; upload:<id> 30/60; eve-route:<id> 60/60.
  2. Spot-trip a cheap one that limits before doing work: /api/eve/route with empty body 61x (returns intent:none each, 61st → 429).
- **Expected:** Inventory matches code. eve-route 61st call → 429. This confirms the limiter increments regardless of whether the handler body succeeds.

### RL-3 — Shared-bucket / mismatched-max behavior (gen: and pf:) [P2]

- **Methods:** api
- **Risk:** merge/generate parse body before generating; send bodies that fail validation (e.g. merge without the two design ids) so the counter increments without a Nano Banana call. Verify the validation error precedes the model call in each file.
- **Steps:**
  1. Interleave calls on the shared gen: bucket: alternate /api/merge (max 20) and /api/generate (max 40) with $TOK and empty/invalid bodies to increment the single gen:<id> counter without generating.
  2. Observe that once the shared counter reaches 20, /api/merge returns 429 while /api/generate still passes (its check is >=40) — the effective limit depends on which route you hit, not a per-route budget.
- **Expected:** Confirmed: gen:<id> is one counter shared by generate/composite/merge/edit; merge/edit (max 20) 429 earlier than generate/composite (max 40) on the same bucket. Same pattern for pf: (publish 30 trips before mockup/preview-shots 60). Flag as a design inconsistency worth documenting.

### RL-4 — Limiter fails OPEN on DB error (never blocks the feature) [P2]

- **Methods:** unit
- **Risk:** Do not induce real DB failures against production.
- **Steps:**
  1. Code-inspect rate-limit.ts: rateLimit() try/catch returns true on any non-RateLimitError; guardRate returns null on non-RateLimitError.
  2. Optional: if the rate_limits table can be made unreachable in a staging DB, confirm requests still pass. Do NOT tamper with the production DB.
- **Expected:** A limiter/DB hiccup yields fail-open (requests allowed), by design. Cover primarily by inspection + a unit test that mocks db to throw.

### RL-5 — Retry-After header + window reset [P2]

- **Methods:** api
- **Risk:** The single valid /api/say after reset does spend one small Gemini TTS call (comp not credit-billed, but real API cost).
- **Steps:**
  1. After tripping say (RL-1), read the Retry-After value from the 429.
  2. Wait that many seconds, POST /api/say again with a valid {"text":"hi"} + $TOK.
- **Expected:** After the window elapses the counter resets (fresh window via onConflictDoUpdate) and the call passes (200 with audio, or 502 only on a Gemini error). Retry-After is >=1 and <=60.

## Credit gating (402 insufficient_credits) + comp exemption

**Entry:** src/lib/credits.ts (debit/debitCredits → InsufficientCreditsError → 402), src/lib/comp.ts (isCompCreator no-ops the debit). 402 emitters: /api/generate, /api/edit, /api/merge, /api/video, /api/creator/model-shots, /api/creator/model-videos, /api/creator/preview-shots, /api/creator/scene-video, /api/creator/stores/[slug]/domain/buy. Entitlement 402s (different shape): /api/store, build-site, go-live, publish, iap-verify.

### CRED-1 — Comp account (claudetest) is NEVER charged and never 402s on credits [P0]

- **Methods:** api, simulator-ui
- **Risk:** This DOES make one real Nano Banana call (real API $ though not credit-billed). Keep to one call. Verifies the whole point of testing as a comp account.
- **Steps:**
  1. GET /api/creator/credits with $TOK → note balance B0 and ledger.
  2. POST /api/generate {"prompt":"a plain black circle logo"} (cheapest, 8 credits).
  3. GET /api/creator/credits again → balance B1.
- **Expected:** B1 === B0 (debitCredits early-returns for comp via isCompCreator; no ledger debit row added). Generation succeeds (200 with an image) or fails 502 — but balance is unchanged either way. Confirms COMP_EMAILS exemption end-to-end.

### CRED-2 — Real 402 path WITHOUT spend — requires a non-comp, near-zero-balance account [P0]

- **Methods:** api
- **Risk:** Comp account can never reach this branch, so a non-comp account is mandatory to exercise the real 402. No AI spend occurs because the debit throws first.
- **Steps:**
  1. Provision a second, NON-comp test creator (not in COMP_EMAILS) and drain/leave its credit balance below 8.
  2. As that account, POST /api/generate {"prompt":"x"}.
  3. Confirm the 402 is returned BEFORE the model call (generate+api.ts debits before the Gemini call; InsufficientCreditsError short-circuits).
- **Expected:** 402 {error:'insufficient_credits',needed:8,balance:<B>} and NO Nano Banana call / no charge. This is the safe way to observe the gate — the guard rejects before spend.

### CRED-3 — Atomic debit guard — no negative balance under concurrency [P1]

- **Methods:** api
- **Risk:** One successful generation = one real Nano Banana call. Keep the account balance at exactly one op's cost to bound spend to a single image.
- **Steps:**
  1. With the non-comp near-zero account (balance == cost of one op, e.g. 8), fire 5 concurrent POST /api/generate at once (curl & background jobs).
  2. Then GET /api/creator/credits.
- **Expected:** At most one debit succeeds; the rest 402. Final balance never negative (debitCredits uses guarded UPDATE ... WHERE balance >= amount). Ledger shows exactly one -8 row, matching balanceAfter.

### CRED-4 — Entitlement 402s (subscription/website) are distinct from credit 402s [P1]

- **Methods:** api, simulator-ui
- **⛔ Boundary:** domain/buy, on a Pro account with credits, would purchase a REAL domain (money). STOP at the entitlement/credit 402 for a non-entitled account; never let domain/buy reach the purchase call.
- **Steps:**
  1. As claudetest, POST /api/creator/build-site (or GET/POST the go-live / domain/buy routes) for a store without the required plan/website entitlement.
  2. Inspect the 402 body.
- **Expected:** 402 with {error:'upgrade_required',feature:'website'} (build-site/go-live/domain/buy) or {error:'subscription_required'} (publish) or {error:'brand_limit'...} (store). These carry entitlement fields, NOT needed/balance — the client branches on the error string. Confirm the two 402 dialects are handled distinctly.

### CRED-5 — Signup bonus + comp lookup config [P2]

- **Methods:** api, unit
- **Steps:**
  1. Code-inspect comp.ts: COMP falls back to PLATFORM_ADMIN_EMAILS when COMP_EMAILS unset; isCompCreator returns false when list empty.
  2. GET /api/creator/credits on a brand-new non-comp account → first touch grants SIGNUP_BONUS (300).
  3. Unit-test isCompEmail case-insensitivity and trimming.
- **Expected:** First credits call ensures the account and grants 300 (ledger reason 'signup_bonus'). isCompEmail is case-insensitive and trims whitespace; empty config → nobody comp.

## /api/transcribe (STT utility)

**Entry:** src/app/api/transcribe+api.ts. POST { audio: base64 m4a/mp4 } → { text }. Authed; NOT rate-limited; NOT credit-gated. Used by the site-critique voice flow. Simulator has no mic — feed base64 audio directly via api.

### TR-1 — Auth + validation [P1]

- **Methods:** api
- **Steps:**
  1. POST /api/transcribe with no auth → 401.
  2. POST with $TOK and no body / body missing audio → 400 {error:'audio required'}.
  3. POST with $TOK and {"audio":"<not-base64-garbage>"}.
- **Expected:** 401 unauthorized; 400 audio required; garbage audio → 502 {error:...} from the Gemini call (or {text:''} if the model returns empty). Never 500-with-stacktrace.

### TR-2 — Happy path with real base64 audio [P1]

- **Methods:** api, manual-device-only
- **Risk:** Simulator has no mic so end-to-end record→transcribe is manual-device-only; the api path fully covers the server behavior.
- **Steps:**
  1. base64-encode a short m4a/mp4 speech clip: `base64 -i clip.m4a`.
  2. POST /api/transcribe {"audio":"<b64>"} with $TOK.
- **Expected:** 200 {text:'<verbatim transcription>'}; empty string when no clear speech. Confirms the site-critique dictation path works without a simulator mic.

### TR-3 — Unconfigured key path [P2]

- **Methods:** unit
- **Steps:**
  1. Code-inspect: if GOOGLE_GENAI_API_KEY/GEMINI_API_KEY unset → 503 {error:'transcription not configured'}. (Both are set in .env.local, so this is inspection unless keys are temporarily unset in a staging env.)
- **Expected:** 503 when unconfigured — a distinct status from the 502 model-failure path.

## /api/say (TTS utility)

**Entry:** src/app/api/say+api.ts. POST { text, voice? } → { audio: base64 WAV }. Authed + rate-limited say:<id> 30/60. Venus's Gemini 'Kore' voice; text capped 500 chars; voice must be in VOICE_OPTIONS (the Lab audition).

### SAY-1 — Auth + validation + happy path [P1]

- **Methods:** api, simulator-ui
- **Steps:**
  1. POST /api/say no auth → 401.
  2. POST $TOK {} → 400 {error:'text is required'}.
  3. POST $TOK {"text":"Your store is online."} → expect 200 {audio:'<b64 WAV>'}.
  4. Decode base64, write to say.wav, confirm a valid 44-byte-header WAV (RIFF/WAVE, 24kHz mono 16-bit) that plays.
- **Expected:** 200 with playable WAV. Production text is wrapped in the VENUS_TONE british-robot prefix (explicitVoice=false). AUDIO OUTPUT is observable; state transitions in the app are observable in simulator-ui.

### SAY-2 — Voice audition allow-list [P2]

- **Methods:** api
- **Steps:**
  1. POST $TOK {"text":"test","voice":"Puck"} (in VOICE_OPTIONS) → 200 (explicitVoice, no tone prefix).
  2. POST $TOK {"text":"test","voice":"NotAVoice"} → voice ignored, falls back to Kore + tone prefix, 200.
- **Expected:** Only names in VOICE_OPTIONS switch the voice; unknown names are silently ignored (default Kore). No injection of arbitrary voice strings into the model config.

### SAY-3 — Text length cap (500 chars) [P2]

- **Methods:** api
- **Steps:**
  1. POST $TOK with a 2000-char text.
  2. Confirm server slices to 500 before the model call (say+api.ts line 67).
- **Expected:** 200; only the first 500 chars are spoken (no error, no unbounded model input).

### SAY-4 — Rate limit (covered by RL-1) + model-failure 502 [P2]

- **Methods:** api
- **Steps:**
  1. Cross-ref RL-1 for the 429.
  2. If the model returns no audio part → 502 {error:'no audio returned'}; any Gemini throw → 502 {error:<message>}.
- **Expected:** 429 on 31st/min; 502 on model failures; 500 only if the API key is unconfigured.

## /api/voice-live-token (Gemini Live ephemeral token)

**Entry:** src/app/api/voice-live-token+api.ts. POST → { token, model }. Authed + rate-limited live:<id> 20/60. Mints a 30-min, 2-use ephemeral token; session must start within 2 min.

### VLT-1 — Auth + happy path token mint [P1]

- **Methods:** api
- **Steps:**
  1. POST /api/voice-live-token no auth → 401.
  2. POST with $TOK → expect 200 {token:'<ephemeral>',model:'gemini-2.5-flash-native-audio-preview-12-2025'}.
- **Expected:** 200 with a token name string. The real Gemini key never reaches the client. Live WS connect itself is manual-device-only (needs mic), but token minting is fully api-testable.

### VLT-2 — Rate limit live:<id> 20/60 [P2]

- **Methods:** api
- **Risk:** 20 real token mints per trip; harmless but not free. Space out or accept the minor cost.
- **Steps:**
  1. Fire 21 rapid POSTs with $TOK.
  2. NOTE: unlike say, this route has no cheap pre-work short-circuit — each allowed call mints a real ephemeral token (authTokens.create). It's a lightweight call but hits Gemini each time.
- **Expected:** 21st → 429 with Retry-After. First 20 mint tokens.

### VLT-3 — Live session behavior on device [P1]

- **Methods:** manual-device-only, simulator-ui
- **Risk:** Voice INPUT is manual-device-only; token mint + audio OUTPUT + state transitions are the simulator-observable parts.
- **Steps:**
  1. On a physical device: open Eve, start a live voice session, confirm audio out + session state transitions (connecting→live→idle), reconnect (2 uses), and 30-min ceiling.
  2. In simulator: no mic — verify only that the token mints and the UI reaches the 'connecting/listening' state, then cannot capture speech.
- **Expected:** Device: full duplex voice works. Simulator: token + UI state observable; speech capture is impossible (documented manual-device-only).

## /api/report (UGC moderation report)

**Entry:** src/app/api/report+api.ts. POST { targetType?, slug, reason } → { ok:true }. Authed, no DB write (logs + notifyPlatform email). Reached from Market via the report action (lib/blocklist.ts pairs the on-device block).

### REP-1 — Auth + validation [P1]

- **Methods:** api
- **Steps:**
  1. POST /api/report no auth → 401.
  2. POST $TOK {} → 400 {error:'slug and reason are required'}.
  3. POST $TOK {"slug":"x"} (no reason) → 400.
  4. POST $TOK {"slug":"x","reason":"   "} (whitespace) → 400 (reason.trim() empty).
- **Expected:** 401; 400 for missing slug or blank reason.

### REP-2 — Happy path + field normalization [P1]

- **Methods:** api, simulator-ui
- **⛔ Boundary:** notifyPlatform sends an ops email. This is a report-to-self ops channel (not user-facing send), so it's acceptable, but note it dispatches a real email each success.
- **Steps:**
  1. POST $TOK {"targetType":"product","slug":"some-slug","reason":"<1500-char reason>"}.
  2. POST $TOK {"targetType":"weird","slug":"s","reason":"spam"} (unknown targetType).
- **Expected:** 200 {ok:true}. reason truncated to 1000 chars; targetType coerced to 'brand' unless exactly 'product'. Server logs the report; notifyPlatform fires best-effort (email may be unconfigured — still returns ok).

## /api/feed + hidden /feed tab

**Entry:** src/app/api/feed+api.ts (public GET, optional auth for likedByMe), src/app/api/feed/[id]/like+api.ts (authed toggle), src/app/api/feed/[id]/share+api.ts. Screen src/app/feed.tsx — the tab is hidden for v1 (app-tabs.tsx comment) but the route still exists and is navigable.

### FEED-1 — Public feed loads anonymously and with auth [P1]

- **Methods:** api, simulator-ui
- **Steps:**
  1. curl GET http://localhost:8081/api/feed (no auth).
  2. curl GET /api/feed with $TOK.
  3. Compare payloads: authed adds likedByMe per item.
- **Expected:** 200 {items:[...]} both times. Only isPublished products from isPublic+status='live' stores appear (max 60), media-forward sorted (video, then model shot). Anonymous → likedByMe absent/false; authed → likedByMe reflects claudetest's likes. modelShots stripped; imageUrl = modelShots[0] fallback; siteUrl resolved (customDomain wins).

### FEED-2 — Hidden /feed screen still routes and renders [P2]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui
- **Steps:**
  1. In the simulator, deep-link to the hidden route: xcrun simctl openurl booted 'nanocrew://feed' (or navigate via router in a dev build).
  2. Confirm feed.tsx mounts and renders the vertical feed (or an empty state) without crashing, even though the tab is not shown.
- **Expected:** The /feed screen loads (it's preserved for v1). Video autoplay/mute, like/share controls render. No tab bar entry for it (hidden by design).

### FEED-3 — Like toggle requires auth and is idempotent per user [P2]

- **Methods:** api, simulator-ui
- **Steps:**
  1. POST /api/feed/<productId>/like no auth → 401 {error:'sign in to like'}.
  2. POST with $TOK once → {liked:true, likeCount:n+1}; again → {liked:false, likeCount:n}.
  3. POST with a forged/nonexistent productId.
- **Expected:** Toggle flips liked and adjusts count; onConflictDoNothing prevents dup rows. Nonexistent product id inserts/deletes against a bad FK → likely 500 (no product-existence guard) — note whether it 500s vs silently no-ops; a 500 here is a minor finding.

### FEED-4 — feed DB error → 500 error shape [P2]

- **Methods:** unit
- **Steps:**
  1. Code-inspect: feed GET wraps in try/catch → 500 {error:<message>} on failure (never leaks a raw HTML 500).
- **Expected:** Consistent JSON error envelope on failure.

## Error-shape consistency across routes

**Entry:** All routes; readJson (src/lib/api.ts) expects a JSON {error:string} envelope on non-2xx. TenantError → its status; guardRate → 429 {error:'rate_limited',retryAfter}; credit → 402 {error:'insufficient_credits',needed,balance}; entitlement → 402 {error:'upgrade_required'|'subscription_required'|...}.

### ERR-1 — Every error path returns JSON {error} (never raw HTML/empty) [P1]

- **Methods:** api
- **Steps:**
  1. Collect the 400/401/402/403/404/429/500/502/503 responses produced by AUTH-1/2, TEN-1/2, CRED-2/4, RL-1, TR-1, SAY-1, REP-1, ADM-1.
  2. Assert each has Content-Type application/json and a top-level string `error` (and status-specific fields: retryAfter on 429, needed/balance on credit 402, feature/plan on entitlement 402).
- **Expected:** Uniform envelope. This is what readJson() relies on to surface real failures instead of swallowing them; a route returning HTML or an empty body on error is a finding.

### ERR-2 — Malformed JSON body handled without a 500 stacktrace [P1]

- **Methods:** api
- **Steps:**
  1. POST /api/report, /api/generate, /api/say, /api/transcribe with $TOK and a non-JSON body (e.g. -d 'not json' and Content-Type application/json).
  2. Also send an empty body.
- **Expected:** Routes using `req.json().catch(()=>null)` → clean 400 (validation). Routes using bare `req.json()` in a try (say) → 400 'text is required'. None should 500 with a parser stacktrace.

### ERR-3 — TenantError status mapping (403 vs 404 vs 500) [P2]

- **Methods:** api, unit
- **Steps:**
  1. Cross-ref TEN-1/TEN-2: forged id → 404, other-owner → 403, unexpected error → 500.
  2. Confirm the fail() helper in compositions/[id] and the inline mapping in designs/products all use `e instanceof TenantError ? e.status : 500`.
- **Expected:** Consistent status derivation from TenantError across all tenant-scoped routes; non-TenantError falls to 500 with a message (not a leak of internal details beyond e.message).

## Dev-server vs deployed env differences (apiUrl / EXPO_PUBLIC_API_URL)

**Entry:** src/lib/api.ts apiUrl() + apiFetch(). Web → bare path (same-origin). Native → EXPO_PUBLIC_API_URL if set, else Constants.expoConfig.hostUri (Metro). Retry only on GET for 502/503/504.

### ENV-1 — Simulator app targets DEPLOYED backend when EXPO_PUBLIC_API_URL is set [P0]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui, api
- **Risk:** Misreading this means UI tests silently validate PROD, not your local changes. Decide per-test whether you want local (curl :8081, or unset EXPO_PUBLIC_API_URL) or deployed.
- **Steps:**
  1. Confirm .env.local sets EXPO_PUBLIC_API_URL to a deployed https host.
  2. In the running simulator, trigger an authed call (e.g. open Account → credits) and capture the request (Charles/proxy, or add a temporary log) to see which host it hits.
  3. Compare with expected: native + EXPO_PUBLIC_API_URL set → deployed host, NOT localhost:8081.
- **Expected:** Simulator UI talks to the DEPLOYED backend (both share prod Supabase + LIVE Stripe). To exercise LOCAL dev-server code via the UI, unset EXPO_PUBLIC_API_URL so apiUrl() falls back to the Metro hostUri. This is the single most important env gotcha for interpreting simulator-ui results.

### ENV-2 — GET-only retry on cold-start 5xx (no double-write) [P1]

- **Methods:** unit, simulator-ui
- **Steps:**
  1. Code-inspect apiFetch: RETRY_STATUS {502,503,504}, delays [400ms,1200ms], retryable only when method==='GET'.
  2. Optionally force a 502 (point at a down host) and confirm a GET retries up to 2x while a POST throws immediately.
- **Expected:** GET retries transparently on cold start; POST/PATCH/DELETE never retried (prevents duplicate Stripe charge / order). Confirms the safety comment in api.ts.

### ENV-3 — Missing token → apiFetch sends no Authorization → route 401s [P2]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui, api
- **Steps:**
  1. Signed out, trigger an authed call in the UI (or call apiFetch with no session).
  2. Confirm no Authorization header is attached and the route returns 401 with the JSON envelope.
- **Expected:** apiFetch omits the header when there's no session; server returns 401 unauthorized; the app surfaces a real error via readJson (ApiError status 401), not a swallowed success.

## Brand Console (StudioComposer) — the whole management surface

**Entry:** Studio tab → swipe down for BrandDeck → Edit brand → StudioComposer modal (src/components/studio-composer.tsx, mounted in src/app/studio.tsx). Also opened by the push deep-link params ?reviewSlug=<slug>&reviewName=<name> on /studio.

  2. On Studio, swipe down from the top edge (or tap the summon pill) to open the BrandDeck
  3. Tap Edit on a brand card
  4. Observe the full-screen console modal
- **Expected:** Console opens titled with the brand name, tab defaults to 'Edit site', /api/creator/stats + /api/creator/credits fire, and per-brand loads (posts, revisions, products, insights) follow. Close ✕ returns to the deck.

### CON-2 — Tab bar: Edit site / Posts / Sell / Settings all render with data [P1]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui
- **Steps:**
  1. Inside the console, tap each of the four tabs in turn
  2. On Sell verify products list with per-product actions; on Settings verify insights (revenue/orders/views/margins), recent orders, publish toggle, Go live, Returns, Delete brand
- **Expected:** Each tab renders without crash; Settings shows numbers from /api/creator/stats + /api/creator/orders + /api/creator/margins filtered to the active brand; no cross-brand rows leak in.

### CON-3 — Live-site preview + critique mode (SitePreview WebView) [P1]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui
- **Risk:** Depends on the prod forge worker being up; the revision may take minutes — assert the 'building' card appears rather than waiting for completion.
- **Steps:**
  1. On a brand with a deployed site, tap the site preview tile on the Edit tab
  2. SitePreview opens the deploymentUrl (or customDomain) in a WebView
  3. Enter critique mode and send a change request
  4. Close the preview
- **Expected:** The WebView loads the same origin siteUrlFor() computes (custom domain preferred over vercel.app, github.com URLs never used). Sending a critique creates a revision (loadRevisions refreshes) and the review bar appears when it is ready.

### CON-4 — 'Ask Eve' handoff from the console into EveDeveloping [P1]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui
- **Steps:**
  1. On the Edit tab tap the Ask-Eve tile (calls summonEve({state:'developing', payload:{slug, url, name}}))
  2. Observe the console yields to the EveDeveloping full-screen surface for the same brand
- **Expected:** EveDeveloping opens with the correct slug/url; exiting returns to Studio; submitting an edit reopens the console on that brand (onSubmitted path in studio.tsx).

### CON-5 — Build-in-progress state: durable status, honest progress, 6s poll [P1]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui, api
- **Risk:** Real forge build on the DO droplet — takes ~5 min and consumes prod resources; prefer asserting the building UI + poll behavior, not waiting for completion.
- **Steps:**
  1. Trigger POST /api/creator/build-site for a brand with a design system but no site (or open the console mid-build)
  2. Watch the progress card: elapsed timer from the revision's server createdAt, fill capped at 95%, ETA copy transitions
  3. Background the app and foreground it again
- **Expected:** Status derives from store.status==='building' or the provision revision — not a local flag; reopening mid-build still shows building; foregrounding refetches (AppState listener); when the forge flips status to ready the live site appears without user action. 402 shows the 'Pro feature' note; error no_design_system shows the 'finish setup with Eve' note.

  2. Observe the console
- **Expected:** Console opens for that slug (reviewHandled ref dedupes repeats — opening the same URL twice does not re-open after close). A ready revision with a previewUrl shows the review bar; a 'ready' revision WITHOUT previewUrl must not render a bare Publish card.

### CON-7 — In-console revision review: approve lands on Settings, decline hides + survives failure [P1]

  2. Separately, on another ready revision tap ✕ (decline)
- **Expected:** Approve merges (POST /revisions/:id/approve), closes the preview, and switches the console to the Settings tab. Decline hides the card immediately, POSTs decline, then loadRevisions clears the local dismissed set — if the server call failed the card must REAPPEAR for retry (the reviewDismissed re-derivation).

- **Steps:**
  1. Via API with a non-comp low-credit account (or mock by asserting the client pre-check), invoke makeVideoAd / makeModelVideo where credits < cost
- **Expected:** Client pre-check blocks with 'You need N credits… you have M.' before any request; a server 402 updates the local balance from the response and shows the needed amount. genId busy-lock prevents double-fires while one generation runs.

### SELL-3 — Model-videos rate limit and API validation [P1]

- **Methods:** api, simulator-ui
- **Steps:**
  1. POST /api/creator/model-videos repeatedly to trip the rate limiter
  2. POST with a productId owned by another creator; POST with an unknown productId
- **Expected:** 429 surfaces the 'rate-limited, try again shortly' note in the UI; foreign/unknown products return 404 (ownership check), never generating.

### SELL-4 — Console product delete propagates (catalog + storefront + Printful) [P1]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui, api
- **⛔ Boundary:** Only delete products created by this test run — Printful deletion on the prod store is irreversible.
- **Steps:**
  1. Sell tab → delete a throwaway test product → cancel once → confirm
  2. Verify it is gone from /api/creator/products and from GET /api/public/stores/:slug/products
- **Expected:** Confirm dialog names the product; delete removes it everywhere; the optimistic list update sticks; a failed delete alerts and keeps the row.

## Scene shorts (SceneShortComposer + /api/creator/scene-video)

**Entry:** Console → Sell tab → 'Make a scene short' (src/components/scene-short-composer.tsx) → POST /api/creator/scene-video {productId, scene, aspectRatio, target, model}.

### SCENE-1 — Config flow: product → scene preset/custom → aspect → model tier [P1]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui
- **Steps:**
  1. Open the short composer, pick a product, tap a scene chip, switch 9:16 ↔ 16:9, switch Wan/Seedance/Veo tiers
  2. Verify the displayed credit price changes per tier (from /api/creator/credits catalog, falling back to 60/160/400)
- **Expected:** All selections render; generate button disabled until product + scene chosen; per-model credits shown match the server catalog.

### SCENE-2 — Generate + publish target website vs feed [P1]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui, api
- **Risk:** fal video generation takes minutes and is the priciest call; rate-limited 4 per 10 min per creator — schedule these tests last.
- **Steps:**
  1. Generate a short with target 'website' on a product that already has fewer than 3 model videos
  2. Generate another with target 'feed'
- **Expected:** Website target appends to products.model_videos (capped at MAX_WEBSITE_VIDEOS=3); feed target sets products.video_url so the item shows in /feed. Video preview plays in the done phase.

### SCENE-3 — scene-video API validation battery [P0]

- **Methods:** api
- **Steps:**
  1. POST without productId/scene → 400
  2. POST with unknown productId → 404
  3. POST for a product with no imageUrl → 400 'product has no image'
  4. POST for another creator's product → ownership rejection
  5. 5th call inside 10 min → 429
- **Expected:** Each returns the documented status and JSON error shape; no credits debited on any rejection.

## Creator orders + refunds (live-Stripe money boundary)

**Entry:** Console → Settings tab recent-orders list (confirmRefund/refundOrder in studio-composer.tsx) → GET /api/creator/orders, POST /api/creator/orders/[id]/refund (refundOrder() branches on payoutStatus).

### ORD-1 — Creator orders list is tenant-scoped [P1]

- **Methods:** api
- **Steps:**
  1. GET /api/creator/orders with the comp token
  2. GET with a second account's token that owns no stores
- **Expected:** Returns up to 100 orders only for accessible stores with storeSlug attached; a storeless creator gets {orders: []}; 401 without a token.

### ORD-2 — Refund route validation without moving money [P0]

- **Methods:** api
- **⛔ Boundary:** STOP before refunding any genuinely paid order — refundOrder() moves real money on live Stripe (refund + transfer reversal). Only exercise the 404/409/idempotent-refunded rungs; never POST refund on an order in a REFUNDABLE status with a real paymentIntentId.
- **Steps:**
  1. POST /api/creator/orders/<random-uuid>/refund → expect 404
  2. POST for an order belonging to another creator → 404 (ownership join)
  3. If a test order exists in a non-refundable status (e.g. pending_payment), POST → 409 'cannot refund a pending_payment order'
- **Expected:** All validation rungs return the exact statuses; the Stripe refund path is never reached.

### ORD-3 — Refund confirm dialog in the console (cancel path only) [P1]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui
- **⛔ Boundary:** Never tap the destructive 'Refund' confirm on a real paid order — verify the dialog and cancel.
- **Steps:**
  1. On Settings, tap Refund on a refundable-looking order row
  2. Read the alert copy, tap Cancel
- **Expected:** Alert warns 'The customer is paid back in full and any transfer is reversed.'; Cancel makes no network call (verify no POST in the dev-server log); the refunding busy-lock (refundingId) prevents double taps.

## Returns: customer request form + creator ReturnsInbox

**Entry:** Customer side: Account → Purchases → 'Your orders' → Request a return (src/components/purchases.tsx ReturnForm) → POST /api/customer/returns (proxies platform-api). Creator side: Console → Settings → Returns (src/components/returns-inbox.tsx) → GET /api/creator/returns, POST /api/creator/returns/[id]/{approve,decline}.

### RET-1 — Return-request form: reasons + photo requirement [P1]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui, api
- **⛔ Boundary:** A submitted claim is a real record in the prod platform-api — only file claims against a test order made by the comp account, and clean up by declining it from the creator inbox.
- **Risk:** Requires PLATFORM_API_BASE set on the dev server; without it the route 503s 'Returns are not available yet' — assert that env branch too.
- **Steps:**
  1. Open Purchases with an account that has an eligible order (canRequestReturn), tap Request a return
  2. Pick 'Defective / misprint' with no photo and submit
  3. Pick 'Never arrived' and submit
- **Expected:** Defective/damaged claims without a photo are blocked with 'Add at least one photo for a defective or damaged item.' (client mirrors the server 400). A valid claim POSTs with the verified account email attached; on success the order card flips to 'Return requested' after refresh.

### RET-2 — POST /api/customer/returns validation battery [P0]

- **Methods:** api
- **Steps:**
  1. POST unauthenticated → 401
  2. POST without orderId → 400
  3. POST with reason 'because' → 400 'a valid reason is required'
  4. POST reason defective with photoUrls ['not-a-url'] → 400 (non-https URLs filtered, empty list rejected)
- **Expected:** Each rung returns the documented status; nothing is forwarded to platform-api on validation failure.

### RET-3 — ReturnsInbox renders claims with status badges and order handles [P1]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui, api
- **Steps:**
  1. Console → Settings → open Returns
  2. Verify each card: short #order-handle, reason label, amount, status (needs review/approved/declined/refunded), photos if present
- **Expected:** GET /api/creator/returns rows render newest-first across the creator's stores; load failure shows the retryable error state.

### RET-4 — Decline a claim with a reason [P1]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui, api
- **Steps:**
  1. On a 'needs review' claim tap Decline, enter a reason, confirm
- **Expected:** POST /api/creator/returns/:id/decline resolves the claim to declined with the stored resolution; the list refreshes silently; busyId prevents concurrent actions.

### RET-5 — Approve-and-refund boundary [P0]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui, api
- **⛔ Boundary:** Never confirm Approve on a claim tied to a real paid order — it issues a real Stripe refund. Verify dialog + cancel, and only exercise API 404/validation rungs.
- **Steps:**
  1. Tap Approve on a claim, read the confirm alert ('$X is refunded to <email> in full. This can't be undone.')
  2. Tap Cancel
  3. Via API, POST approve on a nonexistent claim id → 404
- **Expected:** Cancel makes no call; the API path validates ownership/state before any money moves.

## Go live: custom domain attach / search / buy (GoLiveComposer)

**Entry:** Console → Settings → Go live (src/components/go-live-composer.tsx) → GET /api/creator/stores/[slug], POST .../go-live, GET .../domain/search, POST .../domain/buy.

### DOM-1 — Status card + mode toggle [P1]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui
- **Steps:**
  1. Open Go live for a non-live brand
  2. Verify the NOT LIVE card shows the preview URL host; toggle 'I own a domain' ↔ 'Buy a domain'; verify credit balance renders on the buy tab
- **Expected:** Store info + credits load in parallel; a live brand instead shows the LIVE card with its customDomain.

### DOM-2 — Domain search (read-only, safe) [P1]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui, api
- **Steps:**
  1. Buy tab → search a clearly taken domain (e.g. google.com) → expect 'isn't available'
  2. Search an available junk domain (random string .com) → expect the offer card with price + credits/yr
  3. Via API: GET domain/search?q=not_a_domain → 400 'enter a domain like mybrand.com'; GET for another creator's slug → 404
- **Expected:** Search never charges (explicitly read-only); offers carry priceUsd + computed credits; ownership check keeps the Vercel token behind the creator's own store.

### DOM-3 — Buy-domain boundary [P0]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui, api
- **⛔ Boundary:** Never POST domain/buy for an available domain with an owning token — it registers a REAL domain via Vercel and debits credits (comp exemption could make it free and still register). Stop at the offer card; verify only the foreign-slug 404 and the client credit gate.
- **Steps:**
  1. With an available offer shown, verify the 'Buy & go live' button exists
  2. STOP — do not tap it
  3. Via API only: POST domain/buy for a store slug the token does not own → 404
- **Expected:** Ownership rung returns 404 without registering anything. The client pre-check blocks buys when credits < offer.credits with balance-aware copy.

### DOM-4 — Attach-own-domain path stops before touching Vercel [P1]

- **Methods:** api, simulator-ui
- **⛔ Boundary:** Do not attach any real domain — it mutates the prod Vercel project. Test validation rungs only.
- **Steps:**
  1. Own tab: verify the button is disabled with an empty field
  2. Via API: POST go-live with no domain body → 4xx; POST for a foreign slug → 404
  3. STOP before submitting any real domain
- **Expected:** Validation rungs pass; the DNS-records card and 'I've added them — re-check' relabel are code-reviewed but not exercised against prod Vercel.

### CMS-2 — Save a copy + color change and verify it persists [P1]

  2. Reopen the editor and verify the values held

### CMS-3 — Enhance (✦) button per field [P1]

- **Expected:** Enhance returns brand-voiced copy respecting the field's length guide (headline ≤ ~6 words); validation and rate-limit rungs return documented statuses; the call is free (no credit debit).

### CMS-4 — site-config tenant scoping [P0]

- **Methods:** api

- **Expected:** 404 'store not found' via storeForMember — no cross-tenant reads or writes.

## Feed engagement + public storefront APIs (like, share, beacon, public products)

**Entry:** Hidden /feed screen actions (src/app/feed.tsx toggleLike/share) → POST /api/feed/[id]/like, /api/feed/[id]/share; brand sites → POST /api/public/beacon, GET /api/public/stores/[slug]/products.

### ENG-1 — Like toggle: optimistic UI + server reconcile + signed-out grace [P1]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui, api
- **Steps:**
  1. Navigate to /feed signed in, tap ♡ on an item, then tap again
  2. Repeat while signed out
- **Expected:** Signed in: heart fills immediately, POST returns {liked, likeCount} and the count reconciles; second tap unlikes. Signed out: the tap no-ops gracefully (server 401 'sign in to like', UI rolls back without crashing).

### ENG-2 — Share sheet bumps the share count (no auth) [P2]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui, api
- **Steps:**
  1. Tap Share on a feed item, complete the native share (Copy)
  2. Cancel the sheet on a second attempt
- **Expected:** Completed share increments the local count and fires POST /api/feed/:id/share (unauthenticated is fine); a cancelled sheet fires nothing. API: POST for a random product id returns shareCount 0, not 500.

### ENG-3 — Beacon pageview counter [P2]

- **Methods:** api
- **⛔ Boundary:** Writes real pageview ticks to prod analytics — keep to a couple of calls against the comp account's own test brand only.
- **Risk:** views30d aggregates by day, so the increment may fold into existing traffic; assert on the delta.

  1. POST /api/public/beacon with the comp test brand's storeSlug twice in one day
  2. POST with no storeSlug → 400; unknown slug → 404
  3. GET /api/creator/stats and check views30d moved
- **Expected:** Daily counter upserts (+1 per call, conflict-safe), anonymous by design; validation rungs return 400/404 with {ok:false}.

### ENG-4 — Public storefront catalog endpoint [P1]

- **Methods:** api
- **Steps:**
  1. GET /api/public/stores/<comp-brand-slug>/products with no auth
  2. GET an unknown slug → 404
  3. Cross-check: an unpublished product from the same brand must NOT appear
- **Expected:** Published-only products with variant rows (sku, color, size, retailPriceCents, inStock) matching the templates' Product/Variant contract; no auth required; no draft leakage.

## Apple IAP: paywall iOS branch + /api/creator/billing/iap-verify

**Entry:** Paywall subscribe on iOS (src/components/paywall.tsx iapReady/purchaseInApp, src/lib/iap.ios.ts, src/lib/iap-products.ts) → POST /api/creator/billing/iap-verify {transactionId} (App Store Server API, idempotent on transactionId).

### IAP-1 — Simulator falls back from IAP to Stripe [P1]

- **Methods:** simulator-ui
- **Steps:**
  1. Open the Paywall on the simulator (no StoreKit entitlement / native module)
  2. Tap subscribe on a plan
  3. Check the iapNote diagnostic state
- **Expected:** iapReady() resolves false in the dev-client simulator, so the flow routes to Stripe checkout (the covered BOUNDARY) instead of silently failing; the iapNote explains why IAP didn't engage.

### IAP-2 — iap-verify API battery [P0]

- **Methods:** api
- **Steps:**
  1. POST unauthenticated → 401
  2. POST authed with no body → 400 'transactionId required'
  3. POST authed with transactionId 'nope-123' → 501 iap_not_configured if App Store keys are absent on the dev server, else 402 transaction_not_found
- **Expected:** Each rung returns the documented status; no credits are ever granted for an unverifiable transaction; a replayed transactionId returns alreadyApplied via the ledger refId idempotency key.

### IAP-3 — Real IAP purchase end-to-end [P2]

- **Methods:** manual-device-only
- **⛔ Boundary:** Sandbox App Store only — never a real Apple ID payment method.
- **Steps:**
  1. On a physical device with a sandbox Apple ID: buy a credit pack, then a plan subscription; relaunch and let the client re-verify
- **Expected:** Consumable grants credits once; the plan activates + grants the month's credits; re-verification hits the alreadyApplied path; appAccountToken mismatch is rejected 403.

## Internal money-release job hardening (/api/internal/release-payouts)

**Entry:** POST /api/internal/release-payouts with x-internal-key (src/app/api/internal/release-payouts+api.ts) — the deferred-payout release job that transfers each brand its held net via live Stripe.

### PAYREL-1 — Auth rejection rungs (never run the job) [P0]

- **Methods:** api
- **⛔ Boundary:** NEVER POST with the real INTERNAL_API_KEY — a successful run executes real Stripe transfers of held payouts against the prod DB. Auth-rejection rungs only; verify release logic by code review/unit only.
- **Risk:** Nothing auto-invokes this route (cron is owner-config) — a stray authorized call in testing would be the only trigger.
- **Steps:**
  1. POST with no x-internal-key header
  2. POST with a wrong key of the same length and of a different length (constant-time compare path)
  3. Confirm the route also rejects when INTERNAL_API_KEY is unset in env
- **Expected:** 401 in all cases with no DB scan and no Stripe call; an empty configured key must fail closed, not open.
